# 科技产品

**device**
英/美 /dɪˈvaɪs/
n. 设备；装置
[义] A piece of equipment made for a particular purpose, especially an electronic or mechanical one. 为特定用途制造的器具，尤指电子或机械装置（electronic device 电子设备，mobile device 移动设备，handheld device 手持设备）。可数。科技语境中最通用的「设备」总称，比 gadget 更中性、更正式。
[例] ① Make sure your device is connected to Wi-Fi before updating. 更新前请确认设备已连上 Wi-Fi。 ② The hospital banned personal devices in certain wards. 医院禁止在部分病房使用个人电子设备。
[搭] electronic device 电子设备, mobile device 移动设备, handheld device 手持设备, smart device 智能设备, connect a device 连接设备
[联] gadget 小装置, equipment 设备, machine 机器, tool 工具, hardware 硬件, smartphone 智能手机, laptop 笔记本电脑
[记] 可数：a device。device vs. gadget：device 更通用正式，gadget 多指小巧新奇的小装置

**gadget**
英/美 /ˈɡædʒɪt/
n. 小装置；小玩意
[义] A small tool or device, especially one that is clever or novel. 小巧的工具或装置，尤指设计巧妙或新奇者（kitchen gadget 厨房小工具，latest gadget 最新小玩意，tech gadget 科技小装置）。可数。常带「有趣、新潮、非必需」色彩。
[例] ① He loves buying the latest kitchen gadgets online. 他爱在网上买最新厨房小玩意。 ② The fair was full of wearable gadgets and prototypes. 展会上满是可穿戴小装置和原型机。
[搭] kitchen gadget 厨房小工具, latest gadget 最新小玩意, tech gadget 科技小装置, handy gadget 实用小装置
[联] device 设备, tool 工具, toy 玩具, novelty 新奇物, gizmo 小装置（口语）, smartphone 智能手机, wearable 可穿戴设备
[记] 可数：a gadget。略口语；正式报告多用 device 或 equipment

**electronics**
英/美 /ɪˌlekˈtrɒnɪks/
n. 电子产品；电子学
[义] Electronic devices and equipment as products; the science of how electrons behave in circuits. 作为商品的电子装置与设备；研究电路中电子行为的学科（consumer electronics 消费电子产品，electronics store 电子产品店，the electronics industry 电子产业）。通常不可数指「电子产品」整体；复数形式亦指电子元件。
[例] ① She works in consumer electronics, designing new headphones. 她从事消费电子，设计新耳机。 ② The shop sells phones, laptops, and other electronics. 这家店卖手机、笔记本及其他电子产品。
[搭] consumer electronics 消费电子产品, electronics store 电子产品店, electronics industry 电子产业, home electronics 家用电子产品
[联] device 设备, gadget 小装置, electrical 电的, digital 数字的, hardware 硬件, appliance 电器, technology 技术
[记] 不可数指「电子产品」类别；study electronics 指学「电子学」。electronic（形容词）与 electronics 勿混

**hardware**
英/美 /ˈhɑːdweə(r)/
n. 硬件；电脑硬件
[义] The physical parts of a computer or other electronic system, as opposed to the programs that run on it. 计算机或其他电子系统的物理部件，与运行于其上的程序相对（computer hardware 电脑硬件，upgrade the hardware 升级硬件）。不可数。亦泛指「五金件、硬件材料」，依语境区分。
[例] ① The game runs smoothly on older hardware. 这款游戏在旧硬件上也能流畅运行。 ② They sell computer hardware and accessories in the same store. 同一家店既卖电脑硬件也卖配件。
[搭] computer hardware 电脑硬件, upgrade the hardware 升级硬件, hardware failure 硬件故障, hardware store 五金店
[联] software 软件, device 设备, component 组件, processor 处理器, memory 内存, peripheral 外设, firmware 固件
[记] 不可数。hardware vs. software 为经典对举。hardware store 在美式英语中指「五金店」，非仅电脑

**computer**
英/美 /kəmˈpjuːtə(r)/
n. 计算机；电脑
[义] An electronic machine that stores and processes data according to instructions. 按指令存储并处理数据的电子机器（personal computer 个人电脑，desktop computer 台式电脑，laptop computer 笔记本电脑）。可数。
[例] ① She bought a new computer for remote work. 她为远程办公买了一台新电脑。 ② The files are stored on a shared computer in the office. 文件存放在办公室一台共用电脑上。
[搭] personal computer 个人电脑, desktop computer 台式电脑, laptop computer 笔记本电脑, computer screen 电脑屏幕, turn on the computer 开电脑
[联] laptop 笔记本电脑, desktop 台式机, tablet 平板电脑, keyboard 键盘, mouse 鼠标, monitor 显示器, software 软件
[记] 可数：a computer。口语常简称 PC（personal computer）或 machine（较旧）

**laptop**
英/美 /ˈlæptɒp/
n. 笔记本电脑
[义] A portable computer that can be used on your lap, with a screen and keyboard built into one unit. 可放在膝上使用的便携式电脑，屏幕与键盘一体（laptop computer 笔记本电脑，close the laptop 合上笔记本，business laptop 商务本）。可数。
[例] ① He opened his laptop and joined the video call. 他打开笔记本加入了视频通话。 ② The airline asked passengers to stow laptops during takeoff. 航空公司要求乘客在起飞时收好笔记本电脑。
[搭] laptop computer 笔记本电脑, close the laptop 合上笔记本, business laptop 商务本, laptop bag 电脑包, laptop charger 笔记本充电器
[联] computer 电脑, desktop 台式机, tablet 平板, notebook 笔记本（同义）, keyboard 键盘, screen 屏幕, portable 便携的
[记] 可数：a laptop。notebook 在美式英语中常同指 laptop；英式 notebook 有时指「练习本」，须看语境

**desktop**
英/美 /ˈdesktɒp/
n. 台式电脑；桌面
adj. 台式的；桌面上的
[义] A personal computer designed to stay on a desk, with separate monitor and keyboard; relating to such a computer or to a computer's main screen area. 设计置于桌面、显示器与键盘分开的个人电脑；亦指台式机或电脑桌面界面（desktop computer 台式电脑，desktop PC 台式机，desktop icon 桌面图标）。可数/形容词。
[例] ① The office still uses desktops for graphic design work. 办公室做平面设计仍用台式机。 ② Save the file to your desktop for easy access. 把文件存到桌面以便取用。
[搭] desktop computer 台式电脑, desktop PC 台式机, desktop icon 桌面图标, desktop wallpaper 桌面壁纸
[联] laptop 笔记本, computer 电脑, monitor 显示器, keyboard 键盘, tower 主机箱, portable 便携的, workstation 工作站
[记] 可数指「台式机」；不可数/单数指电脑「桌面」界面。desktop vs. laptop 为常见对举

**tablet**
英/美 /ˈtæblət/
n. 平板电脑；药片
[义] A flat, portable computer with a touchscreen, larger than a phone but smaller than a laptop. 带触摸屏的扁平便携电脑，比手机大、比笔记本小（tablet computer 平板电脑，Android tablet 安卓平板）。可数。亦指「药片」，依语境区分。
[例] ① She reads e-books on a tablet before bed. 她睡前用平板读电子书。 ② The kids use a tablet for educational games. 孩子们用平板玩益智游戏。
[搭] tablet computer 平板电脑, Android tablet 安卓平板, tablet case 平板保护套, swipe on a tablet 在平板上滑动
[联] smartphone 智能手机, laptop 笔记本, touchscreen 触摸屏, e-reader 电子阅读器, screen 屏幕, portable 便携的, stylus 触控笔
[记] 可数：a tablet。tablet vs. phone：tablet 屏幕更大，多不主打通话。药片义 totally different context

**smartphone**
英/美 /ˈsmɑːtfəʊn/
n. 智能手机
[义] A mobile phone that can connect to the internet, run apps, and perform many functions of a small computer. 能连网、运行应用程序、具备小型电脑多种功能的手机（latest smartphone 最新智能手机，smartphone camera 手机摄像头）。可数。
[例] ① Most people check email on their smartphones throughout the day. 多数人一天里会用智能手机查邮件。 ② The new smartphone has a better battery and camera. 新款智能手机电池和相机更好。
[搭] latest smartphone 最新智能手机, smartphone camera 手机摄像头, smartphone app 手机应用, unlock a smartphone 解锁手机
[联] phone 手机, mobile 手机（英式）, tablet 平板, device 设备, screen 屏幕, charger 充电器, cellular 蜂窝的
[记] 可数：a smartphone。口语常简称 phone。smartphone vs. mobile phone：smartphone 强调智能功能，mobile 在英式仍常用

**phone**
英/美 /fəʊn/
n. 电话；手机
v. 打电话
[义] A device used to speak to someone who is far away; especially a mobile phone in everyday speech. 用于与远方的人通话的装置；日常口语尤指手机（mobile phone 手机，cell phone 手机〔美式〕，answer the phone 接电话）。可数。
[例] ① He left his phone on the kitchen counter. 他把手机落在厨房台面上。 ② Could you phone me when you arrive? 到了能给我打个电话吗？
[搭] mobile phone 手机, cell phone 手机, answer the phone 接电话, phone call 电话, phone charger 手机充电器
[联] smartphone 智能手机, tablet 平板, call 打电话, text 发短信, screen 屏幕, battery 电池, landline 固定电话
[记] 可数：a phone。cell phone（美式）与 mobile phone（英式）同指手机。指智能手机时 smartphone 更精确。动词 phone sb. 较英式；美式常说 call

**monitor**
英/美 /ˈmɒnɪtə(r)/
n. 显示器；监视器
v. 监视；监测
[义] A screen used with a computer to display information; a device that checks or records something. 与电脑配合显示信息的屏幕；监测或记录某物的装置（computer monitor 电脑显示器，widescreen monitor 宽屏显示器，heart monitor 心电监护仪）。可数。
[例] ① She connected a second monitor to her laptop for editing. 她为剪辑给笔记本接了第二块显示器。 ② The nurse checked the monitor by the patient's bed. 护士查看了床边的监护仪。
[搭] computer monitor 电脑显示器, widescreen monitor 宽屏显示器, dual monitors 双显示器, monitor stand 显示器支架
[联] screen 屏幕, display 显示屏, laptop 笔记本, desktop 台式机, keyboard 键盘, resolution 分辨率, pixel 像素
[记] 可数：a monitor。monitor 作动词（监测）与名词（显示器）同形。TV screen 常说 screen 或 TV，较少说 monitor

**screen**
英/美 /skriːn/
n. 屏幕；银幕
v. 筛查；放映
[义] The flat surface on a phone, computer, or TV where images and text appear; the large surface in a cinema. 手机、电脑或电视上显示图像与文字的平面；电影院的银幕（phone screen 手机屏幕，touch screen 触摸屏，lock screen 锁屏界面）。可数。
[例] ① The screen cracked when the phone fell on the pavement. 手机摔在路面上把屏幕摔裂了。 ② He adjusted the brightness on his laptop screen. 他调了笔记本屏幕的亮度。
[搭] phone screen 手机屏幕, touch screen 触摸屏, lock screen 锁屏, screen protector 屏幕保护膜, full screen 全屏
[联] monitor 显示器, display 显示屏, smartphone 智能手机, tablet 平板, pixel 像素, brightness 亮度, crack 破裂
[记] 可数：a screen。screen 比 monitor 更通用，可指任何「显示面」。screen time 指「看屏幕的时间」

**keyboard**
英/美 /ˈkiːbɔːd/
n. 键盘
[义] A set of keys on a computer, phone, or typewriter used for entering text and commands. 电脑、手机或打字机上一组用于输入文字与指令的键（wireless keyboard 无线键盘, mechanical keyboard 机械键盘, on-screen keyboard 屏幕键盘）。可数。
[例] ① She spilled coffee on her keyboard and had to replace it. 她把咖啡洒在键盘上，只好换一块。 ② The tablet's on-screen keyboard is fine for short messages. 平板的屏幕键盘发短消息够用。
[搭] wireless keyboard 无线键盘, mechanical keyboard 机械键盘, on-screen keyboard 屏幕键盘, keyboard shortcut 键盘快捷键
[联] mouse 鼠标, laptop 笔记本, type 打字, key 键, touchpad 触控板, monitor 显示器, input 输入
[记] 可数：a keyboard。keyboard vs. keypad：keypad 多指电话或小键盘上的数字键区

**mouse**
英/美 /maʊs/
n. 鼠标；老鼠
（复数 mice /maɪs/ 或 mouses）
[义] A small device moved by hand to control the cursor on a computer screen. 用手移动以控制电脑屏幕光标的小装置（wireless mouse 无线鼠标, left-click 左键点击, scroll wheel 滚轮）。可数。复数计算机义常用 mice，亦见 mouses。
[例] ① He prefers a mouse to a touchpad for gaming. 打游戏他更喜欢鼠标而非触控板。 ② Double-click the file icon with your mouse. 用鼠标双击文件图标。
[搭] wireless mouse 无线鼠标, optical mouse 光学鼠标, mouse pad 鼠标垫, left-click 左键点击, scroll wheel 滚轮
[联] keyboard 键盘, cursor 光标, click 点击, touchpad 触控板, laptop 笔记本, computer 电脑, trackball 轨迹球
[记] 复数：mice（计算机）或 mouses。与「老鼠」同形，语境区分。touchpad 为笔记本内置，mouse 多为外接

**touchpad**
英/美 /ˈtʌtʃpæd/
n. 触控板
[义] A flat area on a laptop that you touch to move the cursor, instead of using a separate mouse. 笔记本上用于移动光标的平面触控区，替代外接鼠标（laptop touchpad 笔记本触控板, swipe on the touchpad 在触控板上滑动）。可数。
[例] ① She disabled the touchpad while using an external mouse. 接外接鼠标时她关掉了触控板。 ② Two-finger scrolling works on most modern touchpads. 多数现代触控板支持双指滚动。
[搭] laptop touchpad 笔记本触控板, touchpad gesture 触控板手势, external mouse 外接鼠标, two-finger scroll 双指滚动
[联] mouse 鼠标, laptop 笔记本, cursor 光标, keyboard 键盘, swipe 滑动, click 点击, trackpad：同义，美式更常见
[记] 可数：a touchpad。trackpad 为同义词，Apple 产品说明常用 trackpad

**printer**
英/美 /ˈprɪntə(r)/
n. 打印机
[义] A machine connected to a computer that puts text or images onto paper. 与电脑连接、把文字或图像印到纸上的机器（laser printer 激光打印机, inkjet printer 喷墨打印机, wireless printer 无线打印机）。可数。
[例] ① The printer is out of ink again. 打印机又没墨了。 ② She sent the document to the office printer. 她把文件发送到办公室打印机。
[搭] laser printer 激光打印机, inkjet printer 喷墨打印机, wireless printer 无线打印机, print a document 打印文件, printer paper 打印纸
[联] scanner 扫描仪, ink 墨水, paper 纸, copy 复印, fax 传真, computer 电脑, cartridge 墨盒
[记] 可数：a printer。run out of ink/toner 为常见抱怨。3D printer 指「3D 打印机」，不同类但同族

**scanner**
英/美 /ˈskænə(r)/
n. 扫描仪；扫描器
[义] A device that copies documents or images into a computer as digital files; a machine that reads barcodes or body images. 把文件或图像复制进电脑成为数字文件的装置；读取条码或人体影像的扫描设备（document scanner 文档扫描仪, barcode scanner 条码扫描器, CT scanner CT 扫描仪）。可数。
[例] ① He scanned the receipt with a flatbed scanner. 他用平板扫描仪扫了收据。 ② The cashier passed each item over the barcode scanner. 收银员把每件商品在条码扫描器上扫过。
[搭] document scanner 文档扫描仪, barcode scanner 条码扫描器, flatbed scanner 平板扫描仪, scan a document 扫描文件
[联] printer 打印机, copy 复印, digital 数字的, barcode 条码, image 图像, fax 传真, photocopier 复印机
[记] 可数：a scanner。scan 作动词（扫描）高频。medical scanner 语境下指医学影像设备

**speaker**
英/美 /ˈspiːkə(r)/
n. 扬声器；音箱；演讲者
[义] A device that converts electrical signals into sound; a person who gives a speech. 把电信号转换成声音的装置；发表演讲的人（Bluetooth speaker 蓝牙音箱, built-in speakers 内置扬声器, keynote speaker 主讲人）。可数。
[例] ① Turn up the speakers if you can't hear the video. 听不清视频就把音箱音量调大。 ② The laptop has small built-in speakers on each side. 笔记本两侧各有一个小内置扬声器。
[搭] Bluetooth speaker 蓝牙音箱, built-in speakers 内置扬声器, wireless speaker 无线音箱, speaker volume 扬声器音量
[联] headphone 耳机, microphone 麦克风, sound 声音, audio 音频, loud 响亮的, mute 静音, amplifier 功放
[记] 可数：a speaker。表「演讲者」与「扬声器」同形，语境区分。smart speaker 指智能音箱（如 Echo）

**headphone**
英/美 /ˈhedfəʊn/
n. 耳机
（常用复数 headphones）
[义] A device worn over or in the ears to listen to audio without others hearing. 戴在耳上或耳内、供个人听音而不打扰他人的装置（wireless headphones 无线耳机, noise-cancelling headphones 降噪耳机, over-ear headphones 头戴式耳机）。常用复数 headphones。
[例] ① She put on her headphones and started working. 她戴上耳机开始工作。 ② These headphones block out most background noise. 这款耳机能挡住大部分背景噪音。
[搭] wireless headphones 无线耳机, noise-cancelling headphones 降噪耳机, over-ear headphones 头戴式耳机, put on headphones 戴上耳机
[联] earphone 耳塞, speaker 扬声器, microphone 麦克风, audio 音频, Bluetooth 蓝牙, music 音乐, volume 音量
[记] 常用复数 headphones；单数 headphone 较少单独用。earbuds 指入耳式耳塞，口语极常见

**microphone**
英/美 /ˈmaɪkrəfəʊn/
n. 麦克风；话筒
[义] A device that converts sound into electrical signals for recording or amplification. 把声音转换成电信号以供录音或放大的装置（wireless microphone 无线麦克风, built-in microphone 内置麦克风, mute the microphone 静音麦克风）。可数。口语常简称 mic /maɪk/。
[例] ① Check that the microphone is working before the interview. 采访前确认麦克风正常。 ② He spoke too close to the microphone. 他离麦克风太近了。
[搭] wireless microphone 无线麦克风, built-in microphone 内置麦克风, mute the microphone 静音麦克风, microphone input 麦克风输入
[联] speaker 扬声器, headphone 耳机, record 录音, audio 音频, webcam 摄像头, video call 视频通话, sound 声音
[记] 可数：a microphone。口语 mic 极常见。microphone vs. speaker：一个收声，一个放音

**webcam**
英/美 /ˈwebkæm/
n. 网络摄像头
[义] A small camera connected to a computer, used for video calls or recording. 接在电脑上、用于视频通话或录制的小摄像头（built-in webcam 内置摄像头, external webcam 外接摄像头）。可数。
[例] ① She covered the webcam with a sticker for privacy. 她为隐私在摄像头上贴了贴纸。 ② The laptop's webcam quality is poor in low light. 这款笔记本摄像头在暗光下画质差。
[搭] built-in webcam 内置摄像头, external webcam 外接摄像头, webcam cover 摄像头遮挡盖, turn on the webcam 打开摄像头
[联] camera 相机, microphone 麦克风, video call 视频通话, laptop 笔记本, screen 屏幕, privacy 隐私, stream 直播
[记] 可数：a webcam。web + cam（camera）。视频会议前 check webcam and mic 为常见说法

**charger**
英/美 /ˈtʃɑːdʒə(r)/
n. 充电器
[义] A device used to put electrical power into a battery, especially for phones and laptops. 向电池输入电力的装置，尤指手机、笔记本充电器（phone charger 手机充电器, wireless charger 无线充电器, fast charger 快充头）。可数。
[例] ① I forgot my charger and the phone died by noon. 我忘了带充电器，手机中午就没电了。 ② The wireless charger works through a thin phone case. 无线充电器隔着薄手机壳也能用。
[搭] phone charger 手机充电器, laptop charger 笔记本充电器, wireless charger 无线充电器, fast charger 快充, plug in the charger 插上充电器
[联] battery 电池, cable 数据线, USB USB 接口, power 电力, socket 插座, adapter 适配器, charge 充电
[记] 可数：a charger。charge 作动词（充电）；charger 专指装置。portable charger / power bank 指「移动电源」

**cable**
英/美 /ˈkeɪbl/
n. 电缆；数据线
[义] A thick or thin wire covered in plastic, used to connect devices or carry electricity or signals. 包有塑料外皮、用于连接设备或传输电力/信号的粗细线缆（USB cable 数据线, charging cable 充电线, HDMI cable HDMI 线）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① The monitor won't turn on—maybe the cable is loose. 显示器不亮，可能是线松了。 ② Always unplug the charging cable by the plug, not the cord. 拔充电线应握插头，别拽线身。
[搭] USB cable 数据线, charging cable 充电线, HDMI cable HDMI 线, cable management 理线, connect with a cable 用线连接
[联] charger 充电器, plug 插头, socket 插座, wire 电线, adapter 适配器, port 接口, wireless 无线的
[记] 可数：a cable。cable TV 指「有线电视」，不同义。cable vs. wire：cable 多指包覆的成束线，wire 可更泛指金属丝

**battery**
英/美 /ˈbætəri/
n. 电池
[义] A device that stores chemical energy and provides electrical power for phones, laptops, and other portable devices. 储存化学能并为手机、笔记本等便携设备供电的装置（phone battery 手机电池, battery life 续航, rechargeable battery 充电电池）。可数。
[例] ① My battery is down to ten percent. 我电量只剩百分之十了。 ② Laptop batteries wear out after a few years. 笔记本电池用几年就会老化。
[搭] phone battery 手机电池, battery life 续航, rechargeable battery 充电电池, replace the battery 更换电池, low battery 低电量
[联] charger 充电器, power 电力, charge 充电, portable 便携的, energy 能量, drain 耗电, plug 插电
[记] 可数：a battery。battery life 指「续航时间」；dead battery 指「没电」。不可数义「炮兵连」属军事，不同场

**router**
英/美 /ˈruːtə(r)/
n. 路由器
[义] A device that connects computers and phones to the internet, often also providing Wi-Fi in a home or office. 把电脑、手机连到互联网的设备，通常也为家庭或办公室提供 Wi-Fi（Wi-Fi router 无线路由器, reset the router 重启路由器, wireless router 无线路由器）。可数。
[例] ① Restart the router if the Wi-Fi keeps dropping. Wi-Fi 老断就重启一下路由器。 ② The router sits on a shelf near the front door. 路由器放在靠近前门的架子上。
[搭] Wi-Fi router 无线路由器, wireless router 无线路由器, reset the router 重启路由器, router password 路由器密码
[联] modem 调制解调器, Wi-Fi 无线网络, internet 互联网, network 网络, connection 连接, broadband 宽带, signal 信号
[记] 可数：a router。router vs. modem：modem 接宽带信号，router 分发网络；许多家用设备二者合一

**Wi-Fi**
英/美 /ˈwaɪ faɪ/
n. 无线网络
[义] A system for connecting computers and phones to the internet without wires, using radio signals. 用无线电信号让电脑、手机无线上网的系统（Wi-Fi password Wi-Fi 密码, connect to Wi-Fi 连 Wi-Fi, free Wi-Fi 免费无线网络）。不可数。亦写作 WiFi、wifi。
[例] ① What's the Wi-Fi password for the hotel room? 酒店房间的 Wi-Fi 密码是什么？ ② The cafe offers free Wi-Fi for customers. 咖啡馆为顾客提供免费 Wi-Fi。
[搭] Wi-Fi password Wi-Fi 密码, connect to Wi-Fi 连 Wi-Fi, free Wi-Fi 免费无线网络, Wi-Fi signal Wi-Fi 信号, public Wi-Fi 公共 Wi-Fi
[联] router 路由器, internet 互联网, wireless 无线的, network 网络, password 密码, connection 连接, smartphone 智能手机, laptop 笔记本电脑, signal 信号, hotspot 热点
[记] 不可数。全称 wireless fidelity，日常几乎只说 Wi-Fi。connect to Wi-Fi 比 connect Wi-Fi 更自然

**Bluetooth**
英/美 /ˈbluːtuːθ/
n. 蓝牙
[义] A short-range wireless technology for connecting phones, headphones, speakers, and other devices. 短距离无线技术，用于连接手机、耳机、音箱等设备（Bluetooth headphones 蓝牙耳机, pair via Bluetooth 蓝牙配对, Bluetooth speaker 蓝牙音箱）。不可数。
[例] ① She paired her phone with the car via Bluetooth. 她用手机蓝牙与车机配对。 ② The speaker connects over Bluetooth within ten metres. 音箱十米内可蓝牙连接。
[搭] Bluetooth headphones 蓝牙耳机, Bluetooth speaker 蓝牙音箱, pair via Bluetooth 蓝牙配对, Bluetooth connection 蓝牙连接, enable Bluetooth 开启蓝牙
[联] wireless 无线的, headphone 耳机, speaker 音箱, phone 手机, pair 配对, connection 连接, device 设备, earbuds 耳塞, signal 信号, car 汽车
[记] 不可数。来自北欧国王 Harald Bluetooth 之名。Bluetooth 5.x 等指协议版本

**app**
英/美 /æp/
n. 应用程序
[义] A program on a phone, tablet, or computer designed for a specific task. 手机、平板或电脑上为特定任务设计的程序（download an app 下载应用, mobile app 手机应用, app store 应用商店）。可数。
[例] ① Download the airline app to check in faster. 下载航司应用可更快值机。 ② She deleted apps she no longer used to free up space. 她删了不用的应用腾出空间。
[搭] download an app 下载应用, mobile app 手机应用, app store 应用商店, open the app 打开应用, update the app 更新应用
[联] smartphone 智能手机, tablet 平板, software 软件, download 下载, device 设备, store 商店, notification 通知, online 在线, install 安装, delete 删除
[记] 可数：an app。application 的缩写，日常几乎只说 app。native app vs. web app 指安装方式不同

**wireless**
英/美 /ˈwaɪələs/
adj. 无线的
n. 无线连接；无线电
[义] Using radio signals instead of wires to send data or power. 用无线电信号而非线缆传输数据或电力（wireless charging 无线充电, wireless headphones 无线耳机, go wireless 改用无线）。形容词/不可数名词。
[例] ① The keyboard uses a wireless connection to the laptop. 键盘与笔记本无线连接。 ② Wireless charging works if you align the phone on the pad. 手机对准充电板即可无线充电。
[搭] wireless charging 无线充电, wireless headphones 无线耳机, wireless connection 无线连接, go wireless 改用无线, wireless network 无线网络
[联] Wi-Fi 无线网络, Bluetooth 蓝牙, cable 线缆, charger 充电器, headphone 耳机, signal 信号, connect 连接, router 路由器, phone 手机, battery 电池
[记] 形容词/不可数。wireless 旧义「无线电广播」今较少用。wireless vs. Wi-Fi：Wi-Fi 是 wireless 的一种常见形式

**earbuds**
英/美 /ˈɪəbʌdz/
n. 耳塞式耳机
[义] Small headphones that fit inside the ear, often connected by Bluetooth. 塞入耳内的小型耳机，常经蓝牙连接（wireless earbuds 无线耳塞, noise-cancelling earbuds 降噪耳塞, pair of earbuds 一副耳塞）。复数形式。
[例] ① She lost one of her wireless earbuds on the train. 她在火车上丢了一只无线耳塞。 ② These earbuds block out most background noise on flights. 这款耳塞在航班上能挡住大部分背景噪音。
[搭] wireless earbuds 无线耳塞, noise-cancelling earbuds 降噪耳塞, pair of earbuds 一副耳塞, charge the earbuds 给耳塞充电, earbud case 耳塞充电盒
[联] headphone 耳机, Bluetooth 蓝牙, wireless 无线的, music 音乐, phone 手机, charge 充电, noise 噪音, listen 听, AirPods 苹果耳塞, speaker 音箱
[记] 复数形式；不说 × an earbud（可说 one earbud）。earbud vs. earphone：earbuds 更强调入耳式

**television**
英/美 /ˈtelɪvɪʒn/
n. 电视；电视机
[义] The system of broadcasting pictures and sound; the device used to watch programmes. 传送图像与声音的广播系统；用来观看节目的装置（watch television 看电视, flat-screen television 平板电视, cable television 有线电视）。不可数/可数。比 TV 略正式，多指媒介或正式说法。
[例] ① They bought a new television with a bigger screen. 他们买了台屏幕更大的新电视。 ② Television has changed a lot since streaming services appeared. 流媒体出现后，电视这一媒介变化很大。
[搭] watch television 看电视, flat-screen television 平板电视, cable television 有线电视, television set 电视机（略旧）
[联] TV 电视, screen 屏幕, remote 遥控器, channel 频道, broadcast 广播, streaming 串流, monitor 显示器
[记] 不可数指「电视媒介」；可数指「电视机」。口语日常多说 TV

**remote**
英/美 /rɪˈməʊt/
n. 遥控器
adj. 遥远的；远程的
[义] A device used to control a TV or other equipment from a distance; far away in space or time. 从远处控制电视或其他设备的装置；空间或时间上遥远的（TV remote 电视遥控器, remote control 遥控〔名词短语〕, lose the remote 遥控器丢了）。可数/形容词。
[例] ① Where's the remote? I want to change the channel. 遥控器呢？我想换台。 ② She works on a remote team across three time zones. 她在跨三个时区的远程团队工作。
[搭] TV remote 电视遥控器, remote control 遥控, lose the remote 丢了遥控器, remote button 遥控按钮
[联] television 电视, channel 频道, volume 音量, button 按钮, control 控制, wireless 无线的, clicker 遥控器（美式口语）
[记] 可数指「遥控器」时，常说 remote 或 remote control。形容词义（远程的）极常见：remote work 远程办公

**camera**
英/美 /ˈkæmərə/
n. 相机；摄像头
[义] A device for taking photographs or recording video, either as a separate item or built into a phone. 用于拍照或录像的装置，可为独立设备或内置于手机（digital camera 数码相机, security camera 监控摄像头, camera lens 镜头）。可数。
[例] ① She brought a digital camera on the hiking trip. 徒步旅行她带了一台数码相机。 ② The phone camera takes sharp photos in daylight. 这款手机相机在白天拍出来很清晰。
[搭] digital camera 数码相机, security camera 监控摄像头, camera lens 镜头, take a photo 拍照, front camera 前置摄像头
[联] photo 照片, lens 镜头, video 视频, smartphone 智能手机, webcam 网络摄像头, shutter 快门, flash 闪光灯
[记] 可数：a camera。on camera 指「被拍到/在镜头前」。camera 在 phone 语境常指「摄像头」而非独立相机

**TV**
英/美 /ˌtiː ˈviː/
n. 电视；电视机
[义] Television; a television set, including smart TVs that connect to the internet and run apps. 电视；电视机，含可连网、运行应用的智能电视（watch TV 看电视, smart TV 智能电视, TV show 电视节目, stream on TV 在电视上串流）。可数/不可数。television 的缩写，口语极常见。
[例] ① The kids were watching TV in the living room. 孩子们在客厅看电视。 ② Their new smart TV came with several streaming apps preinstalled. 新智能电视预装了好几个串流应用。
[搭] watch TV 看电视, smart TV 智能电视, TV show 电视节目, TV screen 电视屏幕, stream on TV 电视串流
[联] television 电视, remote 遥控器, screen 屏幕, channel 频道, streaming 串流, soundbar 回音壁, broadcast 广播
[记] 可数/不可数。smart TV 强调联网与应用；普通 TV 仅接有线或外接盒子

**game console**
英/美 /ɡeɪm ˈkɒnsəʊl/
n. 游戏主机
[义] An electronic device designed mainly for playing video games on a TV or monitor. 主要用来在电视或显示器上玩电子游戏的电子设备（video game console 电子游戏主机, handheld console 掌机, next-gen console 次世代主机）。可数。
[例] ① He saved up to buy the latest game console. 他攒钱买了最新款游戏主机。 ② The game console connects to the TV with an HDMI cable. 游戏主机用 HDMI 线连电视。
[搭] video game console 电子游戏主机, handheld console 掌机, console game 主机游戏, connect a console 连接主机
[联] controller 手柄, video game 电子游戏, TV 电视, screen 屏幕, gaming 游戏, PlayStation 索尼 PlayStation, Xbox 微软 Xbox
[记] 可数：a game console。console 单独可指「主机」，但易与「控制台」混淆，gaming 语境下义明确

**controller**
英/美 /kənˈtrəʊlə(r)/
n. 控制器；手柄
[义] A device used to control a machine or video game; a person who manages something. 控制机器或电子游戏的装置；管理者（game controller 游戏手柄, wireless controller 无线手柄, air-traffic controller 空管员）。可数。
[例] ① Pass me the other controller—this one's battery is dead. 把另一个手柄递我，这个没电了。 ② The wireless controller connects to the console via Bluetooth. 无线手柄通过蓝牙连主机。
[搭] game controller 游戏手柄, wireless controller 无线手柄, controller battery 手柄电池, pair a controller 配对手柄
[联] game console 游戏主机, button 按钮, joystick 摇杆, gaming 游戏, player 玩家, Bluetooth 蓝牙, video game 电子游戏
[记] 可数：a controller。游戏语境下 controller 与 gamepad 常可互换；joystick 多指带杆操控。与「管理者」义同形，看语境

**smartwatch**
英/美 /ˈsmɑːtwɒtʃ/
n. 智能手表
[义] A watch that connects to a smartphone, shows notifications, and often tracks fitness or health data. 可连接智能手机、显示通知并常具备健身或健康监测功能的腕表（wear a smartwatch 戴智能手表, smartwatch band 表带, fitness tracking 健身追踪）。可数。
[例] ① Her smartwatch buzzed with a message from work. 她的智能手表震了一下，是工作消息。 ② He checks his step count on his smartwatch every evening. 他每晚看智能手表上的步数。
[搭] wear a smartwatch 戴智能手表, smartwatch band 表带, fitness tracking 健身追踪, sync with a phone 与手机同步
[联] watch 手表, smartphone 智能手机, wearable 可穿戴设备, fitness tracker 运动手环, notification 通知, battery 电池, screen 屏幕
[记] 可数：a smartwatch。亦写作 smart watch（两词）。fitness tracker 功能重叠但 tracker 通常无完整表盘

**drone**
英/美 /drəʊn/
n. 无人机；嗡嗡声
v. 嗡嗡作响；单调地说
[义] A small aircraft without a pilot, controlled remotely, often with a camera for photography or video. 无飞行员、遥控飞行的小型飞行器，常带相机用于拍照或录像（camera drone 航拍无人机, fly a drone 飞无人机, drone footage 无人机画面）。可数。
[例] ① They used a drone to film the wedding from above. 他们用无人机从上方拍婚礼。 ② New rules limit where you can fly a drone in the city. 新规限制了城里可飞无人机的区域。
[搭] camera drone 航拍无人机, fly a drone 飞无人机, drone footage 无人机画面, drone battery 无人机电池, remote control 遥控
[联] camera 相机, remote 遥控, aircraft 飞行器, video 视频, photograph 拍照, pilot 飞手, battery 电池
[记] 可数：a drone。军事语境 drone 可指「无人攻击机」；消费语境多指带相机的航拍机。动词 drone on 指「单调唠叨」

**e-reader**
英/美 /ˈiː riːdə(r)/
n. 电子阅读器
[义] A portable device designed mainly for reading digital books and documents. 主要用于阅读电子书与文档的便携设备（Kindle e-reader Kindle 阅读器, download books to an e-reader 往阅读器下载书籍）。可数。
[例] ① She prefers an e-reader for travel because it is lighter than hardcovers. 旅行她更爱用阅读器，比精装书轻。 ② The e-reader's screen is easy on the eyes in bright sunlight. 这款阅读器屏幕在强光下也不刺眼。
[搭] Kindle e-reader Kindle 阅读器, e-reader screen 阅读器屏幕, download books 下载书籍, digital book 电子书
[联] tablet 平板, book 书, screen 屏幕, Kindle Kindle, download 下载, portable 便携的, smartphone 智能手机
[记] 可数：an e-reader。亦写作 e-reader / eReader。与 tablet 相比，e-reader 专攻阅读、通常无彩色大屏

**smart speaker**
英/美 /smɑːt ˈspiːkə(r)/
n. 智能音箱
[义] A speaker connected to the internet that responds to voice commands, plays music, and controls smart home devices. 连网、可响应语音指令、播放音乐并控制智能家居的音箱（voice-controlled speaker 声控音箱, set up a smart speaker 设置智能音箱）。可数。
[例] ① Ask the smart speaker to play your playlist. 让智能音箱播放你的歌单。 ② They placed a smart speaker in the kitchen for recipes and timers. 他们在厨房放了一个智能音箱查菜谱、设计时。
[搭] voice-controlled speaker 声控音箱, set up a smart speaker 设置智能音箱, smart home 智能家居, voice command 语音指令
[联] speaker 扬声器, microphone 麦克风, voice 语音, music 音乐, smart home 智能家居, Wi-Fi 无线网络, assistant 助手
[记] 可数：a smart speaker。常见品牌如 Echo、HomePod；产品类别词，非专指某一品牌

**power bank**
英/美 /ˈpaʊə bæŋk/
n. 移动电源；充电宝
[义] A portable battery pack used to charge phones and other devices when no wall outlet is available. 在无插座时为手机等设备充电的便携电池（portable charger 便携充电器, charge a phone with a power bank 用充电宝给手机充电）。可数。
[例] ① I always carry a power bank when I travel. 出门旅行我总带充电宝。 ② The power bank can fully charge my phone twice. 这个充电宝能给我的手机满充两次。
[搭] portable charger 便携充电器, power bank capacity 充电宝容量, charge a phone 给手机充电, USB port USB 口
[联] charger 充电器, battery 电池, cable 数据线, portable 便携的, phone 手机, travel 旅行, outlet 插座
[记] 可数：a power bank。亦说 portable charger / external battery。capacity 常以 mAh 标注容量

**USB**
英/美 /ˌjuː es ˈbiː/
n. USB 接口；USB 标准
[义] Universal Serial Bus; a standard port and cable type used to connect and power phones, computers, and accessories. 通用串行总线；用于连接并为手机、电脑及配件供电的接口与线缆标准（USB port USB 口, USB cable 数据线, USB-C USB-C 接口）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Plug the drive into the USB port on the side of the laptop. 把 U 盘插到笔记本侧面的 USB 口。 ② Most new phones use USB-C for charging. 多数新手机用 USB-C 充电。
[搭] USB port USB 口, USB cable 数据线, USB drive U 盘, USB-C USB-C 接口, USB hub 扩展坞
[联] cable 数据线, charger 充电器, port 接口, plug 插头, adapter 适配器, computer 电脑, connect 连接
[记] 常大写 USB。USB-C / USB-A 指不同物理接口形状。flash drive / USB drive 指「U 盘」

**flash drive**
英/美 /flæʃ draɪv/
n. U 盘；闪存盘
[义] A small portable storage device that plugs into a USB port to copy and carry files. 插入 USB 口、用于复制与携带文件的小型便携存储设备（USB flash drive U 盘, plug in a flash drive 插入 U 盘, copy files to a flash drive 把文件拷到 U 盘）。可数。
[例] ① She saved the presentation on a flash drive and brought it to the meeting. 她把演示文稿存进 U 盘带到会场。 ② Never plug an unknown flash drive into your work computer. 别往工作电脑插来历不明的 U 盘。
[搭] USB flash drive U 盘, plug in a flash drive 插入 U 盘, copy files 复制文件, storage device 存储设备
[联] USB USB 接口, file 文件, storage 存储, computer 电脑, backup 备份, memory 存储, portable 便携的
[记] 可数：a flash drive。亦说 USB drive / thumb drive（美式口语）。cloud storage 为另一存储方式

**modem**
英/美 /ˈməʊdem/
n. 调制解调器；光猫
[义] A device that connects a home or office to an internet service through a phone line, cable, or fibre. 通过电话线、电缆或光纤将家庭或办公室连到互联网服务的设备（cable modem 电缆调制解调器, reset the modem 重启光猫, modem and router 猫和路由器）。可数。
[例] ① The technician replaced the old modem during the visit. 师傅上门时换了个旧光猫。 ② If pages load slowly, try rebooting the modem first. 网页加载慢就先重启调制解调器试试。
[搭] cable modem 电缆调制解调器, reset the modem 重启调制解调器, modem and router 猫和路由器, internet modem 上网调制解调器
[联] router 路由器, broadband 宽带, internet 互联网, Wi-Fi 无线网络, connection 连接, ISP 网络服务商, signal 信号
[记] 可数：a modem。口语常说「光猫」。许多家用设备将 modem 与 router 合为一体

**projector**
英/美 /prəˈdʒektə(r)/
n. 投影仪
[义] A device that shines light through an image or video so it appears large on a wall or screen. 把图像或视频用光投射到墙面或幕布上放大的设备（video projector 视频投影仪, connect a projector 连接投影仪, projector screen 投影幕布）。可数。
[例] ① The teacher connected a laptop to the classroom projector. 老师把笔记本连上了教室投影仪。 ② They set up a projector in the backyard for movie night. 他们在后院架了投影仪办电影之夜。
[搭] video projector 视频投影仪, connect a projector 连接投影仪, projector screen 投影幕布, home projector 家用投影仪
[联] screen 屏幕, monitor 显示器, presentation 演示, movie 电影, HDMI HDMI 线, laptop 笔记本, display 显示
[记] 可数：a projector。project 作动词（投射）同族。home theater 常配 projector + screen

**earbud**
英/美 /ˈɪəbʌd/
n. 耳塞式耳机
（常用复数 earbuds）
[义] A small headphone that fits inside the ear, often wireless and used with phones. 放入耳内的小型耳机，常为无线、与手机配用（wireless earbuds 无线耳塞, noise-cancelling earbuds 降噪耳塞, earbud case 耳机充电盒）。常用复数 earbuds。
[例] ① She lost one earbud on the train. 她在火车上丢了一只耳塞。 ② The earbuds come in a charging case that fits in your pocket. 耳塞配充电盒，能装进口袋。
[搭] wireless earbuds 无线耳塞, noise-cancelling earbuds 降噪耳塞, earbud case 耳机充电盒, put in earbuds 戴上耳塞
[联] headphone 耳机, Bluetooth 蓝牙, smartphone 智能手机, music 音乐, microphone 麦克风, charger 充电器, audio 音频
[记] 常用复数 earbuds。AirPods 为品牌名；earbud 为通用类别词。与 over-ear headphones 相对

**fitness tracker**
英/美 /ˈfɪtnəs ˌtrækə(r)/
n. 运动手环；健身追踪器
[义] A wearable device that counts steps, heart rate, sleep, and other health-related activity. 可穿戴设备，计步、测心率、睡眠等健康相关数据（wear a fitness tracker 戴运动手环, fitness tracker app 配套应用, step count 步数）。可数。
[例] ① Her fitness tracker reminded her to stand up every hour. 运动手环提醒她每小时站起来。 ② He syncs his fitness tracker with an app on his phone. 他把运动手环和手机上的应用同步。
[搭] wear a fitness tracker 戴运动手环, step count 步数, heart rate 心率, sync with an app 与应用同步, fitness band 运动手环（同义）
[联] smartwatch 智能手表, wearable 可穿戴设备, exercise 锻炼, step 步, health 健康, battery 电池, smartphone 智能手机
[记] 可数：a fitness tracker。与 smartwatch 功能重叠；tracker 通常更轻、屏幕更小。亦说 fitness band

**VR headset**
英/美 /ˌviː ˈɑː ˈhedset/
n. VR 头显；虚拟现实头盔
[义] A device worn on the head with screens in front of the eyes, used for virtual-reality games and experiences. 戴在头上、眼前有屏幕的装置，用于虚拟现实游戏与体验（put on a VR headset 戴上 VR 头显, VR gaming VR 游戏, wireless VR headset 无线 VR 头盔）。可数。
[例] ① He put on the VR headset and walked around the virtual room. 他戴上 VR 头显在虚拟房间里走动。 ② Some people feel dizzy after using a VR headset for too long. 有人戴 VR 头显太久会头晕。
[搭] put on a VR headset 戴上 VR 头显, VR gaming VR 游戏, wireless VR headset 无线 VR 头盔, VR experience VR 体验
[联] game console 游戏主机, controller 手柄, screen 屏幕, gaming 游戏, virtual 虚拟的, immersive 沉浸式的, video 视频
[记] 可数：a VR headset。VR = virtual reality。AR headset（增强现实）为相关产品，义不同

**hard drive**
英/美 /hɑːd draɪv/
n. 硬盘
[义] A device inside or connected to a computer that stores large amounts of data on spinning disks or solid-state memory. 电脑内部或外接、在磁盘或固态存储上保存大量数据的装置（internal hard drive 内置硬盘, external hard drive 移动硬盘, hard drive failure 硬盘故障）。可数。
[例] ① The laptop's hard drive was almost full of photos. 笔记本硬盘几乎被照片占满。 ② He backed up his files to an external hard drive. 他把文件备份到移动硬盘。
[搭] internal hard drive 内置硬盘, external hard drive 移动硬盘, hard drive space 硬盘空间, hard drive failure 硬盘故障, back up to a hard drive 备份到硬盘
[联] SSD 固态硬盘, storage 存储, memory 内存, computer 电脑, file 文件, backup 备份, data 数据
[记] 可数：a hard drive。HDD 常指传统机械硬盘；与 SSD 相对。口语说「硬盘满了」极常见

**SSD**
英/美 /ˌes es ˈdiː/
n. 固态硬盘
[义] Solid State Drive; a type of storage with no moving parts, faster and more durable than traditional hard drives. 固态硬盘；无机械部件的存储设备，比传统硬盘更快、更抗震（install an SSD 安装固态硬盘, SSD storage 固态存储, upgrade to an SSD 升级到 SSD）。可数。
[例] ① Replacing the old drive with an SSD made the computer much faster. 把旧硬盘换成 SSD 后电脑快很多。 ② The laptop comes with a 512 GB SSD. 这款笔记本配 512 GB 固态硬盘。
[搭] install an SSD 安装固态硬盘, SSD storage 固态存储, upgrade to an SSD 升级到 SSD, SSD capacity 固态容量
[联] hard drive 硬盘, storage 存储, memory 内存, computer 电脑, fast 快的, data 数据, boot 启动
[记] 可数：an SSD。Solid State Drive 全称。消费语境谈电脑配置时 SSD 为高频词

**processor**
英/美 /ˈprəʊsesə(r)/
n. 处理器；芯片
[义] The main chip in a computer or phone that performs calculations and runs programs; a machine that processes something. 电脑或手机中执行运算、运行程序的主芯片；加工某物的机器（computer processor 电脑处理器, fast processor 高性能处理器, food processor 食物料理机）。可数。
[例] ① Video editing needs a powerful processor and plenty of memory. 视频剪辑需要强处理器和大内存。 ② The new phone has a faster processor than last year's model. 新款手机处理器比去年的型号更快。
[搭] computer processor 电脑处理器, fast processor 高性能处理器, processor speed 处理器速度, upgrade the processor 升级处理器
[联] chip 芯片, CPU 中央处理器, memory 内存, computer 电脑, laptop 笔记本, smartphone 智能手机, speed 速度
[记] 可数：a processor。CPU 为同义缩写。food processor 指「食物料理机」，不同义

**stylus**
英/美 /ˈstaɪləs/
n. 触控笔；手写笔
[义] A pen-shaped tool used to write or draw on a touchscreen or tablet. 笔形工具，用于在触摸屏或平板上书写、绘图（stylus pen 触控笔, use a stylus 用触控笔, compatible stylus 兼容手写笔）。可数。
[例] ① She takes notes on her tablet with a stylus. 她用触控笔在平板上记笔记。 ② The stylus attaches magnetically to the side of the device. 触控笔磁吸在设备侧面。
[搭] stylus pen 触控笔, use a stylus 用触控笔, compatible stylus 兼容手写笔, replace the stylus tip 更换笔头
[联] tablet 平板, touchscreen 触摸屏, screen 屏幕, draw 绘画, write 书写, finger 手指, note 笔记
[记] 可数：a stylus。复数 styluses 或 styli。finger vs. stylus：精细绘图多用 stylus

**adapter**
英/美 /əˈdæptə(r)/
n. 适配器；转换插头
[义] A device that lets equipment work with a different type of plug, port, or power supply. 使设备能配合不同插头、接口或电源的装置（power adapter 电源适配器, travel adapter 旅行转换插头, USB adapter 转接头）。可数。
[例] ① Bring a travel adapter if you are visiting another country. 去国外要带旅行转换插头。 ② The laptop charger includes a small power adapter. 笔记本充电器含一个小电源适配器。
[搭] power adapter 电源适配器, travel adapter 旅行转换插头, USB adapter 转接头, plug adapter 插头转换器
[联] charger 充电器, plug 插头, cable 数据线, socket 插座, USB USB 接口, port 接口, voltage 电压
[记] 可数：an adapter。英式拼写 adaptor 亦见。adapter vs. charger：adapter 强调「转换/适配」，charger 强调「充电」

**tripod**
英/美 /ˈtraɪpɒd/
n. 三脚架
[义] A stand with three legs used to hold a camera, phone, or projector steady. 三条腿支架，用来稳定支撑相机、手机或投影仪（camera tripod 相机三脚架, tripod mount 三脚架云台, set up a tripod 架三脚架）。可数。
[例] ① She mounted the camera on a tripod for the long exposure shot. 她为长曝光把相机架在三脚架上。 ② A small tripod helps when filming videos on your phone. 用手机拍视频时小脚架很有用。
[搭] camera tripod 相机三脚架, tripod mount 三脚架云台, set up a tripod 架三脚架, foldable tripod 可折叠三脚架
[联] camera 相机, photo 照片, video 视频, stable 稳定的, mount 安装, shot 镜头, photography 摄影
[记] 可数：a tripod。tri-（三）+ pod（脚）。smartphone tripod 为常见配件类别

**blender**
英/美 /ˈblendə(r)/
n. 搅拌机；榨汁机
[义] An electric kitchen machine with spinning blades used to mix, chop, or liquidize food. 带旋转刀片的电动厨房机器，用于混合、切碎或打浆食物（kitchen blender 厨房搅拌机, hand blender 手持搅拌棒, blend smoothies 打果昔）。可数。
[例] ① She made a smoothie in the blender before work. 上班前她用搅拌机打了果昔。 ② The blender is loud, so she closes the kitchen door. 搅拌机很响，她把厨房门关上了。
[搭] kitchen blender 厨房搅拌机, hand blender 手持搅拌棒, blend smoothies 打果昔, blender jar 搅拌杯
[联] mixer 搅拌器, kitchen 厨房, appliance 电器, juice 果汁, food 食物, blade 刀片, electric 电动的
[记] 可数：a blender。blend 作动词（混合）。immersion blender = hand blender 手持搅拌棒。大功率碎冰款见 high-speed blender

**vacuum cleaner**
英/美 /ˈvækjuːm ˌkliːnə(r)/
n. 吸尘器
[义] An electric machine that sucks up dust and dirt from floors and carpets. 从地板和地毯吸除灰尘污垢的电动机器（cordless vacuum cleaner 无线吸尘器, vacuum the carpet 吸地毯, vacuum cleaner bag 尘袋）。可数。
[例] ① He runs the vacuum cleaner every Saturday morning. 他每周六早上用吸尘器。 ② The cordless vacuum cleaner is easier to carry upstairs. 无线吸尘器搬上楼更方便。
[搭] cordless vacuum cleaner 无线吸尘器, vacuum the carpet 吸地毯, vacuum cleaner bag 尘袋, robot vacuum 扫地机器人
[联] clean 清洁, dust 灰尘, carpet 地毯, floor 地板, appliance 电器, robot 机器人, household 家用的
[记] 可数：a vacuum cleaner。口语常简称 vacuum：run the vacuum。robot vacuum / robovac 指扫地机器人

**robot vacuum**
英/美 /ˈrəʊbɒt ˈvækjuːm/
n. 扫地机器人
[义] A small automatic vacuum cleaner that moves around the floor on its own to clean dust and dirt. 在地面自行移动、自动吸尘的小型吸尘器（robot vacuum cleaner 扫地机器人, schedule the robot vacuum 预约清扫, empty the dust bin 清空尘盒）。可数。
[例] ① The robot vacuum runs every morning while they are at work. 扫地机器人每天他们上班时自动清扫。 ② Hair gets tangled in the robot vacuum's brushes sometimes. 头发有时会缠在扫地机器人的刷子上。
[搭] robot vacuum cleaner 扫地机器人, schedule cleaning 预约清扫, empty the dust bin 清空尘盒, mapping 地图建图
[联] vacuum cleaner 吸尘器, smart home 智能家居, floor 地板, dust 灰尘, battery 电池, sensor 传感器, appliance 电器
[记] 可数：a robot vacuum。亦说 robovac（口语/品牌语境）。属 smart home 常见设备

**air purifier**
英/美 /eə ˈpjʊərɪfaɪə(r)/
n. 空气净化器
[义] A device that removes dust, pollen, smoke, and other particles from the air in a room. 去除室内空气中灰尘、花粉、烟雾等颗粒物的设备（HEPA air purifier HEPA 空气净化器, run an air purifier 开空气净化器, replace the filter 更换滤芯）。可数。
[例] ① They bought an air purifier for the baby's room. 他们给婴儿房买了空气净化器。 ② The air purifier's filter should be changed every few months. 空气净化器滤芯每隔几个月要换。
[搭] HEPA air purifier HEPA 空气净化器, run an air purifier 开空气净化器, replace the filter 更换滤芯, air quality 空气质量
[联] filter 滤芯, air 空气, dust 灰尘, pollen 花粉, smoke 烟雾, appliance 电器, smart home 智能家居
[记] 可数：an air purifier。与 humidifier（加湿器）、dehumidifier（除湿机）常并列提及

**smart thermostat**
英/美 /smɑːt ˈθɜːməstæt/
n. 智能温控器
[义] A thermostat connected to Wi-Fi that lets you control heating and cooling from an app or by voice. 连 Wi-Fi 的温控器，可用应用或语音控制冷暖（install a smart thermostat 安装智能温控器, adjust the temperature 调节温度, energy saving 节能）。可数。
[例] ① She lowered the heat from her phone using the smart thermostat. 她用手机通过智能温控器把暖气调低了。 ② The smart thermostat learns your schedule over time. 智能温控器会逐渐学习你的作息。
[搭] install a smart thermostat 安装智能温控器, adjust the temperature 调节温度, energy saving 节能, Wi-Fi thermostat Wi-Fi 温控器
[联] smart home 智能家居, temperature 温度, heating 暖气, cooling 制冷, Wi-Fi 无线网络, sensor 传感器, appliance 电器
[记] 可数：a smart thermostat。常见消费级 smart home 设备。thermostat 亦可指普通温控器

**doorbell camera**
英/美 /ˈdɔːbel ˌkæmərə/
n. 门铃摄像头；可视门铃
[义] A doorbell with a built-in camera and microphone that lets you see and speak to visitors on your phone. 内置摄像头和麦克风、可在手机上查看并与访客通话的门铃（video doorbell 可视门铃, doorbell camera app 门铃应用, answer the door remotely 远程应门）。可数。
[例] ① The doorbell camera sent an alert when a package was delivered. 快递送达时门铃摄像头发了通知。 ② She checked the doorbell camera before opening the door. 开门前她看了门铃摄像头画面。
[搭] video doorbell 可视门铃, doorbell camera app 门铃应用, answer the door remotely 远程应门, motion alert 移动侦测提醒
[联] camera 摄像头, smart home 智能家居, security 安防, door 门, visitor 访客, smartphone 智能手机, notification 通知
[记] 可数：a doorbell camera。Ring 等为常见品牌；doorbell camera 为产品类别词

**microwave**
英/美 /ˈmaɪkrəweɪv/
n. 微波炉
v. 用微波炉加热
[义] An oven that cooks or heats food quickly using electromagnetic waves; to cook food in such an oven. 用电磁波快速烹饪或加热食物的炉具；用微波炉加热（microwave oven 微波炉, heat in the microwave 用微波加热, microwave-safe container 可微波容器）。可数/动词。
[例] ① She reheated the soup in the microwave for two minutes. 她用微波炉把汤热了两分钟。 ② Do not put metal in the microwave. 别把金属放进微波炉。
[搭] microwave oven 微波炉, heat in the microwave 用微波加热, microwave-safe 可微波的, microwave timer 微波炉定时
[联] oven 烤箱, kitchen 厨房, appliance 电器, heat 加热, cook 烹饪, food 食物, timer 定时器
[记] 可数：a microwave。口语常说 microwave 代指 microwave oven。microwave 亦指「微波」物理概念，语境区分

**toaster**
英/美 /ˈtəʊstə(r)/
n. 烤面包机
[义] A small electric machine that browns slices of bread by heating them. 通过加热把面包片烤至褐色的小型电器（pop-up toaster 弹出式烤面包机, toast bread 烤面包, toaster oven 小烤箱）。可数。
[例] ① The toaster burned the bread because she forgot to set the timer. 她忘了定时，烤面包机把面包烤糊了。 ② He made breakfast with the toaster and a coffee maker. 他用烤面包机和咖啡机做早餐。
[搭] pop-up toaster 弹出式烤面包机, toast bread 烤面包, toaster oven 小烤箱, slice of toast 一片烤面包
[联] bread 面包, breakfast 早餐, kitchen 厨房, appliance 电器, oven 烤箱, heat 加热, butter 黄油
[记] 可数：a toaster。toast 作动词（烤）与名词（烤面包片）。toaster oven 兼具小烤箱功能

**coffee maker**
英/美 /ˈkɒfi meɪkə(r)/
n. 咖啡机
[义] An electric machine used to brew coffee, from simple drip models to espresso machines. 用来冲泡咖啡的电器，从简单滴漏式到意式浓缩机（drip coffee maker 滴漏咖啡机, espresso machine 意式咖啡机, brew coffee 煮咖啡）。可数。
[例] ① The coffee maker starts automatically every morning at six. 咖啡机每天早上六点自动启动。 ② They upgraded to a coffee maker with a built-in grinder. 他们换了一台带内置研磨器的咖啡机。
[搭] drip coffee maker 滴漏咖啡机, espresso machine 意式咖啡机, brew coffee 煮咖啡, coffee filter 咖啡滤纸
[联] coffee 咖啡, kitchen 厨房, appliance 电器, mug 杯子, breakfast 早餐, kettle 水壶, grinder 研磨器
[记] 可数：a coffee maker。espresso machine 专指意式机，通常更贵、更复杂

**rice cooker**
英/美 /ˈraɪs kʊkə(r)/
n. 电饭煲
[义] An electric pot that cooks rice automatically by controlling heat and timing. 自动控制火候与时间煮饭的电锅（electric rice cooker 电饭煲, cook rice 煮饭, rice cooker pot 内胆）。可数。
[例] ① She put rice and water in the rice cooker and pressed start. 她把米和水放进电饭煲按了开始。 ② The rice cooker keeps rice warm for hours after cooking. 电饭煲煮好后能保温数小时。
[搭] electric rice cooker 电饭煲, cook rice 煮饭, rice cooker pot 内胆, keep-warm function 保温功能
[联] rice 米饭, kitchen 厨房, appliance 电器, cook 烹饪, pot 锅, steam 蒸, meal 餐
[记] 可数：a rice cooker。亚洲家庭高频厨电。亦可用于蒸菜、煮粥，视型号而定

**dishwasher**
英/美 /ˈdɪʃwɔːʃə(r)/
n. 洗碗机
[义] A machine that washes dishes, glasses, and cutlery automatically with water and detergent. 用水和洗涤剂自动清洗碗碟、杯子和餐具的机器（run the dishwasher 开洗碗机, load the dishwasher 装洗碗机, built-in dishwasher 嵌入式洗碗机）。可数。
[例] ① He loaded the dishwasher after dinner and set it to run overnight. 晚饭后他把碗装进洗碗机，设成夜间运行。 ② The dishwasher saves them a lot of time during busy weeks. 忙的时候洗碗机帮他们省很多时间。
[搭] run the dishwasher 开洗碗机, load the dishwasher 装洗碗机, built-in dishwasher 嵌入式洗碗机, dishwasher detergent 洗碗粉/块
[联] kitchen 厨房, appliance 电器, dish 碗碟, clean 清洁, water 水, soap 洗涤剂, sink 水槽
[记] 可数：a dishwasher。load vs. unload the dishwasher。西式厨房常见标配

**dryer**
英/美 /ˈdraɪə(r)/
n. 烘干机；吹风机
[义] A machine that dries clothes with heat after washing; a device that blows hot air to dry hair. 洗衣后用热气烘干衣物的机器；吹热风干发的装置（clothes dryer 烘干机, tumble dryer 滚筒烘干机, hair dryer 吹风机）。可数。
[例] ① He moved the wet laundry from the washer to the dryer. 他把湿衣服从洗衣机移到烘干机。 ② The dryer shrank her favourite sweater. 烘干机把她最爱的毛衣缩小了。
[搭] clothes dryer 烘干机, tumble dryer 滚筒烘干机, dryer sheet 烘干香衣纸, run the dryer 开烘干机
[联] washing machine 洗衣机, laundry 洗衣, clothes 衣服, heat 热量, appliance 电器, shrink 缩水, wet 湿的
[记] 可数：a dryer。clothes dryer vs. hair dryer 语境区分。美式 laundry 常指 washer + dryer 组合

**refrigerator**
英/美 /rɪˈfrɪdʒərətə(r)/
n. 冰箱
[义] A large electrical appliance that keeps food and drinks cold to slow spoilage. 使食物和饮料保持低温、延缓变质的大型电器（fridge 冰箱〔口语〕, refrigerator door 冰箱门, side-by-side refrigerator 对开门冰箱）。可数。
[例] ① She stored the leftovers in the refrigerator. 她把剩菜放进冰箱。 ② The refrigerator motor hums quietly in the kitchen. 冰箱电机在厨房里轻声嗡嗡响。
[搭] fridge 冰箱, refrigerator door 冰箱门, side-by-side refrigerator 对开门冰箱, open the fridge 开冰箱
[联] freezer 冷冻室, kitchen 厨房, appliance 电器, food 食物, cold 冷的, fresh 新鲜的, cool 冷藏
[记] 可数：a refrigerator。口语几乎都说 fridge。freezer 指冷冻部分或独立冷冻柜

**air fryer**
英/美 /eə ˈfraɪə(r)/
n. 空气炸锅
[义] A small kitchen appliance that cooks food with hot circulating air, producing a fried texture with little or no oil. 用热风循环烹饪的小型厨电，少油或无油也能做出油炸口感（use an air fryer 用空气炸锅, air-fried chicken 空气炸鸡肉, air fryer basket 炸篮）。可数。
[例] ① She cooks frozen fries in the air fryer instead of deep-frying them. 她用空气炸锅做冷冻薯条，不再油炸。 ② The air fryer heats up faster than a conventional oven. 空气炸锅比传统烤箱升温更快。
[搭] use an air fryer 用空气炸锅, air-fried chicken 空气炸鸡肉, air fryer basket 炸篮, preheat the air fryer 预热空气炸锅
[联] oven 烤箱, kitchen 厨房, appliance 电器, cook 烹饪, oil 油, fry 炸, healthy 健康的
[记] 可数：an air fryer。近十年消费厨房电器热门品类。与 deep fryer（油炸锅）烹饪方式不同

**soundbar**
英/美 /ˈsaʊndbɑː(r)/
n. 回音壁；条形音箱
[义] A long, slim speaker designed to sit below a TV and improve sound quality. 细长音箱，设计置于电视下方以改善音质（TV soundbar 电视回音壁, connect a soundbar 连接回音壁, wireless soundbar 无线回音壁）。可数。
[例] ① They added a soundbar because the TV speakers were too weak. 电视扬声器太弱，他们加了回音壁。 ② The soundbar connects to the TV with HDMI or Bluetooth. 回音壁用 HDMI 或蓝牙连电视。
[搭] TV soundbar 电视回音壁, connect a soundbar 连接回音壁, wireless soundbar 无线回音壁, surround sound 环绕声
[联] speaker 扬声器, TV 电视, audio 音频, Bluetooth 蓝牙, HDMI HDMI 线, bass 低音, movie 电影
[记] 可数：a soundbar。home theater 常见配置：TV + soundbar。比完整音响系统更省空间

**graphics card**
英/美 /ˈɡræfɪks kɑːd/
n. 显卡
[义] A component inside or attached to a computer that processes images and video, especially important for gaming and video editing. 电脑内部或外接的组件，处理图像与视频，对游戏和视频剪辑尤为重要（install a graphics card 安装显卡, dedicated graphics card 独立显卡, graphics card upgrade 显卡升级）。可数。
[例] ① The game needs a powerful graphics card to run smoothly. 这款游戏需要强显卡才能流畅运行。 ② He replaced the old graphics card to speed up video rendering. 他换了旧显卡以加快视频渲染。
[搭] install a graphics card 安装显卡, dedicated graphics card 独立显卡, graphics card upgrade 显卡升级, GPU 图形处理器
[联] processor 处理器, computer 电脑, gaming 游戏, screen 屏幕, video 视频, memory 内存, performance 性能
[记] 可数：a graphics card。GPU 为同义技术缩写。integrated graphics 指「核显/集成显卡」

**memory**
英/美 /ˈmeməri/
n. 内存；记忆
[义] The part of a computer that holds data while programs are running; the ability to remember things. 电脑在程序运行时暂存数据的部件；记住事物的能力（computer memory 电脑内存, RAM 内存, add more memory 加内存, memory card 存储卡）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① The laptop needs more memory to handle large video files. 这台笔记本需要更多内存才能处理大视频文件。 ② He upgraded the memory from 8 GB to 16 GB. 他把内存从 8 GB 升到 16 GB。
[搭] computer memory 电脑内存, add more memory 加内存, memory card 存储卡, RAM 内存, memory upgrade 内存升级
[联] processor 处理器, storage 存储, hard drive 硬盘, computer 电脑, gigabyte 千兆字节, speed 速度, data 数据
[记] 电脑语境 memory = RAM（运行内存），与 storage（硬盘存储）不同。memory card 指相机/手机存储卡

**motherboard**
英/美 /ˈmʌðərbɔːd/
n. 主板
[义] The main circuit board in a computer that connects the processor, memory, and other components. 电脑中连接处理器、内存及其他组件的主电路板（computer motherboard 电脑主板, replace the motherboard 更换主板, motherboard slot 主板插槽）。可数。
[例] ① The technician said the motherboard was damaged by a power surge. 师傅说主板被电涌烧坏了。 ② Every component in the desktop connects to the motherboard. 台式机里每个组件都连在主板上。
[搭] computer motherboard 电脑主板, replace the motherboard 更换主板, motherboard slot 主板插槽, mainboard 主板（同义）
[联] processor 处理器, memory 内存, graphics card 显卡, computer 电脑, circuit 电路, component 组件, desktop 台式机
[记] 可数：a motherboard。mainboard 为同义词。DIY 装机语境高频词

**phone case**
英/美 /fəʊn keɪs/
n. 手机壳
[义] A cover fitted around a phone to protect it from drops, scratches, and dirt. 套在手机上、防摔防刮防污的保护套（protective phone case 保护壳, silicone phone case 硅胶壳, phone case design 手机壳款式）。可数。
[例] ① She dropped her phone, but the case saved the screen. 她手机摔了，但手机壳保住了屏幕。 ② He changes his phone case every few months for fun. 他每隔几个月换手机壳图新鲜。
[搭] protective phone case 保护壳, silicone phone case 硅胶壳, clear phone case 透明壳, phone case cover 手机保护套
[联] smartphone 智能手机, screen protector 屏幕保护膜, drop 摔落, scratch 刮痕, protect 保护, accessory 配件, battery 电池
[记] 可数：a phone case。case 单独在 phone 语境即指手机壳。rugged case 指「防摔加固壳」

**screen protector**
英/美 /skriːn prəˈtektə(r)/
n. 屏幕保护膜
[义] A thin sheet of plastic or glass placed over a phone or tablet screen to prevent scratches and cracks. 贴在手机或平板屏幕上的薄塑料或玻璃膜，防刮防裂（tempered glass screen protector 钢化膜, apply a screen protector 贴保护膜, peel off the protector 撕下保护膜）。可数。
[例] ① The screen protector cracked, but the phone screen was fine. 保护膜裂了，但手机屏幕没事。 ② He watched a video on how to apply a screen protector without bubbles. 他看了如何贴保护膜不留气泡的视频。
[搭] tempered glass screen protector 钢化膜, apply a screen protector 贴保护膜, bubble-free 无气泡, replace the protector 更换保护膜
[联] screen 屏幕, phone 手机, scratch 刮痕, crack 破裂, protect 保护, smartphone 智能手机, case 手机壳
[记] 可数：a screen protector。tempered glass 指「钢化玻璃膜」，比塑料膜更常见

**lens**
英/美 /lens/
n. 镜头；镜片
（复数 lenses /ˈlenzɪz/）
[义] A curved piece of glass or plastic in a camera or phone that focuses light to form an image; a contact lens. 相机或手机中聚焦光线成像的弧形玻璃或塑料部件；隐形眼镜（camera lens 相机镜头, zoom lens 变焦镜头, wide-angle lens 广角镜头）。可数。
[例] ① She switched to a wider lens for landscape photos. 她换广角镜头拍风景。 ② Clean the camera lens gently with a soft cloth. 用软布轻轻擦相机镜头。
[搭] camera lens 相机镜头, zoom lens 变焦镜头, wide-angle lens 广角镜头, lens cap 镜头盖, clean the lens 清洁镜头
[联] camera 相机, photo 照片, focus 对焦, zoom 变焦, glass 玻璃, picture 图片, photography 摄影
[记] 可数：a lens，复数 lenses。contact lens 指「隐形眼镜」。lens 亦指眼镜镜片

**action camera**
英/美 /ˈækʃn ˌkæmərə/
n. 运动相机
[义] A small, rugged camera designed to film sports and outdoor activities, often mountable on helmets or bikes. 小巧坚固、专为运动与户外活动拍摄设计、常可固定在头盔或自行车上的相机（mount an action camera 安装运动相机, action camera footage 运动相机画面, waterproof action camera 防水运动相机）。可数。
[例] ① He mounted an action camera on his helmet for the ski trip. 滑雪旅行他把运动相机固定在头盔上。 ② Action cameras are popular for vlogging while hiking. 徒步时用运动相机拍 vlog 很流行。
[搭] mount an action camera 安装运动相机, waterproof action camera 防水运动相机, action camera mount 支架, helmet mount 头盔支架
[联] camera 相机, video 视频, sport 运动, outdoor 户外, waterproof 防水的, drone 无人机, footage 画面
[记] 可数：an action camera。GoPro 为知名品牌，action camera 为通用类别词

**dash cam**
英/美 /ˈdæʃ kæm/
n. 行车记录仪
[义] A small camera mounted in a car that records video of the road while driving. 安装在车内、行车时录制路况视频的小型摄像头（install a dash cam 安装行车记录仪, dash cam footage 行车记录仪画面, front dash cam 前置行车记录仪）。可数。
[例] ① The dash cam recorded the moment another car cut in front of him. 行车记录仪录下了另一辆车突然插队的瞬间。 ② Insurance companies sometimes accept dash cam footage as evidence. 保险公司有时接受行车记录仪画面作为证据。
[搭] install a dash cam 安装行车记录仪, dash cam footage 行车记录仪画面, front dash cam 前置行车记录仪, loop recording 循环录制
[联] camera 摄像头, car 汽车, drive 驾驶, road 道路, video 视频, record 录制, accident 事故
[记] 可数：a dash cam。dash = dashboard（仪表盘）。亦拼 dashcam

**smart bulb**
英/美 /smɑːt bʌlb/
n. 智能灯泡
[义] A light bulb that connects to Wi-Fi or Bluetooth so you can control brightness and color from an app or voice assistant. 连 Wi-Fi 或蓝牙、可用应用或语音助手调节亮度和颜色的灯泡（install smart bulbs 安装智能灯泡, dim the smart bulb 调暗智能灯, color-changing bulb 变色灯泡）。可数。
[例] ① She dimmed the smart bulbs from her phone before bed. 睡前她用手机把智能灯泡调暗。 ② Smart bulbs can change color for movie nights. 智能灯泡看电影之夜可以换颜色。
[搭] install smart bulbs 安装智能灯泡, dim the smart bulb 调暗, color-changing bulb 变色灯泡, smart lighting 智能照明
[联] smart home 智能家居, light 灯, Wi-Fi 无线网络, app 应用, voice 语音, brightness 亮度, lamp 灯具
[记] 可数：a smart bulb。比换整套智能灯具更便宜的入门方案。smart light 可泛指智能灯

**smart lock**
英/美 /smɑːt lɒk/
n. 智能门锁
[义] A door lock that can be opened with a phone app, code, fingerprint, or key, and often logs entry. 可用手机应用、密码、指纹或钥匙开启，并常记录出入的门锁（install a smart lock 安装智能门锁, unlock with an app 用应用开锁, smart lock code 智能锁密码）。可数。
[例] ① He unlocked the smart lock when his guest arrived. 客人到了他用应用开了智能门锁。 ② The smart lock sends a notification when someone enters the code. 有人输入密码时智能门锁会发通知。
[搭] install a smart lock 安装智能门锁, unlock with an app 用应用开锁, smart lock code 智能锁密码, fingerprint lock 指纹锁
[联] smart home 智能家居, door 门, security 安防, key 钥匙, password 密码, fingerprint 指纹, visitor 访客
[记] 可数：a smart lock。与 doorbell camera 常搭配构成入户安防

**hair dryer**
英/美 /ˈheə draɪə(r)/
n. 吹风机
[义] An electric device that blows hot or warm air to dry wet hair. 吹出热风或温风以吹干湿发的电动装置（blow-dry hair 吹干头发, travel hair dryer 旅行吹风机, hair dryer setting 风速档位）。可数。
[例] ① She used the hair dryer after washing her hair. 洗完头她用吹风机吹干。 ② The hotel room has a built-in hair dryer in the bathroom. 酒店浴室里有内置吹风机。
[搭] blow-dry hair 吹干头发, travel hair dryer 旅行吹风机, hair dryer setting 风速档位, cool air setting 冷风档
[联] dryer 烘干机, hair 头发, bathroom 浴室, heat 热量, wet 湿的, brush 梳子, appliance 电器
[记] 可数：a hair dryer。亦说 blow dryer（美式）。blow-dry 作动词指「吹干头发」

**electric toothbrush**
英/美 /ɪˈlektrɪk ˈtʊθbrʌʃ/
n. 电动牙刷
[义] A toothbrush with a motor that moves the brush head to clean teeth more effectively than manual brushing. 刷头由马达驱动、比手动刷牙清洁更有效的牙刷（charge an electric toothbrush 给电动牙刷充电, replace the brush head 更换刷头, sonic toothbrush 声波牙刷）。可数。
[例] ① She charges her electric toothbrush on the bathroom counter. 她在浴室台面上给电动牙刷充电。 ② Dentists often recommend electric toothbrushes for better cleaning. 牙医常推荐电动牙刷以更好清洁。
[搭] charge an electric toothbrush 给电动牙刷充电, replace the brush head 更换刷头, sonic toothbrush 声波牙刷, rechargeable toothbrush 充电牙刷
[联] toothbrush 牙刷, teeth 牙齿, bathroom 浴室, charger 充电器, battery 电池, clean 清洁, dental 牙齿的
[记] 可数：an electric toothbrush。刷头需定期更换。与 manual toothbrush 相对

**calculator**
英/美 /ˈkælkjuleɪtə(r)/
n. 计算器
[义] A small electronic device used for doing arithmetic; a program on a phone or computer with the same function. 做算术用的小型电子装置；手机或电脑上同功能的程序（pocket calculator 袖珍计算器, scientific calculator 科学计算器, calculator app 计算器应用）。可数。
[例] ① He pulled out a calculator to check the bill. 他掏出计算器核对账单。 ② The phone's built-in calculator is enough for simple math. 手机自带计算器够做简单算术。
[搭] pocket calculator 袖珍计算器, scientific calculator 科学计算器, calculator app 计算器应用, use a calculator 用计算器
[联] math 数学, number 数字, compute 计算, phone 手机, electronic 电子的, button 按钮, screen 屏幕
[记] 可数：a calculator。学生考试有时禁带智能设备，仅允许专用计算器

**GPS navigator**
英/美 /ˌdʒiː piː es ˈnævɪɡeɪtə(r)/
n. GPS 导航仪
[义] A device or system that uses satellite signals to show your position and guide you along a route. 用卫星信号显示位置并引导路线的设备或系统（car GPS navigator 车载导航, GPS navigation 卫星导航, update the maps 更新地图）。可数。
[例] ① The GPS navigator directed them through the mountain roads. GPS 导航仪引导他们穿过山路。 ② Most people now use phone apps instead of a separate GPS navigator. 多数人现在用手机应用，不再单独买导航仪。
[搭] car GPS navigator 车载导航, GPS navigation 卫星导航, update the maps 更新地图, turn-by-turn directions 逐向导航
[联] map 地图, car 汽车, drive 驾驶, route 路线, satellite 卫星, location 位置, smartphone 智能手机
[记] 可数：a GPS navigator。口语常说 GPS 或 satnav（英式）。手机导航已大幅替代独立设备

**joystick**
英/美 /ˈdʒɔɪstɪk/
n. 操纵杆；游戏摇杆
[义] A control lever used in video games, flight simulators, or some machines; moved by hand to control direction or action. 电子游戏、飞行模拟或某些机器上的手控杆，用于控制方向或动作（game joystick 游戏摇杆, flight joystick 飞行摇杆, joystick controller 摇杆控制器）。可数。
[例] ① He prefers a joystick for flight simulation games. 玩飞行模拟游戏他更喜欢摇杆。 ② The arcade machine had a joystick and two buttons. 街机有一根摇杆和两个按钮。
[搭] game joystick 游戏摇杆, flight joystick 飞行摇杆, joystick controller 摇杆控制器, arcade joystick 街机摇杆
[联] controller 手柄, game console 游戏主机, button 按钮, gaming 游戏, pilot 飞行员, arcade 街机, video game 电子游戏
[记] 可数：a joystick。街机、飞行模拟语境高频。与 gamepad/controller 部分功能重叠

**handheld console**
英/美 /ˈhændheld ˈkɒnsəʊl/
n. 掌机；掌上游戏机
[义] A portable video game device with a built-in screen and controls, played without a separate TV. 自带屏幕和操控、无需外接电视的便携游戏机（portable game console 便携游戏机, handheld gaming device 掌上游戏设备, play on a handheld 用掌机玩）。可数。
[例] ① She plays puzzle games on her handheld console during commutes. 通勤时她在掌机上玩益智游戏。 ② The new handheld console can also connect to a TV. 新款掌机也能连电视。
[搭] portable game console 便携游戏机, handheld gaming device 掌上游戏设备, play on a handheld 用掌机玩, handheld screen 掌机屏幕
[联] game console 游戏主机, controller 手柄, video game 电子游戏, portable 便携的, screen 屏幕, gaming 游戏, battery 电池
[记] 可数：a handheld console。Nintendo Switch 等可切换掌机与主机模式。与 game console 不同：默认自带屏幕

**humidifier**
英/美 /hjuːˈmɪdɪfaɪə(r)/
n. 加湿器
[义] A device that adds moisture to the air in a room, often used in dry winter weather or for health comfort. 为室内空气加湿的设备，常用于干燥冬季或健康舒适（room humidifier 房间加湿器, run a humidifier 开加湿器, humidifier tank 加湿器水箱）。可数。
[例] ① They run a humidifier in the bedroom during winter. 冬天他们在卧室开加湿器。 ② Fill the humidifier tank with clean water each day. 每天给加湿器水箱加干净水。
[搭] room humidifier 房间加湿器, run a humidifier 开加湿器, humidifier tank 加湿器水箱, ultrasonic humidifier 超声波加湿器
[联] air purifier 空气净化器, dehumidifier 除湿机, air 空气, moisture 湿气, dry 干燥的, winter 冬天, health 健康
[记] 可数：a humidifier。与 dehumidifier（除湿）、air purifier（净化）功能不同，常并列选购

**radio**
英/美 /ˈreɪdiəʊ/
n. 收音机；无线电
v. 用无线电联系
[义] A device that receives and plays broadcast sound programmes; the system of sending signals through the air without wires. 接收并播放广播声音节目的设备；通过空中无线传送信号的系统（portable radio 便携收音机, turn on the radio 开收音机, radio station 电台）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① He listens to the news on a small radio in the kitchen. 他在厨房用小收音机听新闻。 ② The car radio stopped working after the battery died. 电池没电后车载收音机不工作了。
[搭] portable radio 便携收音机, turn on the radio 开收音机, radio station 电台, car radio 车载收音机, FM radio 调频广播
[联] music 音乐, broadcast 广播, speaker 扬声器, antenna 天线, news 新闻, station 电台, audio 音频
[记] 可数：a radio。radio 亦指「无线电广播」媒介。smart speaker 可播电台，但 radio 仍指传统收音机设备

**fax machine**
英/美 /fæks məˈʃiːn/
n. 传真机
[义] A machine that sends and receives copies of documents over a phone line. 通过电话线发送和接收文件复印件的机器（send a fax 发传真, fax machine paper 传真纸, office fax machine 办公室传真机）。可数。
[例] ① The law firm still keeps a fax machine for signed contracts. 律所仍保留传真机处理签字合同。 ② She fed the document into the fax machine and pressed send. 她把文件放进传真机按了发送。
[搭] send a fax 发传真, fax machine paper 传真纸, office fax machine 办公室传真机, receive a fax 收传真
[联] printer 打印机, scanner 扫描仪, photocopier 复印机, document 文件, office 办公室, phone 电话, paper 纸
[记] 可数：a fax machine。虽已过时，商务与法律场景仍偶见。fax 亦可作动词

**photocopier**
英/美 /ˈfəʊtəʊkɒpiə(r)/
n. 复印机
[义] A machine that makes paper copies of documents, usually by scanning and printing. 复印文件的机器，通常先扫描再打印（office photocopier 办公室复印机, photocopy a document 复印文件, photocopier paper 复印纸）。可数。
[例] ① Please use the photocopier on the second floor. 请使用二楼的复印机。 ② The photocopier jammed because someone used the wrong paper size. 有人用了不对尺寸的纸，复印机卡纸了。
[搭] office photocopier 办公室复印机, photocopy a document 复印文件, photocopier paper 复印纸, copy machine 复印机（同义）
[联] printer 打印机, scanner 扫描仪, fax machine 传真机, document 文件, office 办公室, paper 纸, copy 复印
[记] 可数：a photocopier。口语常说 copy machine。与 printer 功能部分重叠（现代多功能一体机）

**electric kettle**
英/美 /ɪˈlektrɪk ˈketl/
n. 电水壶
[义] A kettle that boils water quickly using electricity, with an automatic shut-off when the water reaches boiling point. 用电快速烧水、水沸后自动断电的水壶（boil water 烧水, cordless electric kettle 无线电水壶, kettle base 底座）。可数。
[例] ① She filled the electric kettle and switched it on for tea. 她装满电水壶烧水泡茶。 ② The electric kettle shuts off automatically when the water boils. 水烧开时电水壶会自动断电。
[搭] boil water 烧水, cordless electric kettle 无线电水壶, kettle base 底座, rapid boil 快速沸腾
[联] kettle 水壶, kitchen 厨房, appliance 电器, tea 茶, coffee 咖啡, hot 热的, water 水
[记] 可数：an electric kettle。无线款指壶身与底座分离。英式泡茶文化里极常见

**oven**
英/美 /ˈʌvn/
n. 烤箱
[义] A chamber in a stove or a separate appliance used for baking, roasting, or heating food with dry heat. 炉灶内的腔体或独立电器，用干热烘烤、炙烤或加热食物（electric oven 电烤箱, preheat the oven 预热烤箱, oven temperature 烤箱温度）。可数。
[例] ① Preheat the oven to 180 degrees before baking the cake. 烤蛋糕前把烤箱预热到 180 度。 ② The pizza came out of the oven golden and crisp. 披萨从烤箱里出来时金黄酥脆。
[搭] electric oven 电烤箱, preheat the oven 预热烤箱, oven temperature 烤箱温度, bake in the oven 用烤箱烘烤
[联] microwave 微波炉, kitchen 厨房, bake 烘烤, roast 烤, heat 加热, stove 炉灶, timer 定时器
[记] 可数：an oven。built-in oven 指嵌入式烤箱。oven vs. microwave：烤箱干热烘烤，微波炉用微波

**juicer**
英/美 /ˈdʒuːsə(r)/
n. 榨汁机
[义] A machine that extracts juice from fruit or vegetables by crushing or squeezing them. 通过挤压或碾碎从水果或蔬菜中提取汁液的机器（citrus juicer 柑橘榨汁机, juice extractor 榨汁机, fresh juice 鲜榨果汁）。可数。
[例] ① She made orange juice with a small citrus juicer. 她用小型柑橘榨汁机做了橙汁。 ② The juicer leaves pulp in a separate container. 榨汁机把果肉留到另一个容器里。
[搭] citrus juicer 柑橘榨汁机, juice extractor 榨汁机, fresh juice 鲜榨果汁, clean the juicer 清洗榨汁机
[联] blender 搅拌机, fruit 水果, juice 果汁, kitchen 厨房, appliance 电器, orange 橙子, healthy 健康的
[记] 可数：a juicer。与 blender 不同：juicer 取汁，blender 打浆混合

**food processor**
英/美 /fuːd ˈprəʊsesə(r)/
n. 食物料理机
[义] An electric kitchen machine with blades that chops, slices, grinds, or mixes food quickly. 带刀片、可快速切碎、切片、研磨或搅拌食物的电动厨房机器（kitchen food processor 厨房料理机, chop vegetables 切菜, food processor blade 刀片）。可数。
[例] ① The food processor chopped the onions in seconds. 料理机几秒钟就把洋葱切好了。 ② She uses a food processor to make hummus at home. 她用料理机在家做鹰嘴豆泥。
[搭] kitchen food processor 厨房料理机, chop vegetables 切菜, food processor blade 刀片, pulse setting 点动模式
[联] blender 搅拌机, kitchen 厨房, appliance 电器, chop 切碎, slice 切片, cook 烹饪, knife 刀
[记] 可数：a food processor。processor 在电脑语境指「处理器」，此处为厨房设备。功能比 blender 更全

**slow cooker**
英/美 /sləʊ ˈkʊkə(r)/
n. 慢炖锅
[义] An electric pot that cooks food slowly at low heat over several hours. 以低温长时间慢炖食物的电锅（crock pot 慢炖锅〔美式品牌俗称〕, slow-cook stew 慢炖炖菜, leave in the slow cooker 放在慢炖锅里炖）。可数。
[例] ① She left beef stew in the slow cooker overnight. 她把炖牛肉放在慢炖锅里炖了一夜。 ② Slow cookers are handy for busy families on weekdays. 慢炖锅对工作日忙碌的家庭很实用。
[搭] crock pot 慢炖锅, slow-cook stew 慢炖炖菜, low heat 低温, slow cooker recipe 慢炖菜谱
[联] rice cooker 电饭煲, kitchen 厨房, appliance 电器, cook 烹饪, stew 炖菜, pot 锅, heat 加热
[记] 可数：a slow cooker。crock pot 为美式常见品牌俗称，泛指慢炖锅

**dehumidifier**
英/美 /ˌdiːhjuːˈmɪdɪfaɪə(r)/
n. 除湿机
[义] A device that removes excess moisture from the air in a room, making it feel drier and less damp. 去除室内空气中多余湿气、使环境更干爽的设备（room dehumidifier 房间除湿机, run a dehumidifier 开除湿机, empty the water tank 倒水箱）。可数。
[例] ① They use a dehumidifier in the basement to prevent mold. 他们在地下室用除湿机防霉。 ② The dehumidifier fills its tank quickly on humid days. 潮湿天除湿机水箱很快就满。
[搭] room dehumidifier 房间除湿机, run a dehumidifier 开除湿机, empty the water tank 倒水箱, humidity level 湿度水平
[联] humidifier 加湿器, air purifier 空气净化器, moisture 湿气, damp 潮湿的, mold 霉菌, basement 地下室, air 空气
[记] 可数：a dehumidifier。与 humidifier 功能相反。梅雨、地下室场景常见

**electric fan**
英/美 /ɪˈlektrɪk fæn/
n. 电风扇
[义] A device with rotating blades powered by electricity that moves air to create a cooling breeze. 用电驱动旋转叶片推动空气、产生凉风降温的装置（desk fan 台扇, ceiling fan 吊扇, fan speed 风速）。可数。
[例] ① She turned on the electric fan because the air conditioner was broken. 空调坏了，她开了电风扇。 ② The desk fan rattled when set to the highest speed. 台扇开到最高档时嘎嘎响。
[搭] desk fan 台扇, ceiling fan 吊扇, fan speed 风速, turn on the fan 开电扇
[联] air conditioner 空调, cool 凉爽, breeze 微风, heat 炎热, summer 夏天, blade 叶片, appliance 电器
[记] 可数：an electric fan。口语常说 fan。ceiling fan 吊扇与 standing fan 落地扇为常见类型

**air conditioner**
英/美 /eə kənˈdɪʃənə(r)/
n. 空调
[义] A machine that cools and often dehumidifies the air in a building or vehicle. 冷却并常同时除湿建筑物或车内空气的机器（central air conditioner 中央空调, turn on the air conditioner 开空调, air conditioning unit 空调机组）。可数。
[例] ① They installed a new air conditioner before summer arrived. 夏天到来前他们装了新空调。 ② The air conditioner made the office comfortable during the heat wave. 热浪期间空调让办公室很舒服。
[搭] central air conditioner 中央空调, turn on the air conditioner 开空调, air conditioning unit 空调机组, AC unit 空调机
[联] electric fan 电风扇, cool 制冷, heat 炎热, temperature 温度, summer 夏天, appliance 电器, filter 滤芯
[记] 可数：an air conditioner。口语常说 AC / air con（英式）。window unit 指窗式空调

**steam iron**
英/美 /stiːm aɪən/
n. 蒸汽熨斗
[义] An electric iron that releases steam to remove wrinkles from clothes more effectively. 释放蒸汽、更有效去除衣物褶皱的电熨斗（use a steam iron 用蒸汽熨斗, fill the water tank 加水, steam setting 蒸汽档）。可数。
[例] ① She ironed the shirt with a steam iron for a crisp collar. 她用蒸汽熨斗熨衬衫，领口很挺。 ② Fill the steam iron with distilled water to reduce buildup. 蒸汽熨斗加蒸馏水可减少水垢。
[搭] use a steam iron 用蒸汽熨斗, fill the water tank 加水, steam setting 蒸汽档, iron clothes 熨衣服
[联] iron 熨斗, clothes 衣服, laundry 洗衣, wrinkle 褶皱, steam 蒸汽, appliance 电器, shirt 衬衫
[记] 可数：a steam iron。与 dry iron（干熨斗）相对。travel iron 指旅行用小熨斗

**electric shaver**
英/美 /ɪˈlektrɪk ˈʃeɪvə(r)/
n. 电动剃须刀
[义] A device with moving blades powered by electricity used to shave hair from the face or body. 用电驱动刀片、剃除面部或身体毛发的装置（rechargeable electric shaver 充电剃须刀, wet/dry shaver 干湿两用剃须刀, replace the blade 更换刀头）。可数。
[例] ① He packs a compact electric shaver for business trips. 出差他会带小巧的电动剃须刀。 ② The electric shaver needs charging after a few uses. 电动剃须刀用几次就要充电。
[搭] rechargeable electric shaver 充电剃须刀, wet/dry shaver 干湿两用, replace the blade 更换刀头, clean the shaver 清洁剃须刀
[联] razor 剃刀, beard 胡须, bathroom 浴室, charger 充电器, blade 刀片, grooming 打理, battery 电池
[记] 可数：an electric shaver。razor 可指手动剃刀或泛指剃须工具。foil vs. rotary 为两种刀头类型

**hair straightener**
英/美 /ˈheə ˌstreɪnə(r)/
n. 直发器；直发夹板
[义] A heated electrical device with flat plates used to straighten hair by pressing and sliding along strands. 带加热平板的电器，夹压并沿发丝滑动以拉直头发（flat iron 直发夹板, straighten hair 拉直头发, adjustable temperature 可调温度）。可数。
[例] ① She used a hair straightener to smooth her curls before the event. 活动前她用直发器把卷发拉直。 ② The hair straightener has a temperature dial for fine hair. 这款直发器有调温旋钮，适合细发。
[搭] flat iron 直发夹板, straighten hair 拉直头发, adjustable temperature 可调温度, heat setting 温度档位
[联] hair dryer 吹风机, curling iron 卷发棒, hair 头发, heat 热量, style 造型, bathroom 浴室, appliance 电器
[记] 可数：a hair straightener。flat iron 为同义俗称。使用时注意高温防烫伤

**curling iron**
英 /ˈkɜːlɪŋ aɪən/ 美 /ˈkɜːrlɪŋ aɪən/
n. 卷发棒
[义] A heated rod-shaped tool used to curl hair by wrapping strands around it. 加热的棒状工具，把发丝缠绕其上以烫卷（curl hair 卷发, curling wand 卷发棒〔无夹〕, barrel size 棒径尺寸）。可数。
[例] ① She wrapped sections of hair around the curling iron for loose waves. 她把一缕缕头发缠在卷发棒上做出大波浪。 ② A smaller barrel on the curling iron makes tighter curls. 卷发棒棒径越小，卷度越紧。
[搭] curl hair 卷发, curling wand 卷发棒, barrel size 棒径尺寸, heat protectant 隔热喷雾
[联] hair straightener 直发器, hair dryer 吹风机, hair 头发, curl 卷曲, style 造型, heat 热量, bathroom 浴室
[记] 可数：a curling iron。curling wand 通常无夹钳，靠缠绕卷发。curling iron vs. straightener 功能相反

**memory card**
英/美 /ˈmeməri kɑːd/
n. 存储卡
[义] A small removable card that stores digital photos, videos, or files in cameras, phones, and other devices. 在相机、手机等设备中存储数码照片、视频或文件的小型可拆卸卡（insert a memory card 插入存储卡, memory card slot 卡槽, SD memory card SD 存储卡）。可数。
[例] ① The camera stopped recording because the memory card was full. 存储卡满了，相机停止录制。 ② He backed up the photos before formatting the memory card. 格式化存储卡前他先备份了照片。
[搭] insert a memory card 插入存储卡, memory card slot 卡槽, SD memory card SD 存储卡, format the card 格式化存储卡
[联] camera 相机, photo 照片, storage 存储, SD card SD 卡, file 文件, slot 卡槽, backup 备份
[记] 可数：a memory card。SD / microSD 为常见规格。memory 在电脑语境指 RAM，memory card 专指存储卡

**SD card**
英/美 /ˌes diː kɑːd/
n. SD 卡
[义] Secure Digital card; a common type of small memory card used in cameras, drones, and some laptops. 安全数码卡；相机、无人机及部分笔记本常用的微型存储卡（microSD card 微型 SD 卡, insert an SD card 插入 SD 卡, SD card reader 读卡器）。可数。
[例] ① The drone records video directly to an SD card. 无人机直接把视频录到 SD 卡上。 ② She bought a larger SD card for her travel camera. 她为旅行相机买了更大容量的 SD 卡。
[搭] microSD card 微型 SD 卡, insert an SD card 插入 SD 卡, SD card reader 读卡器, SD card capacity 卡容量
[联] memory card 存储卡, camera 相机, drone 无人机, storage 存储, file 文件, gigabyte 千兆字节, reader 读卡器
[记] 可数：an SD card。microSD 更小，常用于手机。容量常以 GB 标注

**camera flash**
英/美 /ˈkæmərə flæʃ/
n. 相机闪光灯
[义] A device on or attached to a camera that produces a brief bright light to illuminate a scene when taking photos in low light. 相机上或外接的装置，弱光拍照时发出短暂强光照亮场景（built-in flash 内置闪光灯, external flash 外置闪光灯, flash photography 闪光摄影）。可数。
[例] ① The built-in camera flash washed out the subject's face. 内置闪光灯把人物面部拍得过曝。 ② He mounted an external flash for indoor event photos. 拍室内活动照他装了外置闪光灯。
[搭] built-in flash 内置闪光灯, external flash 外置闪光灯, flash photography 闪光摄影, turn off the flash 关闪光灯
[联] camera 相机, photo 照片, lens 镜头, light 光, night 夜晚, photography 摄影, brightness 亮度
[记] 可数：a camera flash。flash 单独在摄影语境可指闪光灯或闪光。red-eye 常由闪光引起

**gimbal**
英/美 /ˈɡɪmbəl/
n. 稳定器；云台
[义] A pivoted support that keeps a camera steady while moving, often motorized for phones or action cameras. 枢轴支架，移动时保持相机稳定，手机或运动相机常用电动云台（phone gimbal 手机稳定器, gimbal stabilizer 云台稳定器, balance the gimbal 平衡云台）。可数。
[例] ① The gimbal kept the footage smooth while she walked downstairs. 她下楼时稳定器让画面保持平稳。 ② He uses a phone gimbal for smooth walking shots. 他用手机稳定器拍平稳的行走镜头。
[搭] phone gimbal 手机稳定器, gimbal stabilizer 云台稳定器, balance the gimbal 平衡云台, motorized gimbal 电动云台
[联] camera 相机, video 视频, action camera 运动相机, smooth 平稳的, shoot 拍摄, tripod 三脚架, footage 画面
[记] 可数：a gimbal。与 tripod 不同：gimbal 适合跟拍移动，tripod 适合固定机位

**selfie stick**
英/美 /ˈselfi stɪk/
n. 自拍杆
[义] A telescopic rod that holds a phone or camera at a distance so you can take selfies or group photos. 可伸缩杆，把手机或相机伸远以便自拍或拍合影（extend a selfie stick 伸长自拍杆, selfie stick mount 手机夹, Bluetooth selfie stick 蓝牙自拍杆）。可数。
[例] ① They pulled out a selfie stick for a group photo at the landmark. 他们在地标前拿出自拍杆拍合影。 ② The selfie stick connects to the phone via Bluetooth for the shutter button. 自拍杆通过蓝牙连手机以遥控快门。
[搭] extend a selfie stick 伸长自拍杆, selfie stick mount 手机夹, Bluetooth selfie stick 蓝牙自拍杆, group photo 合影
[联] camera 相机, smartphone 智能手机, photo 照片, selfie 自拍, stick 杆, Bluetooth 蓝牙, tripod 三脚架
[记] 可数：a selfie stick。旅游场景极常见。部分景区曾限制使用

**instant camera**
英/美 /ˈɪnstənt ˌkæmərə/
n. 拍立得相机
[义] A camera that prints a physical photo immediately after you take a picture. 拍照后立即打印出实体照片的相机（Polaroid camera 宝丽来相机, instant photo 即时照片, instant film 即影即有相纸）。可数。
[例] ① She brought an instant camera to the party for fun snapshots. 她带拍立得去派对拍趣味快照。 ② Instant cameras are popular again at weddings and festivals. 拍立得在婚礼和节庆场合又流行起来。
[搭] Polaroid camera 宝丽来相机, instant photo 即时照片, instant film 即影即有相纸, shake the photo 摇照片（显影）
[联] camera 相机, photo 照片, film 相纸, print 打印, snapshot 快照, memory 回忆, wedding 婚礼
[记] 可数：an instant camera。Polaroid 为知名品牌，常泛指此类相机。digital camera 输出数字文件，instant camera 输出实体片

**security camera**
英/美 /sɪˈkjuːrəti ˌkæmərə/
n. 监控摄像头
[义] A camera used to record video of a building, street, or room for safety and crime prevention. 用于录制建筑物、街道或房间视频以保障安全、防范犯罪的摄像头（CCTV camera 监控摄像头, install security cameras 安装监控, security camera footage 监控画面）。可数。
[例] ① Security cameras in the shop recorded the theft. 店里的监控拍下了盗窃过程。 ② The apartment building has security cameras at every entrance. 公寓楼每个入口都有监控摄像头。
[搭] CCTV camera 监控摄像头, install security cameras 安装监控, security camera footage 监控画面, motion detection 移动侦测
[联] camera 摄像头, doorbell camera 门铃摄像头, surveillance 监视, safety 安全, record 录制, monitor 监控, building 建筑
[记] 可数：a security camera。CCTV（闭路电视）为英式常见说法。与 doorbell camera 功能部分重叠

**smart plug**
英/美 /smɑːt plʌɡ/
n. 智能插座
[义] A plug that connects to Wi-Fi so you can turn connected devices on or off from an app or schedule. 连 Wi-Fi 的插头，可用应用或定时开关所连设备（Wi-Fi smart plug Wi-Fi 智能插座, schedule the plug 定时插座, turn off remotely 远程关闭）。可数。
[例] ① She uses a smart plug to turn off the lamp from bed. 她用智能插座在床上关灯。 ② The smart plug lets them schedule the coffee maker each morning. 智能插座让他们每天早上定时启动咖啡机。
[搭] Wi-Fi smart plug Wi-Fi 智能插座, schedule the plug 定时插座, turn off remotely 远程关闭, smart home 智能家居
[联] smart home 智能家居, plug 插头, socket 插座, Wi-Fi 无线网络, lamp 灯, appliance 电器, timer 定时器
[记] 可数：a smart plug。入门智能家居常见单品，把普通电器「智能化」

**baby monitor**
英/美 /ˈbeɪbi ˌmɒnɪtə(r)/
n. 婴儿监护器
[义] A device with a camera or microphone that lets parents hear or see their baby from another room. 带摄像头或麦克风的装置，让父母在其他房间能听见或看见婴儿（video baby monitor 视频婴儿监护器, baby monitor camera 婴儿监护摄像头, night vision 夜视功能）。可数。
[例] ① They checked the baby monitor before going to bed. 睡前他们看了婴儿监护器。 ② The baby monitor alerted them when the baby started crying. 婴儿一哭监护器就提醒他们。
[搭] video baby monitor 视频婴儿监护器, baby monitor camera 婴儿监护摄像头, night vision 夜视, audio monitor 音频监护器
[联] camera 摄像头, microphone 麦克风, baby 婴儿, parent 父母, bedroom 卧室, night 夜晚, safety 安全
[记] 可数：a baby monitor。video 版比纯音频版更常见。与 doorbell camera 同属家用监控类设备

**wireless charger**
英/美 /ˈwaɪərləs ˈtʃɑːdʒə(r)/
n. 无线充电器
[义] A charging pad or stand that powers a phone or earbuds through induction without plugging in a cable. 通过感应为手机或耳塞充电的垫或座，无需插线（Qi wireless charger Qi 无线充电器, wireless charging pad 无线充电板, place on the charger 放在充电器上）。可数。
[例] ① She dropped the phone on the wireless charger before sleep. 睡前她把手机放在无线充电器上。 ② The wireless charger works through a thin phone case. 无线充电器隔着薄手机壳也能充。
[搭] Qi wireless charger Qi 无线充电器, wireless charging pad 无线充电板, place on the charger 放在充电器上, fast wireless charging 无线快充
[联] charger 充电器, phone 手机, cable 数据线, battery 电池, power 电力, earbud 耳塞, pad 充电板
[记] 可数：a wireless charger。Qi 为常见行业标准。比有线充通常略慢，但更方便

**power strip**
英/美 /ˈpaʊər strɪp/
n. 插线板；排插
[义] A block of electrical sockets on a flexible cable, allowing several devices to share one wall outlet. 柔性电缆上的多孔插座块，让多台设备共用一个墙插（surge-protected power strip 防浪涌插线板, power strip with USB USB 排插, overload the power strip 插线板过载）。可数。
[例] ① Too many devices on one power strip can be dangerous. 一个插线板插太多设备可能有危险。 ② He plugged the laptop, monitor, and lamp into a power strip. 他把笔记本、显示器和台灯都插在插线板上。
[搭] surge-protected power strip 防浪涌插线板, power strip with USB USB 排插, overload the power strip 过载, extension cord 延长线
[联] plug 插头, socket 插座, cable 电缆, charger 充电器, outlet 墙插, electricity 电, surge protector 防浪涌器
[记] 可数：a power strip。英式常说 extension lead / multi-plug adapter。勿过载使用

**docking station**
英/美 /ˈdɒkɪŋ ˌsteɪʃn/
n. 扩展坞
[义] A device that connects a laptop to monitors, keyboards, and other peripherals through a single cable. 让笔记本通过一根线连接显示器、键盘及其他外设的设备（laptop docking station 笔记本扩展坞, USB-C docking station USB-C 扩展坞, connect to a dock 连接扩展坞）。可数。
[例] ① His docking station lets him use two monitors with his laptop. 扩展坞让他笔记本能接两个显示器。 ② She plugs the laptop into the docking station when she arrives at the office. 到办公室她把笔记本插上扩展坞。
[搭] laptop docking station 笔记本扩展坞, USB-C docking station USB-C 扩展坞, connect to a dock 连接扩展坞, dual monitors 双显示器
[联] laptop 笔记本, monitor 显示器, USB USB 接口, port 接口, keyboard 键盘, cable 数据线, hub 集线器
[记] 可数：a docking station。dock 口语简称。与 USB hub 不同：dock 通常供电并支持视频输出

**USB hub**
英/美 /ˌjuː es biː hʌb/
n. USB 扩展坞；USB 集线器
[义] A device that adds extra USB ports to a computer so you can connect more accessories at once. 为电脑增加额外 USB 口、以便同时连接更多配件的设备（powered USB hub 带电源 USB 集线器, USB hub port USB 扩展口, plug into a hub 插入集线器）。可数。
[例] ① The USB hub lets him connect a keyboard, mouse, and flash drive at once. USB 集线器让他能同时接键盘、鼠标和 U 盘。 ② A powered USB hub is needed for high-power devices. 大功率设备需要带电源的 USB 集线器。
[搭] powered USB hub 带电源集线器, USB hub port USB 扩展口, plug into a hub 插入集线器, multi-port hub 多口集线器
[联] USB USB 接口, port 接口, cable 数据线, laptop 笔记本, keyboard 键盘, mouse 鼠标, docking station 扩展坞
[记] 可数：a USB hub。powered hub 自带电源，可为设备供电。与 docking station 功能部分重叠

**mouse pad**
英/美 /maʊs pæd/
n. 鼠标垫
[义] A small flat pad that provides a smooth surface for moving a computer mouse. 为电脑鼠标提供顺滑移动表面的小平板（gaming mouse pad 游戏鼠标垫, ergonomic mouse pad 人体工学鼠标垫, clean the mouse pad 清洁鼠标垫）。可数。
[例] ① His gaming mouse pad covers half the desk. 他的游戏鼠标垫占了半张桌子。 ② A worn mouse pad can make the mouse feel less precise. 磨损的鼠标垫会让鼠标手感不准。
[搭] gaming mouse pad 游戏鼠标垫, ergonomic mouse pad 人体工学鼠标垫, clean the mouse pad 清洁鼠标垫, wrist rest 腕托
[联] mouse 鼠标, keyboard 键盘, desk 书桌, computer 电脑, gaming 游戏, surface 表面, click 点击
[记] 可数：a mouse pad。游戏用鼠标垫常更大。光学鼠标对垫面要求比老式滚球鼠标低

**tower**
英/美 /ˈtaʊə(r)/
n. 主机箱；塔式机箱
[义] The tall case that holds the main components of a desktop computer, standing upright on or under a desk. 立式放置、置于桌面或桌下、容纳台式机主要组件的高机箱（PC tower 电脑主机, desktop tower 台式主机箱, tower case 塔式机箱）。可数。
[例] ① The PC tower sits under the desk with cables running to the monitor. 电脑主机在桌下，线缆连到显示器。 ② He upgraded the graphics card inside the tower. 他在主机箱里升级了显卡。
[搭] PC tower 电脑主机, desktop tower 台式主机箱, tower case 塔式机箱, full-tower case 全塔机箱
[联] desktop 台式机, computer 电脑, motherboard 主板, graphics card 显卡, case 机箱, component 组件, cable 线缆
[记] 可数：a tower。tower vs. mini PC：tower 为传统大机箱。tower 亦指「塔」，语境区分

**power supply**
英/美 /ˈpaʊər səˌplaɪ/
n. 电源；电脑电源
[义] The component in a computer that converts electricity from the wall into power for the motherboard and other parts; a source of electrical power. 电脑中将墙电转换为主板及其他部件可用电力的组件；电力来源（PC power supply 电脑电源, replace the power supply 更换电源, power supply unit PSU 电源单元）。可数。
[例] ① The PC would not start until they replaced the power supply. 换了电源后电脑才能启动。 ② Choose a power supply with enough wattage for your graphics card. 选瓦数够带显卡的电源。
[搭] PC power supply 电脑电源, replace the power supply 更换电源, power supply unit 电源单元, wattage 瓦数
[联] motherboard 主板, graphics card 显卡, computer 电脑, electricity 电力, watt 瓦, component 组件, tower 主机箱
[记] 可数：a power supply。缩写 PSU（power supply unit）。DIY 装机须匹配瓦数与接口

**gamepad**
英/美 /ˈɡeɪmpæd/
n. 游戏手柄
[义] A handheld controller with buttons and joysticks used for video games on consoles or PCs. 带按键和摇杆的手持控制器，用于主机或 PC 游戏（wireless gamepad 无线手柄, connect a gamepad 连接手柄, gamepad buttons 手柄按键）。可数。
[例] ① He prefers a gamepad over keyboard and mouse for racing games. 赛车游戏他更喜欢手柄而非键鼠。 ② The wireless gamepad pairs with the console in seconds. 无线手柄几秒就能与主机配对。
[搭] wireless gamepad 无线手柄, connect a gamepad 连接手柄, gamepad buttons 手柄按键, compatible gamepad 兼容手柄
[联] controller 控制器, game console 游戏主机, joystick 摇杆, button 按钮, gaming 游戏, video game 电子游戏, wireless 无线的
[记] 可数：a gamepad。gamepad 与 controller 在主机语境常可互换。PC 游戏亦广泛支持手柄

**turntable**
英/美 /ˈtɜːnteɪbəl/
n. 唱片机；转盘
[义] A rotating platform on which a vinyl record is played; the part of a record player that spins the record. 播放黑胶唱片的旋转平台；唱片机上带动唱片旋转的部件（vinyl turntable 黑胶唱片机, DJ turntable DJ 转盘, put a record on the turntable 把唱片放上转盘）。可数。
[例] ① He bought a turntable to listen to his parents' old records. 他买了唱片机听父母的旧唱片。 ② The turntable needs a careful setup for the best sound. 唱片机需仔细调校才有最佳音质。
[搭] vinyl turntable 黑胶唱片机, DJ turntable DJ 转盘, record player 唱片机, put a record on the turntable 放上唱片
[联] record 唱片, vinyl 黑胶, music 音乐, speaker 扬声器, audio 音频, needle 唱针, stereo 立体声音响
[记] 可数：a turntable。vinyl revival 让 turntable 重新流行。turntable 亦指 DJ 打碟用转盘

**walkie-talkie**
英/美 /ˈwɔːki tɔːki/
n. 对讲机
[义] A portable radio that can send and receive voice messages over short distances, used by hand. 可在短距离内收发语音的便携无线电，手持使用（pair of walkie-talkies 一对对讲机, talk on walkie-talkies 用对讲机通话, walkie-talkie range 对讲距离）。可数。
[例] ① The security team uses walkie-talkies across the large venue. 安保队在大型场馆用对讲机联络。 ② Kids played with walkie-talkies in the backyard. 孩子们在后院用对讲机玩。
[搭] pair of walkie-talkies 一对对讲机, talk on walkie-talkies 用对讲机通话, walkie-talkie range 对讲距离, two-way radio 双向无线电（同义）
[联] radio 收音机, communicate 通讯, portable 便携的, signal 信号, voice 语音, security 安保, outdoor 户外
[记] 可数：a walkie-talkie。口语趣味叠词。two-way radio 更正式。户外、安保、施工场景常见

**digital scale**
英/美 /ˈdɪdʒɪtəl skeɪl/
n. 电子秤
[义] A scale with a digital display rather than a dial. 以数字显示而非指针表盘显示重量的秤（battery-powered scale 电池供电秤, weigh in grams 以克称重, digital readout 数字读数）。可数。总称；厨房用见 kitchen scale，连 app 的体重秤见 smart scale。
[例] ① Digital scales are more accurate than old mechanical bathroom scales. 电子秤比老式机械体重秤更准。 ② She replaced the batteries in the digital scale. 她给电子秤换了电池。
[搭] battery-powered scale 电池供电秤, digital readout 数字读数, weigh in grams 以克称重, tare function 去皮功能
[联] kitchen scale 厨房秤, smart scale 智能体重秤, weight 重量, measure 测量, gram 克, battery 电池, appliance 电器
[记] 可数：a digital scale。与 mechanical scale（机械秤）相对。具体用途由 kitchen scale、smart scale 等分述

**steam mop**
英/美 /stiːm mɒp/
n. 蒸汽拖把
[义] A mop that uses heated steam to clean and sanitize hard floors without harsh chemicals. 用加热蒸汽清洁并消毒硬地面、无需强效化学品的拖把（use a steam mop 用蒸汽拖把, steam mop pad 拖布垫, fill the water tank 加水）。可数。
[例] ① The steam mop cleaned the kitchen tiles without leaving residue. 蒸汽拖把清洁厨房地砖且不留残留。 ② She replaced the steam mop pad after mopping the whole house. 拖完全屋后她换了蒸汽拖把垫。
[搭] use a steam mop 用蒸汽拖把, steam mop pad 拖布垫, fill the water tank 加水, sanitize floors 消毒地面
[联] mop 拖把, clean 清洁, floor 地板, steam 蒸汽, kitchen 厨房, appliance 电器, vacuum cleaner 吸尘器
[记] 可数：a steam mop。适合瓷砖、木地板等。与 robot vacuum 常搭配作为家用清洁组合

**Blu-ray player**
英/美 /bluː reɪ ˈpleɪə(r)/
n. 蓝光播放器
[义] A device that plays Blu-ray discs, DVDs, and often streams content to a TV. 播放蓝光碟、DVD，并常可向电视串流内容的设备（Blu-ray disc player 蓝光碟播放机, connect to a TV 连接电视, insert a disc 放入光盘）。可数。
[例] ① They still use a Blu-ray player for their film collection. 他们仍用蓝光播放器看收藏的电影碟。 ② The Blu-ray player also streams apps when connected to Wi-Fi. 连上 Wi-Fi 后这款蓝光机也能串流应用。
[搭] Blu-ray disc player 蓝光碟播放机, connect to a TV 连接电视, insert a disc 放入光盘, HDMI output HDMI 输出
[联] TV 电视, DVD DVD 光碟, disc 光盘, movie 电影, HDMI HDMI 线, remote 遥控器, streaming 串流
[记] 可数：a Blu-ray player。Blu-ray 为索尼主导的碟片格式。流媒体普及后销量下降，但影音收藏者仍用

**CD player**
英/美 /ˌsiː ˈdiː ˈpleɪə(r)/
n. CD 播放器
[义] A device that reads and plays audio compact discs. 读取并播放音乐 CD 的设备（portable CD player 便携 CD 机, car CD player 车载 CD 机, insert a CD 放入 CD）。可数。
[例] ① She found her old portable CD player in a drawer. 她在抽屉里找到了旧便携 CD 机。 ② The car still has a CD player in the dashboard. 车里仪表盘还装着 CD 播放器。
[搭] portable CD player 便携 CD 机, car CD player 车载 CD 机, insert a CD 放入 CD, CD disc 音乐 CD
