[联] music 音乐, disc 光盘, album 专辑, speaker 扬声器, radio 收音机, MP3 player MP3 播放器, audio 音频
[记] 可数：a CD player。CD 指 compact disc。已被流媒体大量替代，但车载与怀旧场景仍可见

**stereo**
英/美 /ˈsteriəʊ/
n. 立体声音响；音响系统
[义] A sound system with two or more speakers that creates a sense of direction and depth in music; a radio or player with such speakers. 用两个或多个扬声器呈现音乐方向感与层次感的音响系统；带此类扬声器的收音机或播放器（home stereo 家用音响, stereo system 立体声系统, stereo speakers 立体声扬声器）。可数。
[例] ① He listened to the album on his vintage home stereo. 他用老式家用音响听这张专辑。 ② The stereo sounded much better than the TV speakers. 音响效果比电视扬声器好得多。
[搭] home stereo 家用音响, stereo system 立体声系统, stereo speakers 立体声扬声器, turn up the stereo 把音响开大
[联] speaker 扬声器, music 音乐, sound 声音, amplifier 功放, turntable 唱片机, audio 音频, subwoofer 低音炮
[记] 可数：a stereo。stereo vs. mono：stereo 为立体声。形容词 stereo 亦指「立体的（声）」

**subwoofer**
英/美 /ˈsʌbwʊfə(r)/
n. 低音炮；超低音扬声器
[义] A loudspeaker designed to reproduce very low bass sounds that regular speakers handle poorly. 专门再现普通扬声器难以处理的极低频 bass 的扬声器（powered subwoofer 有源低音炮, connect a subwoofer 连接低音炮, subwoofer bass 低音）。可数。
[例] ① The subwoofer rattled the windows during the action scene. 动作场面里低音炮震得窗户发响。 ② He added a subwoofer to improve movie sound at home. 他为改善在家看电影的音效加了一个低音炮。
[搭] powered subwoofer 有源低音炮, connect a subwoofer 连接低音炮, subwoofer cable 低音炮线, deep bass 深沉低音
[联] speaker 扬声器, soundbar 回音壁, stereo 音响, bass 低音, audio 音频, movie 电影, amplifier 功放
[记] 可数：a subwoofer。home theater 常见配置：speakers + subwoofer。woofer 指低音单元

**portable speaker**
英/美 /ˈpɔːtəbl ˈspiːkə(r)/
n. 便携音箱
[义] A small, battery-powered speaker that connects to a phone or other device via Bluetooth or cable for music on the go. 小型电池供电音箱，通过蓝牙或线缆连手机等设备，便于携带外放（Bluetooth portable speaker 蓝牙便携音箱, waterproof speaker 防水音箱, pair a speaker 配对音箱）。可数。
[例] ① They brought a portable speaker to the picnic for music. 他们野餐带了便携音箱放音乐。 ② The portable speaker lasts about ten hours on one charge. 这款便携音箱充一次电大约用十小时。
[搭] Bluetooth portable speaker 蓝牙便携音箱, waterproof speaker 防水音箱, pair a speaker 配对音箱, rechargeable speaker 充电音箱
[联] speaker 扬声器, Bluetooth 蓝牙, music 音乐, battery 电池, smartphone 智能手机, outdoor 户外, smart speaker 智能音箱
[记] 可数：a portable speaker。与 smart speaker 不同：portable 强调携带外放，smart 强调语音助手与智能家居

**electric heater**
英/美 /ɪˈlektrɪk ˈhiːtə(r)/
n. 电暖器
[义] A portable device that uses electricity to heat a room or small space. 用电为房间或小空间供暖的便携设备（portable electric heater 便携式电暖器, space heater 小型取暖器, turn on the heater 开电暖器）。可数。
[例] ① She plugged in an electric heater under her desk in winter. 冬天她在书桌下插了电暖器。 ② Keep flammable items away from the electric heater. 易燃物要远离电暖器。
[搭] portable electric heater 便携式电暖器, space heater 小型取暖器, turn on the heater 开电暖器, heating element 加热元件
[联] heat 加热, winter 冬天, cold 寒冷, appliance 电器, fan 风扇, air conditioner 空调, warm 温暖的
[记] 可数：an electric heater。space heater 为美式常见说法。使用须注意通风与防火

**freezer**
英/美 /ˈfriːzə(r)/
n. 冷冻柜；冷冻室
[义] A compartment or separate appliance that keeps food frozen below 0°C. 使食物在零度以下冷冻保存的隔层或独立电器（chest freezer 卧式冷冻柜, freezer compartment 冷冻室, put in the freezer 放进冷冻柜）。可数。
[例] ① She stored extra meals in the freezer for busy weeks. 她把多做的餐食放进冷冻柜，忙的时候用。 ② The freezer door was left open and food started to thaw. 冷冻室门没关好，食物开始化了。
[搭] chest freezer 卧式冷冻柜, freezer compartment 冷冻室, put in the freezer 放进冷冻柜, defrost the freezer 给冷冻柜除霜
[联] refrigerator 冰箱, frozen 冷冻的, food 食物, ice 冰, cold 冷的, appliance 电器, thaw 解冻
[记] 可数：a freezer。fridge-freezer 指「冰箱冷冻一体柜」。upright freezer 为立式冷冻柜

**water dispenser**
英/美 /ˈwɔːtə dɪˈspensə(r)/
n. 饮水机
[义] A machine that stores and dispenses drinking water, either from bottles or by filtering tap water. 储存并供应饮用水的机器，水源可为桶装水或过滤自来水（office water dispenser 办公室饮水机, hot and cold water dispenser 冷热饮水机, refill the bottle 换水桶）。可数。
[例] ① Employees gathered at the water dispenser during breaks. 员工休息时聚在饮水机旁。 ② The water dispenser offers both hot and cold water. 这台饮水机有热水也有冷水。
[搭] office water dispenser 办公室饮水机, hot and cold water dispenser 冷热饮水机, refill the bottle 换水桶, filtered water 过滤水
[联] water 水, drink 饮用, office 办公室, bottle 水桶, filter 过滤, hot 热的, cold 冷的
[记] 可数：a water dispenser。桶装水与直饮管线为两种常见类型

**ice maker**
英/美 /aɪs ˈmeɪkə(r)/
n. 制冰机
[义] A device in a refrigerator or as a standalone machine that automatically produces ice cubes. 冰箱内置或独立的、自动制冰块的装置（built-in ice maker 内置制冰机, countertop ice maker 台面制冰机, make ice cubes 制冰块）。可数。
[例] ① The fridge's ice maker stopped working during the heat wave. 热浪期间冰箱制冰机坏了。 ② They bought a countertop ice maker for parties. 他们为聚会买了台面制冰机。
[搭] built-in ice maker 内置制冰机, countertop ice maker 台面制冰机, make ice cubes 制冰块, empty the ice bin 清空储冰盒
[联] refrigerator 冰箱, ice 冰, freezer 冷冻, drink 饮料, party 聚会, appliance 电器, cube 冰块
[记] 可数：an ice maker。美式冰箱常见内置制冰。cocktail / party 场景 countertop 制冰机也常见

**stand mixer**
英/美 /stænd ˈmɪksə(r)/
n. 立式搅拌机；厨师机
[义] A heavy electric mixer with a stand and bowl, used for baking and mixing dough or batter. 带支架和搅拌碗的重型电动搅拌机，用于烘焙、和面或搅拌面糊（kitchen stand mixer 厨房厨师机, mix dough 和面, mixer attachment 搅拌配件）。可数。
[例] ① She uses a stand mixer to knead bread dough quickly. 她用厨师机快速揉面包面团。 ② The stand mixer comes with whisk and dough hook attachments. 厨师机配有打蛋器和和面钩配件。
[搭] kitchen stand mixer 厨房厨师机, mix dough 和面, mixer attachment 搅拌配件, mixing bowl 搅拌碗
[联] blender 搅拌机, food processor 料理机, kitchen 厨房, bake 烘焙, dough 面团, appliance 电器, mix 混合
[记] 可数：a stand mixer。比 handheld mixer（手持搅拌器）功率大、更稳。烘焙爱好者高频设备

**bread maker**
英/美 /bred ˈmeɪkə(r)/
n. 面包机
[义] An electric machine that mixes, kneads, and bakes bread automatically in one container. 在单一容器内自动混合、揉面并烘烤面包的电器（automatic bread maker 自动面包机, bake a loaf 烤一条面包, bread maker pan 面包机内胆）。可数。
[例] ① She set the bread maker before bed and woke up to fresh bread. 睡前她设好面包机，醒来就有新鲜面包。 ② The bread maker saves time on weekday mornings. 面包机在工作日早晨省时间。

[联] oven 烤箱, kitchen 厨房, bake 烘焙, bread 面包, appliance 电器, dough 面团, loaf 一条面包
[记] 可数：a bread maker。亦说 bread machine。与 oven 手工烘焙相比更省心

**3D printer**
英/美 /ˌθriː diː ˈprɪntə(r)/
n. 3D 打印机
[义] A machine that builds solid objects layer by layer from a digital design, using plastic or other materials. 按数字设计逐层堆叠塑料或其他材料以构建实体物体的机器（home 3D printer 家用 3D 打印机, print a model 打印模型, 3D printing filament 打印耗材）。可数。
[例] ① He printed a phone stand on his 3D printer at home. 他在家用 3D 打印机打了一个手机支架。 ② Schools are buying 3D printers for design classes. 学校为设计课采购 3D 打印机。

[联] printer 打印机, plastic 塑料, model 模型, design 设计, machine 机器, gadget 小装置, prototype 原型
[记] 可数：a 3D printer。消费级与爱好者市场已较普及。filament 指线轴耗材

**CPU**
英/美 /ˌsiː piː ˈjuː/
n. 中央处理器
[义] Central Processing Unit; the main chip in a computer that executes instructions and runs programs. 中央处理器；电脑中执行指令、运行程序的主芯片（install a CPU 安装 CPU, CPU speed CPU 速度, upgrade the CPU 升级 CPU）。可数。
[例] ① The laptop's CPU struggled with heavy video editing. 这款笔记本的 CPU 做重度视频剪辑很吃力。 ② He compared CPU models before building his PC. 组装电脑前他比较了各款 CPU。
[搭] install a CPU 安装 CPU, CPU speed CPU 速度, upgrade the CPU 升级 CPU, CPU cooler 散热器
[联] processor 处理器, motherboard 主板, computer 电脑, graphics card 显卡, memory 内存, chip 芯片, performance 性能
[记] 可数：a CPU。processor 为全称通俗说法，CPU 为缩写。Intel / AMD 为常见品牌

**CPU cooler**
英/美 /ˌsiː piː ˈjuː ˈkuːlə(r)/
n. CPU 散热器
[义] A fan or heat sink assembly that keeps the CPU from overheating during use. 在使用中防止 CPU 过热的风扇或散热片组件（install a CPU cooler 安装散热器, air cooler 风冷散热器, liquid cooler 水冷散热器）。可数。
[例] ① The PC was loud because the CPU cooler was working hard. CPU 散热器满负荷转，电脑很响。 ② He replaced the stock CPU cooler with a quieter model. 他把原装散热器换成了更安静的型号。
[搭] install a CPU cooler 安装散热器, air cooler 风冷, liquid cooler 水冷, heat sink 散热片, cooling fan 散热风扇
[联] CPU 中央处理器, motherboard 主板, fan 风扇, heat 热量, computer 电脑, tower 主机箱, overheat 过热
[记] 可数：a CPU cooler。盒装 CPU 附带原装散热器。游戏或超频装机常换更强散热

英/美 /ˈɡræfɪks ˌtæblət/
n. 数位板；绘图板
[义] A flat input device used with a stylus to draw or edit images on a computer, common among designers and artists. 配合触控笔在电脑上绘画或编辑图像的平板输入设备，设计师与画师常用（drawing tablet 绘图板, digital pen 数位笔, pressure sensitivity 压感）。可数。
[例] ① She uses a graphics tablet for digital illustration work. 她做数字插画用数位板。 ② The graphics tablet connects to the laptop via USB. 数位板通过 USB 连笔记本。

[记] 可数：a graphics tablet。与 tablet（平板电脑）不同：graphics tablet 须连电脑作输入设备。Wacom 为常见品牌

英/美 /kɑːd ˈriːdə(r)/

[例] ① He used a card reader to copy photos from the camera's SD card. 他用读卡器把照片从相机 SD 卡拷出来。 ② The laptop has a built-in SD card reader on the side. 笔记本侧面有内置 SD 读卡器。

**racing wheel**
英/美 /ˈreɪsɪŋ wiːl/

[义] A steering-wheel controller with pedals used for racing video games on consoles or PCs. 带踏板的方向盘控制器，用于主机或 PC 赛车游戏（gaming racing wheel 游戏赛车方向盘, attach pedals 连接踏板, force feedback 力反馈）。可数。
[例] ① The racing wheel made driving games feel much more realistic. 赛车方向盘让驾驶游戏真实得多。 ② He clamped the racing wheel to the desk for stability. 他把赛车方向盘夹固定在桌上以求稳定。

英/美 /məˈsɑːʒ ɡʌn/
n. 筋膜枪
[义] A handheld electric device that uses rapid pulses to massage muscles and relieve soreness after exercise. 手持电动设备，以快速脉冲按摩肌肉、缓解运动后酸痛（percussion massage gun 冲击式筋膜枪, massage gun attachment 按摩头, recharge the massage gun 给筋膜枪充电）。可数。
[例] ① He used a massage gun on his legs after the marathon. 马拉松跑完后他用筋膜枪按摩腿部。 ② The massage gun has several speed settings for different muscles. 筋膜枪有多档速度，适合不同肌肉。

[联] massage 按摩, muscle 肌肉, fitness 健身, exercise 锻炼, battery 电池, recovery 恢复, gadget 小装置

**cordless phone**

[例] ① She answered the cordless phone from the kitchen. 她在厨房接了无绳电话。 ② The cordless phone handset needs charging every few days. 无绳电话听筒每隔几天要充电。

[记] 可数：a cordless phone。与 mobile phone（手机）不同：仍依赖家用固定电话线。底座须插电话线

**smart smoke detector**
英/美 /smɑːt sməʊk dɪˈtektə(r)/
n. 智能烟雾报警器
[义] A smoke alarm that connects to Wi-Fi or a smart home system and sends alerts to your phone when smoke is detected. 连 Wi-Fi 或智能家居系统、检测到烟雾时向手机发警报的烟雾报警器（install a smart smoke detector 安装智能烟雾报警器, smoke alert 烟雾警报, test the alarm 测试报警器）。可数。
[例] ① The smart smoke detector sent an alert while they were on vacation. 度假时智能烟雾报警器发来了警报。 ② Test the smart smoke detector every month to make sure it works. 每月测试智能烟雾报警器确保正常。
[搭] install a smart smoke detector 安装智能烟雾报警器, smoke alert 烟雾警报, test the alarm 测试报警器, battery backup 电池备用
[联] smart home 智能家居, smoke 烟雾, safety 安全, alarm 报警器, sensor 传感器, fire 火灾, notification 通知
[记] 可数：a smart smoke detector。亦含 carbon monoxide detection 的 combo 型号。传统 smoke alarm 无联网功能

**motion sensor**

n. 人体感应器；运动传感器
[义] A device that detects movement and can trigger lights, alarms, or cameras. 检测移动并可触发灯光、警报或摄像头的装置（motion sensor light 感应灯, install a motion sensor 安装人体感应器, motion detection 移动侦测）。可数。
[例] ① The hallway light turns on when the motion sensor detects movement. 人体感应器感应到移动，走廊灯就亮。 ② The security system uses motion sensors near the windows. 安防系统在窗户附近装了运动传感器。
[搭] motion sensor light 感应灯, install a motion sensor 安装人体感应器, motion detection 移动侦测, trigger an alarm 触发警报

[义] An automatic machine that moves around a lawn cutting grass without a person pushing it. 在草坪上自动移动、无需人推动即可割草的机器（automatic lawn mower 自动割草机, set up boundary wire 设置边界线, robot mower charging station 充电站）。可数。

[例] ① She set up a ring light before recording her tutorial video. 录教程视频前她架好了环形灯。 ② The ring light reduced shadows on her face during the call. 通话时环形灯减少了面部阴影。
[搭] LED ring light LED 环形灯, ring light for streaming 直播环形灯, adjust brightness 调节亮度, phone mount 手机支架
[联] camera 相机, video 视频, light 灯, photo 照片, streaming 直播, webcam 网络摄像头, brightness 亮度
[记] 可数：a ring light。vlogging / 直播 / 自拍场景极常见。LED 为常见光源

**paper shredder**
英/美 /ˈpeɪpə ˌʃredə(r)/
n. 碎纸机
[义] A machine that cuts documents into small strips or pieces so the information cannot be read. 把文件切成细条或碎屑、使信息无法辨认的机器（office paper shredder 办公碎纸机, shred documents 碎文件, empty the shredder bin 清空碎纸桶）。可数。
[例] ① Shred old bank statements with a paper shredder before recycling. 回收前用碎纸机处理旧银行对账单。 ② The paper shredder jammed because too many sheets were fed at once. 一次塞太多张，碎纸机卡纸了。
[搭] office paper shredder 办公碎纸机, shred documents 碎文件, empty the shredder bin 清空碎纸桶, cross-cut shredder 交叉切割碎纸机
[联] document 文件, office 办公室, privacy 隐私, security 安全, recycle 回收, printer 打印机, confidential 机密的
[记] 可数：a paper shredder。cross-cut 比 strip-cut 更安全。处理含个人信息纸张的办公标配

**ink cartridge**
英/美 /ɪŋk ˈkærɪtrɪdʒ/
n. 墨盒
[义] A replaceable container of ink used in inkjet printers. 喷墨打印机中可更换的墨水容器（replace the ink cartridge 更换墨盒, ink cartridge ran out 墨盒用完, compatible cartridge 兼容墨盒）。可数。
[例] ① The printer stopped because the ink cartridge was empty. 墨盒空了，打印机停了。 ② Compatible ink cartridges cost less than the brand-name ones. 兼容墨盒比原装便宜。
[搭] replace the ink cartridge 更换墨盒, ink cartridge ran out 墨盒用完, compatible cartridge 兼容墨盒, inkjet printer 喷墨打印机
[联] printer 打印机, ink 墨水, toner cartridge 硒鼓, print 打印, paper 纸, replace 更换, document 文件
[记] 可数：an ink cartridge。inkjet 用 ink cartridge；laser printer 用 toner cartridge（硒鼓）

**toner cartridge**
英/美 /ˈtəʊnə ˌkærɪtrɪdʒ/
n. 硒鼓；碳粉盒
[义] A replaceable container of toner powder used in laser printers and copiers. 激光打印机与复印机中可更换的碳粉容器（replace the toner cartridge 更换硒鼓, toner cartridge low 硒鼓余量低, laser printer toner 激光打印机碳粉）。可数。
[例] ① The office printer displayed a message that the toner cartridge was low. 办公室打印机提示硒鼓余量低。 ② He replaced the toner cartridge and the print quality improved. 换了硒鼓后打印质量好了。
[搭] replace the toner cartridge 更换硒鼓, toner cartridge low 硒鼓余量低, laser printer 激光打印机, toner powder 碳粉
[联] printer 打印机, photocopier 复印机, ink cartridge 墨盒, print 打印, office 办公室, replace 更换, document 文件
[记] 可数：a toner cartridge。toner 为粉末非液体 ink。激光打印办公室更常见

**car stereo**
英/美 /kɑː ˈsteriəʊ/
n. 车载音响
[义] The radio and audio system built into or installed in a car for music and broadcasts. 内置或加装于车内、用于播放音乐和广播的收音机与音响系统（upgrade the car stereo 升级车载音响, car stereo system 车载音响系统, connect via Bluetooth 蓝牙连接）。可数。
[例] ① He replaced the old car stereo with one that supports Bluetooth. 他把旧车载音响换成了支持蓝牙的。 ② She listened to podcasts through the car stereo on her commute. 通勤时她通过车载音响听播客。
[搭] upgrade the car stereo 升级车载音响, car stereo system 车载音响系统, connect via Bluetooth 蓝牙连接, car speakers 车载扬声器
[联] car 汽车, radio 收音机, music 音乐, Bluetooth 蓝牙, speaker 扬声器, drive 驾驶, audio 音频
[记] 可数：a car stereo。aftermarket 指「后装」升级件。modern car stereo 常含 touchscreen 与 CarPlay

**backup camera**
英/美 /ˈbækʌp ˌkæmərə/
n. 倒车影像；后视摄像头
[义] A camera mounted on the rear of a vehicle that shows the area behind the car on a screen when reversing. 安装在车辆后部、倒车时在屏幕上显示车后方区域的摄像头（rear backup camera 后置倒车影像, backup camera display 倒车影像显示, reverse safely 安全倒车）。可数。
[例] ① The backup camera helped her park in a tight space. 倒车影像帮她在窄位停车。 ② New cars often come with a backup camera as standard equipment. 新车常把倒车影像列为标配。
[搭] rear backup camera 后置倒车影像, backup camera display 倒车影像显示, reverse safely 安全倒车, parking assist 泊车辅助
[联] dash cam 行车记录仪, camera 摄像头, car 汽车, drive 驾驶, screen 屏幕, parking 停车, safety 安全
[记] 可数：a backup camera。亦说 reversing camera（英式）。与 dash cam（记录前方）功能不同

**MP3 player**
英/美 /ˌem piː ˈθriː ˌpleɪə(r)/
n. MP3 播放器
[义] A portable device that stores and plays digital music files, especially in MP3 format. 存储并播放数字音乐文件、尤指 MP3 格式的便携设备（portable MP3 player 便携 MP3 播放器, download songs 下载歌曲, MP3 file MP3 文件）。可数。
[例] ① Before smartphones, she listened to music on an MP3 player. 智能手机普及前，她用 MP3 播放器听歌。 ② He still keeps an old MP3 player for running without a phone. 跑步时他仍用旧 MP3 播放器，不必带手机。
[搭] portable MP3 player 便携 MP3 播放器, download songs 下载歌曲, MP3 file MP3 文件, digital music 数字音乐
[联] music 音乐, smartphone 智能手机, headphone 耳机, audio 音频, portable 便携的, song 歌曲, player 播放器
[记] 可数：an MP3 player。iPod 为经典品牌。已被 smartphone streaming 大量取代，但词仍常见

**satellite dish**
英/美 /ˈsætəlaɪt dɪʃ/
n. 卫星天线；卫星锅
[义] A dish-shaped antenna that receives television or internet signals from satellites. 接收卫星电视或互联网信号的碟形天线（install a satellite dish 安装卫星天线, satellite TV 卫星电视, align the dish 对准卫星）。可数。
[例] ① The satellite dish on the roof picks up channels from several providers. 屋顶卫星锅接收多家运营商的频道。 ② Heavy snow can block the satellite dish signal. 大雪可能挡住卫星天线信号。
[搭] install a satellite dish 安装卫星天线, satellite TV 卫星电视, align the dish 对准卫星, dish antenna 碟形天线
[联] TV 电视, antenna 天线, signal 信号, broadcast 广播, cable 有线电视, roof 屋顶, channel 频道
[记] 可数：a satellite dish。与 cable TV 为不同接收方式。农村与偏远地区更常见

**mini fridge**
英/美 /ˈmɪni frɪdʒ/
n. 迷你冰箱
[义] A small refrigerator used in dorm rooms, offices, or hotel rooms for drinks and snacks. 用于宿舍、办公室或酒店房间、存放饮料与零食的小型冰箱（dorm mini fridge 宿舍小冰箱, mini fridge with freezer 带冷冻室迷你冰箱, stock the mini fridge 填满小冰箱）。可数。
[例] ① He kept drinks in a mini fridge under his desk. 他在书桌下小冰箱里放饮料。 ② The hotel room had a mini fridge but no kettle. 酒店房间有小冰箱但没有水壶。
[搭] dorm mini fridge 宿舍小冰箱, mini fridge with freezer 带冷冻迷你冰箱, stock the mini fridge 填满小冰箱, compact fridge 紧凑冰箱
[联] refrigerator 冰箱, fridge 冰箱, drink 饮料, dorm 宿舍, office 办公室, hotel 酒店, cold 冷的
[记] 可数：a mini fridge。亦说 compact refrigerator。容量小，不适合大量囤粮

**electric massager**
英/美 /ɪˈlektrɪk məˈsɑːʒə(r)/
n. 电动按摩器
[义] An electric device that vibrates or kneads to relieve muscle tension; a general term for handheld pads and wraps. 通过振动或揉捏缓解肌肉紧张的电器；手持靠垫、围脖等产品的总称（heat and vibration modes 加热与振动模式, massage setting 按摩档位）。可数。
[例] ① She used an electric massager on her shoulders after desk work. 久坐办公后她用电动按摩器按肩膀。 ② The electric massager has several intensity levels. 这款电动按摩器有多档力度。
[搭] heat and vibration modes 加热与振动模式, massage setting 按摩档位, shoulder massager 肩部按摩, rechargeable massager 充电式按摩器
[联] neck massager 颈部按摩器, foot massager 足部按摩器, massage gun 筋膜枪, massage chair 按摩椅, muscle 肌肉, relax 放松, appliance 电器
[记] 可数：an electric massager。具体形态见 neck massager、foot massager、massage gun 等条

**surge protector**
英/美 /sɜːdʒ prəˈtektə(r)/
n. 防浪涌插排；电涌保护器
[义] A power strip or adapter that protects connected electronics from voltage spikes caused by lightning or grid fluctuations. 保护所连电子设备免受雷击或电网波动导致电压尖峰损害的插排或适配器（plug into a surge protector 插入防浪涌插排, protect electronics 保护电子设备, surge protection 浪涌保护）。可数。
[例] ① He plugged the computer and monitor into a surge protector. 他把电脑和显示器插在防浪涌插排上。 ② A good surge protector can save equipment during a storm. 好的防浪涌器暴风雨期间能保护设备。
[搭] plug into a surge protector 插入防浪涌插排, protect electronics 保护电子设备, surge protection 浪涌保护, power strip 插线板
[联] power strip 插线板, plug 插头, electricity 电, computer 电脑, protect 保护, lightning 雷击, outlet 插座
[记] 可数：a surge protector。与普通 power strip 不同：surge protector 含保护电路。贵重电子设备建议使用

**Wi-Fi extender**
英/美 /ˈwaɪ faɪ ɪkˈstendə(r)/
n. Wi-Fi 扩展器
[义] A device that receives an existing Wi-Fi signal and rebroadcasts it to cover dead zones in a home or office. 接收现有 Wi-Fi 信号并重新广播、以覆盖家中或办公室信号死角区域的设备（plug in a Wi-Fi extender 插入 Wi-Fi 扩展器, extend Wi-Fi coverage 扩展 Wi-Fi 覆盖, Wi-Fi dead zone 信号死角）。可数。
[例] ① They added a Wi-Fi extender upstairs because the signal was weak. 楼上信号弱，他们加了一个 Wi-Fi 扩展器。 ② The Wi-Fi extender uses the same network name as the main router. 扩展器与主路由器使用同一网络名称。
[搭] plug in a Wi-Fi extender 插入 Wi-Fi 扩展器, extend Wi-Fi coverage 扩展覆盖, Wi-Fi dead zone 信号死角, mesh network 网状网络
[联] router 路由器, modem 调制解调器, Wi-Fi 无线网络, signal 信号, internet 互联网, network 网络, connection 连接
[记] 可数：a Wi-Fi extender。亦说 range extender / repeater。mesh system 为更现代的全屋覆盖方案

**external hard drive**
英/美 /ɪkˈstɜːnl hɑːd draɪv/
n. 移动硬盘
[义] A portable hard drive in its own case that connects to a computer via USB or similar port for backup and extra storage. 自带外壳、通过 USB 等接口连接电脑、用于备份与额外存储的便携硬盘（portable external hard drive 便携移动硬盘, back up to an external drive 备份到移动硬盘, external drive capacity 容量）。可数。
[例] ① She keeps her photo archive on an external hard drive. 她把照片档案存在移动硬盘上。 ② The external hard drive was not recognized until he tried another cable. 换了一根线后移动硬盘才被识别。
[搭] portable external hard drive 便携移动硬盘, back up to an external drive 备份到移动硬盘, external drive capacity 容量, plug in the drive 插入移动硬盘
[联] hard drive 硬盘, SSD 固态硬盘, storage 存储, backup 备份, USB USB 接口, computer 电脑, file 文件
[记] 可数：an external hard drive。HDD 与 SSD 外置版均有。与 flash drive（U 盘）相比容量通常更大

**gaming headset**
英/美 /ˈɡeɪmɪŋ ˈhedset/
n. 游戏耳机
[义] Headphones with a built-in microphone designed for video games, often with surround sound and a comfortable fit for long sessions. 自带麦克风、为电子游戏设计的长戴舒适耳机，常带环绕声（wireless gaming headset 无线游戏耳机, gaming headset mic 游戏耳机麦克风, surround sound 环绕声）。可数。
[例] ① He wears a gaming headset to communicate with teammates online. 他戴游戏耳机与队友在线沟通。 ② The gaming headset blocks outside noise during competitive matches. 比赛时游戏耳机挡住外界噪音。
[搭] wireless gaming headset 无线游戏耳机, gaming headset mic 麦克风, surround sound 环绕声, noise isolation 隔音
[联] headphone 耳机, microphone 麦克风, gamepad 游戏手柄, gaming 游戏, voice chat 语音聊天, computer 电脑, audio 音频
[记] 可数：a gaming headset。比普通 headphone 更强调 mic 与长时间佩戴。头戴包耳式最常见

**mechanical keyboard**
英/美 /məˈkænɪkl ˈkiːbɔːd/
n. 机械键盘
[义] A keyboard with individual mechanical switches under each key, valued for tactile feedback and durability. 每个键下有独立机械轴体的键盘，以触感和耐用性著称（wireless mechanical keyboard 无线机械键盘, mechanical switches 机械轴, keyboard layout 键盘布局）。可数。
[例] ① Typing on a mechanical keyboard feels louder but more responsive. 机械键盘打字更响，但反馈更清晰。 ② He swapped keycaps on his mechanical keyboard for a custom look. 他给机械键盘换了键帽做个性化外观。
[搭] wireless mechanical keyboard 无线机械键盘, mechanical switches 机械轴, keyboard layout 键盘布局, replace keycaps 更换键帽
[联] keyboard 键盘, computer 电脑, typing 打字, mouse 鼠标, gaming 游戏, switch 轴体, laptop 笔记本
[记] 可数：a mechanical keyboard。与 membrane keyboard（薄膜键盘）相对。游戏与编程用户中极流行

**amplifier**
英/美 /ˈæmplɪfaɪə(r)/
n. 功放；放大器
[义] A device that increases the strength of an audio signal so it can drive speakers at higher volume. 增强音频信号强度、使扬声器能以更大音量发声的设备（audio amplifier 音频功放, guitar amplifier 吉他音箱, power amplifier 功率放大器）。可数。
[例] ① The turntable needs an amplifier before connecting to speakers. 唱片机连扬声器前需要功放。 ② He turned down the amplifier because the neighbors complained. 邻居投诉后他把功放音量调低了。
[搭] audio amplifier 音频功放, guitar amplifier 吉他音箱, power amplifier 功率放大器, connect to speakers 连接扬声器
[联] speaker 扬声器, stereo 音响, subwoofer 低音炮, audio 音频, music 音乐, volume 音量, turntable 唱片机
[记] 可数：an amplifier。口语 amp 指吉他音箱或功放。home audio 与 live music 语境均常见

**DVD player**
英/美 /ˌdiː viː ˈdiː ˈpleɪə(r)/
n. DVD 播放器
[义] A device that plays DVD discs and usually connects to a TV. 播放 DVD 光盘并通常连接电视的设备（portable DVD player 便携 DVD 机, insert a DVD 放入 DVD, DVD player remote DVD 机遥控器）。可数。
[例] ① The hotel room still had a DVD player and a small disc library. 酒店房间还有 DVD 机和一小套碟片。 ② Their old DVD player can no longer read scratched discs. 旧 DVD 机读不了有划痕的碟了。
[搭] portable DVD player 便携 DVD 机, insert a DVD 放入 DVD, connect to a TV 连接电视, DVD disc DVD 光盘
[联] Blu-ray player 蓝光播放器, disc 光盘, movie 电影, TV 电视, remote 遥控器, CD player CD 播放器, video 视频
[记] 可数：a DVD player。已被流媒体大量取代，但车载、酒店、收藏场景仍可见

**electric blanket**
英/美 /ɪˈlektrɪk ˈblæŋkɪt/
n. 电热毯
[义] A blanket with built-in electric wires that produce heat for warmth in bed or on a sofa. 内置电热丝、用于床上或沙发取暖的毯子（heated electric blanket 电热毯, adjust the heat setting 调节温度档位, safety timer 安全定时）。可数。
[例] ① She turned on the electric blanket before getting into bed on cold nights. 寒夜睡前她先开电热毯。 ② The electric blanket has an automatic shut-off for safety. 电热毯有自动断电保护。
[搭] heated electric blanket 电热毯, adjust the heat setting 调节温度, safety timer 安全定时, low heat setting 低温档
[联] electric heater 电暖器, bed 床, warm 温暖的, winter 冬天, heat 加热, sleep 睡眠, appliance 电器
[记] 可数：an electric blanket。使用时勿折叠过厚以免过热。heated mattress pad 为电热床垫垫

**induction cooker**
英/美 /ɪnˈdʌkʃn ˈkʊkə(r)/
n. 电磁炉
[义] A cooktop that heats pots and pans directly through electromagnetic induction rather than an open flame or hot coil. 通过电磁感应直接加热锅具、而非明火或裸露热圈的炉具（portable induction cooker 便携电磁炉, induction-compatible pan 适用电磁炉的锅, induction cooktop 电磁灶）。可数。
[例] ① The induction cooker boils water faster than her old gas stove. 电磁炉比她旧煤气灶烧水快。 ② Not every pan works on an induction cooker. 并非所有锅都能在电磁炉上用。
[搭] portable induction cooker 便携电磁炉, induction-compatible pan 适用锅具, induction cooktop 电磁灶, heat quickly 快速加热
[联] oven 烤箱, kitchen 厨房, cook 烹饪, pot 锅, heat 加热, appliance 电器, stove 炉灶
[记] 可数：an induction cooker。锅具须含铁质底部。induction hob 为英式常见说法

**electric pressure cooker**
英/美 /ɪˈlektrɪk ˈpreʃə kʊkə(r)/
n. 电压力锅
[义] A sealed electric pot that cooks food quickly under high pressure, popular for stews, rice, and one-pot meals. 密封电锅，在高压下快速烹饪，适合炖菜、米饭和一锅出（Instant Pot 电压力锅〔品牌俗称〕, pressure-cook stew 高压炖菜, release pressure 泄压）。可数。
[例] ① She made soup in the electric pressure cooker in under thirty minutes. 她用电子压力锅不到三十分钟就做了汤。 ② Read the manual before using an electric pressure cooker. 用电子压力锅前先看说明书。
[搭] Instant Pot 电压力锅, pressure-cook stew 高压炖菜, release pressure 泄压, one-pot meal 一锅出
[联] rice cooker 电饭煲, slow cooker 慢炖锅, kitchen 厨房, cook 烹饪, pot 锅, stew 炖菜, appliance 电器
[记] 可数：an electric pressure cooker。Instant Pot 为北美极流行品牌名，常泛指此类产品。泄压须按说明操作

**handheld mixer**
英/美 /ˈhændheld ˈmɪksə(r)/
n. 手持搅拌器
[义] A small electric mixer with two beaters, held in the hand for whipping cream, mixing batter, or beating eggs. 带两个搅拌头、手持使用的小型电动搅拌机，用于打发奶油、混合面糊或打蛋（electric handheld mixer 手持电动搅拌器, beat eggs 打蛋, mixer beaters 搅拌头）。可数。
[例] ① She used a handheld mixer to whip cream for the cake. 她用手持搅拌器为蛋糕打发奶油。 ② The handheld mixer is easier to store than a stand mixer. 手持搅拌器比厨师机更好收纳。

**waffle maker**

n. 华夫饼机

**electric grill**
英/美 /ɪˈlektrɪk ɡrɪl/
n. 电烧烤炉
[义] An indoor electric appliance with a ridged surface for grilling meat, vegetables, or sandwiches without an open flame. 室内用电、带棱纹烤盘、无明火烧烤肉、蔬菜或三明治的电器（indoor electric grill 室内电烤炉, grill chicken 烤鸡, removable grill plates 可拆烤盘）。可数。
[例] ① He grilled vegetables on an electric grill when it was too cold to cook outside. 天冷没法户外烤，他在室内电烧烤炉上烤蔬菜。 ② The electric grill smoke is less than a charcoal grill. 电烧烤炉的烟比炭烤少。

**wine cooler**

n. 酒柜；红酒冷藏柜

n. 对讲机；楼宇对讲

**landline**

n. 固定电话

[例] ① Fewer households keep a landline now that everyone has mobiles. 人人都有手机后，保留固定电话的家庭少了。 ② The office landline still handles customer calls during business hours. 办公固定电话仍在营业时间接听客户来电。

**digital photo frame**

n. 数码相框

**smart hub**

[例] ① The smart hub links the lights, lock, and thermostat together. 智能网关把灯、门锁和温控器连在一起。 ② Without a smart hub, each device needs its own app. 没有网关，每个设备都要单独用一个应用。

**carbon monoxide detector**

[义] A device that sounds an alarm when dangerous levels of carbon monoxide gas are detected in the air. 检测到空气中有危险浓度一氧化碳时发出警报的装置（install a carbon monoxide detector 安装一氧化碳报警器, CO detector 一氧化碳报警器, test the detector 测试报警器）。可数。
[例] ① Every bedroom should have a carbon monoxide detector near fuel-burning appliances. 每个卧室在燃烧燃料的电器附近都应有一氧化碳报警器。 ② The carbon monoxide detector woke them when the heater malfunctioned. 暖气故障时一氧化碳报警器把他们吵醒。

[联] smart smoke detector 智能烟雾报警器, alarm 报警器, safety 安全, gas 气体, fire 火灾, home 家庭, sensor 传感器

[例] ① She used a digital thermometer when the child felt warm. 孩子摸起来发热，她用了电子体温计。 ② The digital thermometer beeps when the reading is complete. 测完体温电子体温计会响一声。

**pulse oximeter**

n. 血氧仪
[义] A small clip-on device that measures blood oxygen level and pulse rate, usually placed on a fingertip. 夹在指尖、测量血氧饱和度与脉搏的小型设备（fingertip pulse oximeter 指夹式血氧仪, oxygen saturation 血氧饱和度, pulse rate 脉搏）。可数。
[例] ① The nurse used a pulse oximeter to check his oxygen level. 护士用血氧仪查他的血氧。 ② Home pulse oximeters became common during the pandemic. 疫情期间家用血氧仪普及了。

[联] health 健康, heart 心脏, monitor 监测, digital 数字的, finger 手指, oxygen 氧气, medical 医学的

英/美 /lenz ˈfɪltə(r)/
n. 镜头滤镜
[义] A piece of glass or plastic attached to the front of a camera lens to protect it or change the image, such as UV or polarizing filters. 安装在镜头前、用于保护或改变成像的玻璃或塑料片，如 UV 镜、偏振镜（UV lens filter UV 滤镜, polarizing filter 偏振镜, screw-on filter 旋入式滤镜）。可数。
[例] ① He kept a UV lens filter on the lens to prevent scratches. 他在镜头上一直装着 UV 镜防刮。 ② A polarizing lens filter reduces glare on water and glass. 偏振镜减少水面和玻璃的反光。
[搭] UV lens filter UV 滤镜, polarizing filter 偏振镜, screw-on filter 旋入式滤镜, protect the lens 保护镜头
[联] lens 镜头, camera 相机, photo 照片, glare 反光, protect 保护, photography 摄影, glass 玻璃
[记] 可数：a lens filter。口径须与 lens 匹配（如 58 mm）。protection vs. creative effect 两类用途

**microphone stand**
英/美 /ˈmaɪkrəfəʊn stænd/
n. 麦克风支架
[义] A stand that holds a microphone steady at the correct height for recording, streaming, or live speaking. 在录音、直播或演讲时将麦克风稳定固定在合适高度的支架（adjustable microphone stand 可调麦克风支架, boom arm 悬臂支架, mount a microphone 安装麦克风）。可数。
[例] ① She adjusted the microphone stand before starting the podcast. 播客开始前她调了麦克风支架。 ② A boom arm microphone stand saves desk space. 悬臂麦克风支架省桌面空间。
[搭] adjustable microphone stand 可调支架, boom arm 悬臂支架, mount a microphone 安装麦克风, desk stand 桌面支架
[联] microphone 麦克风, recording 录音, streaming 直播, podcast 播客, audio 音频, webcam 网络摄像头, stand 支架
[记] 可数：a microphone stand。boom arm 为桌面悬臂款，streaming / podcast 极常见

**extension cord**
英/美 /ɪkˈstenʃn kɔːd/
n. 延长线
[义] A flexible electric cable with a plug at one end and one or more sockets at the other, used to reach distant outlets. 一端插头、一端一个或多个插座的柔性电缆，用于够到远处墙插（heavy-duty extension cord 重型延长线, outdoor extension cord 户外延长线, extension cord length 线长）。可数。
[例] ① He used an extension cord to power the lamp across the room. 他用延长线给房间另一头的台灯供电。 ② Do not run an extension cord under a rug where it can overheat. 别把延长线压在地毯下，以免过热。
[搭] heavy-duty extension cord 重型延长线, outdoor extension cord 户外延长线, extension cord length 线长, plug into an outlet 插入墙插
[联] cable 电缆, power strip 插线板, plug 插头, socket 插座, electricity 电, outlet 墙插, charger 充电器
[记] 可数：an extension cord。与 power strip 不同：extension cord 主要「加长距离」。英式常说 extension lead

**car charger**
英/美 /kɑː ˈtʃɑːdʒə(r)/
n. 车载充电器
[义] A device that plugs into a car's power outlet to charge phones and other electronics while driving. 插入车内电源口、在行车时为手机及其他电子设备充电的装置（USB car charger 车载 USB 充电器, plug into the cigarette lighter 插入点烟器口, fast car charger 车载快充）。可数。
[例] ① She kept a car charger in the glove box for long drives. 长途驾驶她在手套箱里备了车载充电器。 ② The car charger has two USB ports for front and back seats. 车载充电器有两个 USB 口供前后排使用。
[搭] USB car charger 车载 USB 充电器, plug into the power outlet 插入车内电源, fast car charger 车载快充, dual-port charger 双口充电器
[联] charger 充电器, car 汽车, phone 手机, USB USB 接口, drive 驾驶, battery 电池, cable 数据线
[记] 可数：a car charger。现代车多为 12V 电源口或 USB 口。cigarette lighter socket 为旧式说法

**hoverboard**
英/美 /ˈhɒvəbɔːd/
n. 平衡车；悬浮滑板
[义] A self-balancing personal transporter with two wheels and a platform for the rider's feet, powered by a battery. 双轮、脚踏平台、电池驱动的自平衡个人代步工具（electric hoverboard 电动平衡车, ride a hoverboard 骑平衡车, hoverboard battery 平衡车电池）。可数。
[例] ① Teenagers rode hoverboards in the park until the path got crowded. 青少年在公园骑平衡车，直到小路挤满人。 ② The hoverboard needs charging after about ten miles. 平衡车大约骑十英里就要充电。
[搭] electric hoverboard 电动平衡车, ride a hoverboard 骑平衡车, hoverboard battery 平衡车电池, self-balancing 自平衡的
[联] battery 电池, wheel 轮子, ride 骑, electric 电动的, gadget 小装置, transport 交通, charge 充电
[记] 可数：a hoverboard。非真正「悬浮」，为营销俗称。部分城市限制在人行道使用

**smart ring**
英/美 /smɑːt rɪŋ/
n. 智能戒指
[义] A ring worn on the finger that tracks health data such as sleep and heart rate and may support contactless payments. 戴在手指上、追踪睡眠与心率等健康数据并可能支持非接触支付的戒指（fitness smart ring 健康智能戒指, track sleep 追踪睡眠, smart ring app 配套应用）。可数。
[例] ① He prefers a smart ring because it is less bulky than a smartwatch. 他觉得智能戒指比智能手表更不碍事。 ② The smart ring syncs sleep data to an app each morning. 智能戒指每天早上把睡眠数据同步到应用。
[搭] fitness smart ring 健康智能戒指, track sleep 追踪睡眠, smart ring app 配套应用, heart rate 心率
[联] smartwatch 智能手表, fitness tracker 运动手环, wearable 可穿戴设备, sleep 睡眠, health 健康, finger 手指, notification 通知
[记] 可数：a smart ring。Oura 等为常见品牌。比 smartwatch 更隐蔽，但屏幕交互有限

**software**
英/美 /ˈsɒftweə(r)/
n. 软件
[义] The programs and other operating information used by a computer or device, as opposed to physical hardware. 电脑或设备使用的程序及其他运行信息，与物理硬件相对（install software 安装软件, software update 软件更新, licensed software 授权软件）。不可数。
[例] ① The printer will not work until you install the correct software. 须安装正确软件打印机才能用。 ② Always download software from official sources to avoid malware. 应从官方渠道下载软件以防恶意程序。
[搭] install software 安装软件, software update 软件更新, licensed software 授权软件, software license 软件许可
[联] hardware 硬件, computer 电脑, program 程序, app 应用, install 安装, update 更新, device 设备
[记] 不可数。software vs. app：software 更泛指，app 多指具体应用。纯 IT 原理术语归 10-04，此处指消费端可安装运行的程序产品

**appliance**
英/美 /əˈplaɪəns/
n. 电器；器具
[义] A machine, especially an electrical one, used in the home for cooking, cleaning, or other domestic tasks. 机器，尤指家中用于烹饪、清洁或其他家务的电气设备（kitchen appliance 厨房电器, household appliance 家用电器, electrical appliance 电器）。可数。
[例] ① Modern kitchen appliances save time on daily cooking. 现代厨房电器为日常做饭省时间。 ② The store sells small appliances as well as large ones. 这家店既卖小家电也卖大家电。
[搭] kitchen appliance 厨房电器, household appliance 家用电器, electrical appliance 电器, small appliance 小家电
[联] device 设备, electronics 电子产品, refrigerator 冰箱, microwave 微波炉, washer 洗衣机, kitchen 厨房, gadget 小装置
[记] 可数：an appliance。small appliance 指咖啡机、搅拌机等；major appliance 指冰箱、洗衣机等

**portable monitor**
英/美 /ˈpɔːtəbl ˈmɒnɪtə(r)/
n. 便携显示器
[义] A lightweight external screen that connects to a laptop or phone for extra display space while traveling or working remotely. 轻便外接屏幕，连笔记本或手机以在旅行或远程办公时扩展显示空间（USB portable monitor USB 便携显示器, dual-screen setup 双屏配置, travel monitor 旅行显示器）。可数。
[例] ① He added a portable monitor to his laptop for coding on the road. 他加了便携显示器，方便路上写代码。 ② The portable monitor folds into a slim case in her backpack. 便携显示器折进背包里的薄壳中。
[搭] USB portable monitor USB 便携显示器, dual-screen setup 双屏配置, travel monitor 旅行显示器, connect via USB-C USB-C 连接
[联] monitor 显示器, laptop 笔记本, screen 屏幕, USB USB 接口, travel 旅行, remote work 远程办公, docking station 扩展坞
[记] 可数：a portable monitor。比固定 monitor 更轻薄。远程办公与差旅场景近年需求上升

**capture card**
英/美 /ˈkæptʃə kɑːd/
n. 采集卡；视频采集卡
[义] A device that records video output from a game console or camera and sends it to a computer for streaming or editing. 录制游戏机或相机视频输出并传至电脑以供直播或剪辑的设备（game capture card 游戏采集卡, HDMI capture card HDMI 采集卡, live streaming 直播）。可数。
[例] ① Streamers use a capture card to broadcast console games on PC. 主播用采集卡在 PC 上直播主机游戏。 ② The capture card records gameplay without slowing the main computer. 采集卡录游戏而不拖慢主电脑。
[搭] game capture card 游戏采集卡, HDMI capture card HDMI 采集卡, live streaming 直播, record gameplay 录制游戏画面
[联] game console 游戏主机, streaming 直播, HDMI HDMI 线, computer 电脑, video 视频, footage 画面, gaming 游戏
[记] 可数：a capture card。游戏直播与录播常见外设。internal vs. external capture card 两种形态

**network switch**
英/美 /ˈnetwɜːrk swɪtʃ/
n. 网络交换机
[义] A device that connects multiple computers, printers, and other equipment on a local wired network. 在本地有线网络中连接多台电脑、打印机及其他设备的装置（Ethernet network switch 以太网交换机, plug into a switch 接入交换机, home network switch 家用交换机）。可数。
[例] ① They added a network switch to wire more devices in the home office. 他们在家庭办公室加了交换机以有线连接更多设备。 ② The network switch lights show which ports are active. 交换机指示灯显示哪些端口在用。
[搭] Ethernet network switch 以太网交换机, plug into a switch 接入交换机, home network switch 家用交换机, gigabit switch 千兆交换机
[联] router 路由器, modem 调制解调器, Ethernet 以太网, cable 网线, internet 互联网, computer 电脑, connection 连接
[记] 可数：a network switch。router 负责上网，switch 负责局域网内多设备有线互联

**mesh Wi-Fi system**
英/美 /meʃ ˈwaɪ faɪ ˈsɪstəm/
n. Mesh 无线路由系统
[义] A set of coordinated Wi-Fi routers or nodes that work together to provide even wireless coverage throughout a home. 一组协同工作的 Wi-Fi 路由器或节点，为全屋提供均匀无线覆盖（whole-home mesh Wi-Fi 全屋 Mesh Wi-Fi, mesh node Mesh 节点, mesh router Mesh 路由器）。可数。
[例] ① They replaced the old router with a mesh Wi-Fi system for better upstairs coverage. 他们为改善楼上信号用 Mesh 系统换了旧路由器。 ② Each mesh node plugs into a different room. 每个 Mesh 节点插在不同房间。
[搭] whole-home mesh Wi-Fi 全屋 Mesh, mesh node Mesh 节点, mesh router Mesh 路由器, seamless roaming 无缝漫游
[联] router 路由器, Wi-Fi extender Wi-Fi 扩展器, Wi-Fi 无线网络, signal 信号, internet 互联网, home 家庭, network 网络
[记] 可数：a mesh Wi-Fi system。比单个 router + extender 漫游更顺滑。node 指系统中的每个子路由

**UPS**
英/美 /ˌjuː piː es/
n. 不间断电源
[义] Uninterruptible Power Supply; a battery backup that keeps computers running briefly during a power outage. 不间断电源；停电时短暂维持电脑运行的电池备份（plug into a UPS 接入 UPS, UPS battery UPS 电池, power outage 停电）。可数。
[例] ① The UPS kept his computer alive during a brief blackout. 短暂停电时 UPS 维持了他的电脑运行。 ② Replace the UPS battery every few years for reliability. 为可靠起见 UPS 电池每隔几年要换。
[搭] plug into a UPS 接入 UPS, UPS battery UPS 电池, power outage 停电, battery backup 电池备份
[联] power supply 电源, battery 电池, computer 电脑, surge protector 防浪涌插排, electricity 电, outage 停电, protect 保护
[记] 可数：a UPS。全称 Uninterruptible Power Supply。给电脑争取保存文件、安全关机的时间

**portable power station**
英/美 /ˈpɔːtəbl ˈpaʊər ˌsteɪʃn/
n. 户外电源；便携储能电源
[义] A large rechargeable battery pack with AC outlets and USB ports for camping, outages, or outdoor work. 带交流插座和 USB 口的大容量充电电池组，用于露营、停电或户外作业（camping power station 露营电源, charge a power station 给户外电源充电, portable AC outlet 便携交流口）。可数。
[例] ① They brought a portable power station to run lights at the campsite. 露营他们用户外电源供电照明。 ② During the outage, the portable power station kept the fridge running for hours. 停电期间户外电源让冰箱运行了数小时。
[搭] camping power station 露营电源, charge a power station 充电, portable AC outlet 便携交流口, solar charging 太阳能充电
[联] power bank 移动电源, battery 电池, camping 露营, charger 充电器, outlet 插座, electricity 电, portable 便携的
[记] 可数：a portable power station。比 power bank 容量大得多，可供电饭煲、笔记本等。亦说 portable power bank（大容量语境）

**laptop stand**
英/美 /ˈlæptɒp stænd/
n. 笔记本支架
[义] A raised platform or adjustable stand that holds a laptop at a better angle for typing and cooling. 抬高或可调节的支架，让笔记本处于更利于打字和散热的角度（adjustable laptop stand 可调笔记本支架, elevate the laptop 抬高笔记本, ergonomic laptop stand 人体工学支架）。可数。
[例] ① The laptop stand improved her posture during long work sessions. 笔记本支架让她长时间工作姿势更好。 ② He uses a laptop stand to keep the fan vents clear. 他用笔记本支架让散热口不被挡住。
[搭] adjustable laptop stand 可调支架, elevate the laptop 抬高笔记本, ergonomic laptop stand 人体工学支架, cooling airflow 散热气流
[联] laptop 笔记本, desk 书桌, monitor 显示器, keyboard 键盘, cooling pad 散热垫, ergonomic 人体工学的, work 工作
[记] 可数：a laptop stand。与 laptop cooling pad 可搭配使用。折叠款适合差旅

**monitor arm**
英/美 /ˈmɒnɪtər ɑːrm/
n. 显示器支架臂
[义] A clamp or mount that holds a monitor above the desk and allows height and angle adjustment. 夹在桌边或墙上的支架，托起显示器并调节高度与角度（desk monitor arm 桌面显示器臂, adjust monitor height 调节显示器高度, VESA mount VESA 挂架）。可数。
[例] ① The monitor arm freed desk space under the screen. 显示器臂腾出了屏幕下方的桌面空间。 ② She swiveled the monitor arm to share the screen with a colleague. 她转动显示器臂与同事共享屏幕。
[搭] desk monitor arm 桌面显示器臂, adjust monitor height 调节高度, VESA mount VESA 挂架, clamp to the desk 夹在桌上
[联] monitor 显示器, desk 书桌, screen 屏幕, ergonomic 人体工学的, mount 支架, laptop 笔记本, work 工作
[记] 可数：a monitor arm。美式 arm，英式亦说 monitor mount。须确认显示器支持 VESA 孔距

**laptop cooling pad**
英/美 /ˈlæptɒp ˈkuːlɪŋ pæd/
n. 笔记本散热垫
[义] A pad with built-in fans that sits under a laptop to improve airflow and reduce heat. 内置风扇、垫在笔记本下方以改善气流、降低温度的垫子（USB cooling pad USB 散热垫, laptop fan pad 笔记本风扇垫, reduce overheating 减少过热）。可数。
[例] ① The laptop cooling pad lowered the temperature during gaming sessions. 打游戏时散热垫降低了温度。 ② He plugs the cooling pad into USB for power. 散热垫插 USB 供电。
[搭] USB cooling pad USB 散热垫, laptop fan pad 风扇垫, reduce overheating 减少过热, adjustable fan speed 可调风速
[联] laptop 笔记本, fan 风扇, heat 热量, gaming 游戏, desk 书桌, laptop stand 笔记本支架, computer 电脑
[记] 可数：a laptop cooling pad。轻薄本与高负载游戏本场景常用。噪音因风扇质量而异

**trackball**
英/美 /ˈtrækbɔːl/
n. 轨迹球鼠标
[义] A pointing device with a large ball on top or side that you roll with your fingers to move the cursor. 顶部或侧面有大球、用手指滚动以移动光标的指向设备（wireless trackball 无线轨迹球, ergonomic trackball 人体工学轨迹球, roll the ball 滚动球体）。可数。
[例] ① She switched to a trackball to reduce wrist strain. 为减轻手腕劳损她换成了轨迹球。 ② The trackball needs less desk space than a mouse. 轨迹球比鼠标占桌面空间更小。
[搭] wireless trackball 无线轨迹球, ergonomic trackball 人体工学轨迹球, roll the ball 滚动球体, thumb trackball 拇指轨迹球
[联] mouse 鼠标, cursor 光标, ergonomic 人体工学的, computer 电脑, wrist 手腕, click 点击, keyboard 键盘
[记] 可数：a trackball。部分用户因腕管综合征偏好轨迹球。thumb-operated vs. finger-operated 两种操控

**car phone mount**
英/美 /kɑː fəʊn maʊnt/
n. 车载手机支架
[义] A holder attached to a car dashboard, vent, or windshield that keeps a phone visible for navigation. 固定在仪表台、出风口或挡风玻璃上的支架，让手机固定可见以便导航（dashboard phone mount 仪表台手机支架, vent mount 出风口支架, GPS navigation 导航）。可数。
[例] ① He clipped the car phone mount to the air vent for navigation. 他把车载支架夹在出风口上用导航。 ② The car phone mount holds the phone steady on bumpy roads. 颠簸路上车载支架让手机很稳。
[搭] dashboard phone mount 仪表台支架, vent mount 出风口支架, windshield mount 吸盘支架, hands-free navigation 免提导航
[联] phone 手机, car 汽车, GPS navigator GPS 导航, drive 驾驶, smartphone 智能手机, mount 支架, navigation 导航
[记] 可数：a car phone mount。vent mount 不挡视野但承重有限。驾车导航几乎必备配件

**tablet stand**
英/美 /ˈtæblət stænd/
n. 平板支架
[义] A stand that holds a tablet at a comfortable viewing angle for reading, video calls, or cooking recipes. 以舒适角度支撑平板、便于阅读、视频通话或看菜谱的支架（adjustable tablet stand 可调平板支架, foldable tablet stand 折叠平板支架, kitchen tablet stand 厨房平板支架）。可数。
[例] ① She propped the recipe on a tablet stand while cooking. 做饭时她用平板支架撑着菜谱。 ② The foldable tablet stand fits in a laptop bag. 折叠平板支架能放进电脑包。
[搭] adjustable tablet stand 可调平板支架, foldable tablet stand 折叠支架, kitchen tablet stand 厨房平板支架, video call 视频通话
[联] tablet 平板, stand 支架, screen 屏幕, read 阅读, kitchen 厨房, desk 书桌, phone mount 手机支架
[记] 可数：a tablet stand。与 phone mount 相比通常承重更大、角度更稳

**charging station**
英/美 /ˈtʃɑːdʒɪŋ ˌsteɪʃn/
n. 多设备充电站
[义] A dock or hub with multiple ports or slots to charge several phones, watches, or earbuds at once. 带多个接口或槽位、可同时给多部手机、手表或耳塞充电的底座或集线器（multi-device charging station 多设备充电站, wireless charging station 无线充电座, charge multiple devices 同时充多台设备）。可数。
[例] ① The charging station on the nightstand powers two phones and a watch. 床头柜充电站同时充两部手机和一个手表。 ② He cleared cable clutter with a single charging station. 他用一个充电站理清了线缆杂乱。
[搭] multi-device charging station 多设备充电站, wireless charging station 无线充电座, charge multiple devices 同时充电, USB charging hub 充电集线器
[联] charger 充电器, wireless charger 无线充电器, phone 手机, smartwatch 智能手表, earbud 耳塞, cable 数据线, desk 书桌
[记] 可数：a charging station。nightstand / desk 整理多设备充电的常见方案

**Bluetooth tracker**
英/美 /ˈbluːtuːθ ˌtrækə(r)/
n. 蓝牙防丢器
[义] A small tag that connects to a phone via Bluetooth to help find keys, wallets, or bags. 通过蓝牙连手机、帮助寻找钥匙、钱包或包包的小型标签（attach a Bluetooth tracker 挂上防丢器, find my keys 找钥匙, tracker app 追踪应用）。可数。
[例] ① She attached a Bluetooth tracker to her keys and checked the app when they were missing. 她在钥匙上挂了防丢器，找不到时用应用定位。 ② The Bluetooth tracker beeped when the phone was nearby. 手机靠近时蓝牙防丢器会响。
[搭] attach a Bluetooth tracker 挂上防丢器, find my keys 找钥匙, tracker app 追踪应用, tile tracker 防丢标签（品牌俗称）
[联] smartphone 智能手机, Bluetooth 蓝牙, key 钥匙, wallet 钱包, bag 包, locate 定位, notification 通知
[记] 可数：a Bluetooth tracker。Tile / AirTag 为常见品牌。依赖蓝牙距离，非 GPS 全球定位

**smart display**
英/美 /smɑːt dɪˈspleɪ/
n. 智能屏；带屏智能音箱
[义] A screen device with a voice assistant that shows weather, video calls, recipes, and smart home controls. 带语音助手屏幕的设备，可显示天气、视频通话、菜谱与智能家居控制（voice-assistant smart display 语音助手智能屏, smart display screen 智能屏屏幕, video call on a smart display 智能屏视频通话）。可数。
[例] ① The smart display in the kitchen shows recipes while she cooks. 厨房智能屏做饭时显示菜谱。 ② He answered a video call on the smart display from across the room. 他在房间另一端用智能屏接了视频通话。
[搭] voice-assistant smart display 语音助手智能屏, video call 视频通话, smart home control 智能家居控制, touch screen 触摸屏
[联] smart speaker 智能音箱, screen 屏幕, voice 语音, smart home 智能家居, video call 视频通话, kitchen 厨房, display 显示
[记] 可数：a smart display。比 smart speaker 多屏幕交互。Echo Show、Nest Hub 等为常见产品形态

**wake-up light**
英/美 /weɪk ʌp laɪt/
n. 唤醒灯；日出闹钟灯
[义] An alarm clock with a lamp that gradually brightens to simulate sunrise and wake you gently. 带灯的闹钟，灯光逐渐变亮模拟日出、温和唤醒（sunrise wake-up light 日出唤醒灯, gradual light alarm 渐亮闹钟, morning alarm 晨间闹钟）。可数。
[例] ① The wake-up light made winter mornings less jarring than a loud alarm. 唤醒灯让冬日早晨比刺耳闹钟好过。 ② She set the wake-up light to brighten thirty minutes before the alarm. 她设唤醒灯在闹钟前三十分钟渐亮。
[搭] sunrise wake-up light 日出唤醒灯, gradual light alarm 渐亮闹钟, morning alarm 晨间闹钟, simulate sunrise 模拟日出
[联] alarm clock 闹钟, light 灯, sleep 睡眠, morning 早晨, lamp 灯具, smart bulb 智能灯泡, bedroom 卧室
[记] 可数：a wake-up light。Philips Wake-up Light 为经典产品线。亦说 sunrise alarm clock

**desk lamp**
英/美 /desk læmp/
n. 台灯
[义] A lamp designed to sit on a desk and provide focused light for reading or computer work. 置于书桌上、为阅读或电脑工作提供集中照明的灯具（LED desk lamp LED 台灯, adjustable desk lamp 可调台灯, dimmable desk lamp 可调光台灯）。可数。
[例] ① She turned on the desk lamp when the overhead light felt too harsh. 顶灯太刺眼时她开了台灯。 ② The LED desk lamp has several brightness levels. 这款 LED 台灯有多档亮度。
[搭] LED desk lamp LED 台灯, adjustable desk lamp 可调台灯, dimmable desk lamp 可调光台灯, eye-care lighting 护眼照明
[联] light 灯, desk 书桌, read 阅读, lamp 灯具, LED 发光二极管, study 学习, office 办公室
[记] 可数：a desk lamp。smart desk lamp 可连应用调光定时。与 smart bulb 不同：专用于桌面照明

**air quality monitor**
英/美 /eə ˈkwɒləti ˌmɒnɪtə(r)/
n. 空气质量监测仪
[义] A device that measures indoor pollutants such as PM2.5, CO₂, or VOCs and displays readings on a screen or app. 测量室内 PM2.5、二氧化碳或挥发性有机物等污染物并在屏幕或应用上显示读数的设备（indoor air quality monitor 室内空气质量监测仪, PM2.5 monitor PM2.5 监测仪, air quality reading 空气质量读数）。可数。
[例] ① The air quality monitor showed high CO₂ after hours in a closed meeting room. 会议室关久了后空气质量监测仪显示二氧化碳偏高。 ② She checks the air quality monitor before opening windows. 开窗前她先看空气质量监测仪。
[搭] indoor air quality monitor 室内监测仪, PM2.5 monitor PM2.5 监测仪, air quality reading 空气质量读数, VOC sensor 挥发性有机物传感器
[联] air purifier 空气净化器, air 空气, pollution 污染, sensor 传感器, health 健康, monitor 监测, home 家庭
[记] 可数：an air quality monitor。与 air purifier 不同：监测仪测数据，净化器改善空气。smart home 常两者搭配

**kitchen scale**
英/美 /ˈkɪtʃɪn skeɪl/
n. 厨房电子秤
[义] A small digital scale used in the kitchen to weigh ingredients accurately for cooking and baking. 厨房用小型电子秤，精确称量食材以供烹饪与烘焙（digital kitchen scale 数字厨房秤, weigh ingredients 称食材, grams and ounces 克与盎司）。可数。
[例] ① Baking requires a kitchen scale for precise flour measurements. 烘焙要用厨房秤精确称面粉。 ② The kitchen scale switches between grams and ounces. 厨房秤可在克与盎司间切换。
[搭] digital kitchen scale 数字厨房秤, weigh ingredients 称食材, grams and ounces 克与盎司, zero the scale 去皮归零
[联] digital scale 电子秤, kitchen 厨房, bake 烘焙, ingredient 食材, measure 测量, gram 克, cook 烹饪
[记] 可数：a kitchen scale。zero / tare 功能用于去皮称重。与 bathroom scale 用途不同

**meat thermometer**
英/美 /miːt θəˈmɒmɪtə(r)/
n. 肉类温度计；探针温度计
[义] A thermometer with a probe inserted into meat to check if it is cooked to a safe internal temperature. 带探针、插入肉类以检查是否达到安全内部温度的温度计（instant-read meat thermometer 即时读数肉类温度计, probe thermometer 探针温度计, internal temperature 内部温度）。可数。
[例] ① She checked the steak with a meat thermometer before serving. 上菜前她用肉类温度计测了牛排。 ② An instant-read meat thermometer gives results in seconds. 即时读数肉类温度计几秒出结果。
[搭] instant-read meat thermometer 即时读数温度计, probe thermometer 探针温度计, internal temperature 内部温度, safe cooking temperature 安全烹饪温度
[联] oven 烤箱, kitchen 厨房, cook 烹饪, meat 肉, temperature 温度, grill 烧烤, food 食物
[记] 可数：a meat thermometer。烧烤与烤肉必备。digital vs. dial 两种显示

**garment steamer**
英/美 /ˈɡɑːmənt ˈstiːmə(r)/
n. 挂烫机
[义] A handheld or standing device that removes wrinkles from clothes using steam without an ironing board. 手持或立式设备，用蒸汽去皱、无需烫衣板（handheld garment steamer 手持挂烫机, steam a shirt 蒸汽熨衬衫, garment steamer tank 水箱）。可数。
[例] ① She steamed the dress with a garment steamer before the interview. 面试前她用挂烫机熨了连衣裙。 ② A garment steamer works well on delicate fabrics. 挂烫机适合娇贵面料。
[搭] handheld garment steamer 手持挂烫机, steam a shirt 蒸汽熨衬衫, garment steamer tank 水箱, wrinkle removal 去皱
[联] steam iron 蒸汽熨斗, clothes 衣服, wrinkle 褶皱, steam 蒸汽, laundry 洗衣, shirt 衬衫, appliance 电器
[记] 可数：a garment steamer。比 steam iron 更适合丝绸等不宜压烫面料。travel steamer 为便携款

**steam cleaner**
英/美 /stiːm ˌkliːnə(r)/
n. 蒸汽清洁机
[义] A machine that cleans surfaces and fabrics using high-temperature steam without chemical cleaners. 用高温蒸汽清洁表面与织物、无需化学清洁剂的机器（handheld steam cleaner 手持蒸汽清洁机, steam clean the floor 蒸汽清洁地面, steam cleaner attachment 清洁头配件）。可数。
[例] ① The steam cleaner removed grime from the tile grout. 蒸汽清洁机去掉了瓷砖缝里的污垢。 ② She uses a handheld steam cleaner for sofa cushions. 她用手持蒸汽清洁机清理沙发垫。
[搭] handheld steam cleaner 手持蒸汽清洁机, steam clean the floor 蒸汽清洁地面, steam cleaner attachment 清洁头, chemical-free cleaning 无化学清洁
[联] steam mop 蒸汽拖把, vacuum cleaner 吸尘器, clean 清洁, steam 蒸汽, floor 地板, appliance 电器, sanitize 消毒
[记] 可数：a steam cleaner。与 steam mop 不同：蒸汽清洁机多用途（台面、缝隙、织物）

**robot mop**
英/美 /ˈrəʊbɒt mɒp/
n. 拖地机器人
[义] A robot that mops hard floors automatically, often after or alongside a robot vacuum. 自动拖硬地板的机器人，常在扫地机器人之后或与之配合（robot mop and vacuum 扫拖一体机, automatic floor mop 自动拖地机, mop pad 拖布垫）。可数。
[例] ① The robot mop runs after the vacuum finishes each night. 每晚扫地机扫完后拖地机器人接着拖。 ② She replaces the robot mop pad when it smells stale. 拖布有异味时她更换拖地机器人的拖布垫。
[搭] robot mop and vacuum 扫拖一体机, automatic floor mop 自动拖地机, mop pad 拖布垫, wet mopping 湿拖
[联] robot vacuum 扫地机器人, mop 拖把, floor 地板, clean 清洁, automatic 自动的, water tank 水箱, appliance 电器
[记] 可数：a robot mop。部分型号与 robot vacuum 合体为「扫拖机器人」。须定期换拖布

**electric lawn mower**
英/美 /ɪˈlektrɪk lɔːn məʊə(r)/
n. 电动割草机
[义] A lawn mower powered by electricity from a cord or battery instead of gasoline. 由电缆或电池供电、而非汽油驱动的割草机（cordless electric lawn mower 无线电动割草机, mow the lawn 割草, battery-powered mower 电池割草机）。可数。
[例] ① The cordless electric lawn mower is quieter than their old gas model. 无线电动割草机比旧汽油机安静。 ② He mows the small yard with an electric lawn mower every week. 他每周用电动割草机修剪小院子。
[搭] cordless electric lawn mower 无线电动割草机, mow the lawn 割草, battery-powered mower 电池割草机, rechargeable battery 充电电池
[联] robot lawn mower 割草机器人, lawn 草坪, grass 草, garden 花园, battery 电池, appliance 电器, outdoor 户外
[记] 可数：an electric lawn mower。suburban 家庭常见。corded 版须注意线缆范围

**leaf blower**
英/美 /liːf ˌblowə(r)/
n. 吹叶机
[义] A machine that blows air to move leaves, grass clippings, and debris off driveways and lawns. 吹气以清理车道和草坪上落叶、草屑与杂物的机器（electric leaf blower 电动吹叶机, cordless leaf blower 无线吹叶机, blow leaves 吹落叶）。可数。
[例] ① He cleared the driveway with an electric leaf blower in minutes. 他用电动吹叶机几分钟清完了车道落叶。 ② The leaf blower is loud, so he wears ear protection. 吹叶机很响，他戴了护耳。
[搭] electric leaf blower 电动吹叶机, cordless leaf blower 无线吹叶机, blow leaves 吹落叶, yard cleanup 庭院清理
[联] lawn 草坪, garden 花园, electric lawn mower 电动割草机, outdoor 户外, wind 风, clean 清理, appliance 电器
[记] 可数：a leaf blower。秋季庭院清理常见工具。部分社区限制使用时段因噪音

**pet camera**
英/美 /pet ˈkæmərə/
n. 宠物摄像头
[义] A camera placed at home to watch pets remotely, often with two-way audio and treat dispensing. 放在家中远程观看宠物的摄像头，常带双向语音与投食功能（Wi-Fi pet camera Wi-Fi 宠物摄像头, watch the dog remotely 远程看狗, pet camera app 宠物摄像头应用）。可数。
[例] ① She checked the pet camera to see if the dog was sleeping. 她看宠物摄像头确认狗是否在睡。 ② The pet camera sends alerts when the cat jumps on the counter. 猫跳上台面时宠物摄像头会发提醒。
[搭] Wi-Fi pet camera Wi-Fi 宠物摄像头, watch pets remotely 远程看宠物, two-way audio 双向语音, motion alert 移动提醒
[联] camera 摄像头, pet 宠物, dog 狗, cat 猫, smartphone 智能手机, monitor 监控, notification 通知
[记] 可数：a pet camera。与 baby monitor 功能类似但常带投食等宠物专属功能

**automatic pet feeder**
英/美 /ˌɔːtəˈmætɪk pet ˈfiːdə(r)/
n. 自动宠物喂食器
[义] A device that dispenses pet food on a schedule or remotely through an app. 按时间表或通过应用远程出粮的宠物喂食装置（timed pet feeder 定时喂食器, automatic cat feeder 自动猫喂食器, schedule feeding 定时喂食）。可数。
[例] ① The automatic pet feeder releases kibble twice a day while they are at work. 上班时自动喂食器每天放两次粮。 ② She filled the automatic pet feeder before a weekend trip. 周末出行前她装满了自动喂食器。
[搭] timed pet feeder 定时喂食器, automatic cat feeder 自动猫喂食器, schedule feeding 定时喂食, portion control 份量控制
[联] pet 宠物, dog 狗, cat 猫, food 食物, camera 摄像头, schedule 定时, appliance 电器
[记] 可数：an automatic pet feeder。与 pet camera 常搭配使用。湿粮款须考虑保鲜

**pop filter**
英/美 /pɒp fɪltə(r)/
n. 麦克风防喷罩
[义] A screen placed in front of a microphone to reduce harsh popping sounds from speech and singing. 置于麦克风前的滤网，减少说话与唱歌时的爆破音（microphone pop filter 麦克风防喷罩, attach a pop filter 安装防喷罩, reduce plosives 减少爆破音）。可数。
[例] ① A pop filter made his podcast recordings sound cleaner. 防喷罩让他的播客录音更干净。 ② She clamped the pop filter onto the microphone stand. 她把防喷罩夹在麦克风支架上。
[搭] microphone pop filter 麦克风防喷罩, attach a pop filter 安装防喷罩, reduce plosives 减少爆破音, foam windscreen 海绵防风罩
[联] microphone 麦克风, microphone stand 麦克风支架, recording 录音, podcast 播客, streaming 直播, audio 音频, voice 语音
[记] 可数：a pop filter。podcast / 配音 / 直播录音常见配件。mesh vs. foam 两种材质

**audio interface**
英/美 /ˈɒdiəʊ ˈɪntəfeɪs/
n. 音频接口
[义] A device that connects microphones and instruments to a computer for high-quality recording. 把麦克风与乐器连到电脑以实现高质量录音的设备（USB audio interface USB 音频接口, connect a microphone 连接麦克风, home recording 家庭录音）。可数。
[例] ① He plugged the studio microphone into an audio interface for recording. 他把录音室麦克风插在音频接口上录音。 ② The audio interface improved sound quality compared to the laptop mic. 相比笔记本麦克风，音频接口提升了音质。
[搭] USB audio interface USB 音频接口, connect a microphone 连接麦克风, home recording 家庭录音, XLR input XLR 输入
[联] microphone 麦克风, recording 录音, computer 电脑, USB USB 接口, podcast 播客, music 音乐, audio 音频
[记] 可数：an audio interface。音乐人、播客主、主播的常见设备。比内置声卡专业

**optical drive**
英/美 /ˈɒptɪkəl draɪv/
n. 光驱
[义] A drive in or attached to a computer that reads and writes CDs, DVDs, or Blu-ray discs. 电脑内置或外接、读取和写入 CD、DVD 或蓝光光盘的驱动器（external optical drive 外置光驱, insert a disc 插入光盘, DVD optical drive DVD 光驱）。可数。
[例] ① Many new laptops no longer include an optical drive. 许多新笔记本已不再内置光驱。 ② He bought an external optical drive to install old software from discs. 他买了外置光驱从光盘安装旧软件。
[搭] external optical drive 外置光驱, insert a disc 插入光盘, DVD optical drive DVD 光驱, Blu-ray drive 蓝光光驱
[联] DVD player DVD 播放器, Blu-ray player 蓝光播放器, disc 光盘, computer 电脑, USB USB 接口, install 安装, software 软件
[记] 可数：an optical drive。笔记本轻薄化后多改外置。read vs. write 能力因型号而异

**portable scanner**
英/美 /ˈpɔːtəbl ˈskænə(r)/
n. 便携扫描仪
[义] A small scanner that can travel with a laptop and digitize documents without a large office machine. 可随笔记本携带、无需大型办公机即可数字化文件的小型扫描仪（portable document scanner 便携文档扫描仪, scan receipts 扫描收据, sheet-fed scanner 进纸式扫描仪）。可数。
[例] ① She scans contracts on the road with a portable scanner. 出差路上她用便携扫描仪扫合同。 ② The portable scanner feeds one page at a time through a slot. 便携扫描仪每次从槽口进一张纸。
[搭] portable document scanner 便携文档扫描仪, scan receipts 扫描收据, sheet-fed scanner 进纸式扫描仪, digitize documents 数字化文件
[联] scanner 扫描仪, document 文件, laptop 笔记本, travel 旅行, office 办公室, file 文件, USB USB 接口
[记] 可数：a portable scanner。flatbed portable 较少，sheet-fed 更常见。差旅商务人士实用

**laminator**
英/美 /ˈlæmɪneɪtə(r)/
n. 塑封机
[义] A machine that seals paper inside a plastic film to protect it from water and wear. 将纸张封入塑料膜中以防水耐磨的机器（office laminator 办公塑封机, laminate a document 塑封文件, laminating pouch 塑封膜）。可数。
[例] ① The teacher used a laminator to protect classroom posters. 老师用塑封机保护课堂海报。 ② Feed the page into the laminator with the pouch aligned correctly. 把塑封膜对齐后连同页面一起送入塑封机。

[例] ① Engineers tested AR glasses for viewing repair instructions on site. 工程师用 AR 眼镜在现场查看维修指引。 ② AR glasses are still heavier than regular eyeglasses. AR 眼镜仍比普通眼镜重。

n. 小烤箱；吐司烤箱

n. 油炸锅

[例] ① They used a deep fryer for homemade fries on game night. 比赛之夜他们家用油炸锅做薯条。 ② Change the oil in the deep fryer regularly for safety. 为安全起见要定期换油炸锅里的油。

英/美 /ˈvækjuːm ˌsiːlər/
n. 真空封口机

[例] ① She vacuum-sealed meat before freezing it. 冷冻前她用真空封口机封了肉。 ② A vacuum sealer cuts down on freezer burn. 真空封口机能减少冷冻室冻伤。

**range hood**
英/美 /reɪndʒ hʊd/
n. 抽油烟机
[义] A device above a stove that sucks up smoke, steam, and cooking smells and vents or filters them. 位于炉灶上方、吸走油烟、蒸汽和烹饪气味并排出或过滤的装置（kitchen range hood 厨房抽油烟机, vent hood 排烟罩, range hood filter 油网/滤芯）。可数。
[例] ① Turn on the range hood when you start frying. 开始油炸时打开抽油烟机。 ② The range hood filter needs cleaning every few months. 抽油烟机滤网每隔几个月要清洗。

[联] stove 炉灶, kitchen 厨房, cook 烹饪, smoke 烟, steam 蒸汽, appliance 电器, filter 滤芯

**garbage disposal**

[例] ① Scrape plates into the garbage disposal after dinner. 晚饭后把盘子残渣倒进垃圾处理器。 ② Never put bones in the garbage disposal. 别把骨头放进垃圾处理器。

[例] ① She stepped on the smart scale each morning to log her weight. 她每天早上站智能秤记录体重。 ② The smart scale syncs data to a fitness app automatically. 智能秤自动把数据同步到健身应用。

**heart rate monitor**
英/美 /hɑːrt reɪt ˈmɒnɪtər/
n. 心率监测器
[义] A device that measures your heart rate during exercise, often worn as a chest strap or watch. 运动期间测量心率的设备，常以胸带或手表形式佩戴（chest strap heart rate monitor 胸带心率监测器, heart rate reading 心率读数, workout heart rate 运动心率）。可数。
[例] ① Runners use a chest strap heart rate monitor for accurate data. 跑者用胸带心率监测器获取准确数据。 ② The heart rate monitor paired with her sports watch. 心率监测器与她的运动手表配对。

**UV sanitizer**

n. UV 消毒器
[义] A box or wand that uses ultraviolet light to kill germs on phones, keys, and small personal items. 用紫外线杀灭手机、钥匙等小件个人物品上病菌的盒子或棒（phone UV sanitizer 手机 UV 消毒盒, UV sanitizing box UV 消毒盒, sanitize devices 消毒设备）。可数。
[例] ① She placed her phone in the UV sanitizer after traveling. 旅行归来她把手机放进 UV 消毒盒。 ② The UV sanitizer runs for a few minutes and shuts off automatically. UV 消毒器运行几分钟后自动关闭。

n. 手持吸尘器；杆式吸尘器
[义] A lightweight, often cordless vacuum cleaner held in the hand for quick floor and surface cleaning. 轻便、常无线的手持吸尘器，用于快速清洁地面与表面（cordless stick vacuum 无线杆式吸尘器, stick vacuum cleaner 杆式吸尘器, quick cleanup 快速清理）。可数。

n. 高压清洗机

[例] ① He used a pressure washer to remove moss from the driveway. 他用高压清洗机去掉车道上的青苔。 ② Keep the pressure washer nozzle moving to avoid damaging wood. 高压清洗时喷嘴要移动，以免损坏木材。

**universal remote**

[例] ① One universal remote replaced three separate remotes on the coffee table. 一个万能遥控器替换了茶几上三个遥控。 ② Programming the universal remote took a few minutes. 编程万能遥控器花了几分钟。

n. 食物脱水机

n. 电子闹钟
[义] A clock with a digital display that sounds an alarm at a set time, often with radio or USB charging. 数字显示、在设定时间响铃的钟，常带收音机或 USB 充电（bedside alarm clock 床头闹钟, set the alarm 设闹钟, snooze button 贪睡键）。可数。
[例] ① He hit the snooze button on the digital alarm clock twice. 他在电子闹钟上按了两次贪睡。 ② The digital alarm clock glows too bright, so she covers it at night. 电子闹钟太亮，她晚上把它盖住。
[搭] bedside alarm clock 床头闹钟, set the alarm 设闹钟, snooze button 贪睡键, USB charging port USB 充电口
[联] wake-up light 唤醒灯, clock 钟, sleep 睡眠, morning 早晨, bedroom 卧室, time 时间, radio 收音机
[记] 可数：a digital alarm clock。smartphone 闹钟普及后销量下降，但 bedside 仍常见。snooze 为「贪睡」

**laser pointer**
英/美 /ˈleɪzə(r) ˌpɔɪntə(r)/
n. 激光笔
[义] A handheld device that projects a bright laser dot used to highlight slides during presentations. 手持设备，投射明亮激光点，用于演示时指示幻灯片要点（presentation laser pointer 演示激光笔, point at the screen 指向屏幕, laser dot 激光光点）。可数。
[例] ① The speaker used a laser pointer to highlight the chart. 演讲者用激光笔指出图表要点。 ② Never shine a laser pointer at people's eyes or aircraft. 切勿用激光笔照射人眼或飞机。
[搭] presentation laser pointer 演示激光笔, point at the screen 指向屏幕, laser dot 激光光点, wireless presenter 无线演示器
[联] presentation clicker 翻页笔, presentation 演示, projector 投影仪, slide 幻灯片, classroom 课堂, meeting 会议, light 光
[记] 可数：a laser pointer。presentation clicker 常内置激光笔功能。航空与交通法规限制滥用

**KVM switch**
英/美 /ˌkeɪ viː em swɪtʃ/
n. KVM 切换器
[义] A device that lets one keyboard, monitor, and mouse control several computers by switching between them. 让一套键盘、显示器和鼠标通过切换控制多台电脑的设备（USB KVM switch USB KVM 切换器, switch between computers 切换电脑, dual-monitor KVM 双显示器 KVM）。可数。
[例] ① A KVM switch saved desk space for his work and personal PCs. KVM 切换器为他的工作和个人电脑省了桌面空间。 ② Press the button on the KVM switch to change to the laptop. 按 KVM 切换器按钮切到笔记本。
[搭] USB KVM switch USB KVM 切换器, switch between computers 切换电脑, dual-monitor KVM 双显示器 KVM, keyboard and mouse 键鼠
[联] keyboard 键盘, mouse 鼠标, monitor 显示器, computer 电脑, desk 书桌, switch 切换, USB USB 接口
[记] 可数：a KVM switch。KVM = keyboard, video, mouse。home office 双机用户常见

**HDMI switch**
英/美 /ˌeɪtʃ diː em aɪ swɪtʃ/
n. HDMI 切换器
[义] A device that connects several HDMI sources to one TV or monitor and switches between them. 将多个 HDMI 信号源连到一台电视或显示器并切换的设备（HDMI switch box HDMI 切换盒, switch input sources 切换输入源, multiple HDMI inputs 多路 HDMI 输入）。可数。
[例] ① An HDMI switch lets them plug in a console, laptop, and Blu-ray player to one TV. HDMI 切换器让游戏机、笔记本和蓝光机共用一个电视口。 ② Press the HDMI switch to select the game console. 按 HDMI 切换器选择游戏机信号。
[搭] HDMI switch box HDMI 切换盒, switch input sources 切换输入源, multiple HDMI inputs 多路输入, remote switching 遥控切换
[联] HDMI splitter HDMI 分配器, TV 电视, monitor 显示器, game console 游戏主机, cable 线缆, signal 信号, display 显示
[记] 可数：an HDMI switch。与 HDMI splitter 相反：switch 为多进一出，splitter 为一进多出

**label printer**
英/美 /ˈleɪbl ˌprɪntə(r)/
n. 标签打印机
[义] A small printer that produces adhesive labels, usually connected to a computer for batch printing. 打印粘胶标签的小型打印机，通常连电脑批量打印（portable label printer 便携标签打印机, print labels 打印标签, label tape cartridge 标签色带）。可数。
[例] ① The office uses a label printer for shipping boxes. 办公室用标签打印机给快递箱贴标。 ② She printed address labels from a spreadsheet through the label printer. 她通过标签打印机从表格批量打印地址标签。
[搭] portable label printer 便携标签打印机, print labels 打印标签, label tape cartridge 标签色带, barcode label 条码标签
[联] label maker 标签机, printer 打印机, office 办公室, organize 整理, tape 色带, document 文件, cable 线缆
[记] 可数：a label printer。与 label maker 不同：label printer 侧重连电脑批量输出

**laser printer**
英/美 /ˈleɪzə(r) ˌprɪntə(r)/
n. 激光打印机
[义] A printer that uses toner and a laser to produce sharp text and graphics, common in offices. 用碳粉和激光产生清晰文字与图形的打印机，办公室常见（office laser printer 办公激光打印机, toner cartridge 硒鼓, fast printing 快速打印）。可数。
[例] ① The office laser printer handles hundreds of pages a day. 办公激光打印机每天印数百页。 ② Laser printers are cheaper per page for black-and-white documents. 黑白文档激光打印机单页成本更低。
[搭] office laser printer 办公激光打印机, toner cartridge 硒鼓, fast printing 快速打印, black-and-white printing 黑白打印
[联] printer 打印机, inkjet printer 喷墨打印机, toner cartridge 硒鼓, office 办公室, document 文件, print 打印, paper 纸
[记] 可数：a laser printer。与 inkjet 相对：激光适合大量文本，喷墨适合照片

**case fan**
英/美 /keɪs fæn/
n. 机箱风扇
[义] A fan mounted inside a computer case to move air and keep components cool. 安装在电脑机箱内、推动气流并为组件降温的风扇（PC case fan 机箱风扇, cooling fan 散热风扇, fan speed 风扇转速）。可数。
[例] ① He replaced the loud case fan with a quieter model. 他把吵的机箱风扇换成了更安静的型号。 ② Good airflow from case fans helps the GPU stay cool. 机箱风扇良好气流有助于显卡保持低温。
[搭] PC case fan 机箱风扇, cooling fan 散热风扇, fan speed 风扇转速, quiet fan 静音风扇
[联] CPU cooler 散热器, tower 主机箱, computer 电脑, heat 热量, cooling 散热, GPU 显卡, airflow 气流
[记] 可数：a case fan。游戏用台式机常装多枚。常见尺寸 120 mm / 140 mm

**ergonomic mouse**
英/美 /ˌɜːrɡəˈnɒmɪk maʊs/
n. 人体工学鼠标
[义] A mouse shaped to reduce wrist strain during long computer use, often with a vertical or sculpted design. 为减少长时间用电脑时的手腕劳损而设计的鼠标，常为垂直或造型贴合手形（vertical ergonomic mouse 垂直人体工学鼠标, reduce wrist strain 减轻手腕劳损, comfortable grip 舒适握感）。可数。
[例] ① Switching to an ergonomic mouse eased her wrist pain. 换人体工学鼠标后她手腕疼痛减轻了。 ② The ergonomic mouse fits the natural angle of the hand. 人体工学鼠标贴合手的自然角度。
[搭] vertical ergonomic mouse 垂直人体工学鼠标, reduce wrist strain 减轻手腕劳损, comfortable grip 舒适握感, long computer use 长时间用电脑
[联] mouse 鼠标, trackball 轨迹球, keyboard 键盘, wrist 手腕, computer 电脑, desk 书桌, vertical mouse 垂直鼠标
[记] 可数：an ergonomic mouse。vertical mouse 为常见子类型。RSI（重复性劳损）预防常用

**mini PC**
英/美 /ˈmɪni piː ˈsiː/
n. 迷你电脑
[义] A very small desktop computer that fits in the palm or behind a monitor, used for office, media, or light computing. 可握在掌心或挂在显示器后的小型台式电脑，用于办公、影音或轻度计算（compact mini PC 紧凑迷你电脑, mount behind a monitor 挂在显示器后, media mini PC 影音迷你电脑）。可数。
[例] ① The mini PC behind the TV streams movies quietly. 电视后面的迷你电脑安静串流电影。 ② A mini PC saves desk space compared to a full tower. 迷你电脑比大主机省桌面空间。
[搭] compact mini PC 紧凑迷你电脑, mount behind a monitor 挂在显示器后, media mini PC 影音迷你电脑, small form factor 小型机身
[联] computer 电脑, desktop 台式机, tower 主机箱, monitor 显示器, compact 紧凑的, office 办公室, streaming 串流
[记] 可数：a mini PC。NUC 为 Intel 经典产品线名。比 laptop 更省空间、更易固定部署

**FPV goggles**
英/美 /ˌef piː ˈviː ˈɡʌɡlz/
n. FPV 眼镜
[义] Head-mounted goggles that show a live video feed from a drone or RC vehicle for first-person flying. 头戴式眼镜，显示来自无人机或遥控载具的实时画面，用于第一人称飞行（drone FPV goggles 无人机 FPV 眼镜, first-person view 第一人称视角, live video feed 实时画面）。可数。
[例] ① He flew the racing drone through FPV goggles in the park. 他在公园戴 FPV 眼镜飞竞速无人机。 ② FPV goggles make you feel like you are inside the cockpit. FPV 眼镜让你感觉像坐在驾驶舱里。
[搭] drone FPV goggles 无人机 FPV 眼镜, first-person view 第一人称视角, live video feed 实时画面, head tracking 头部追踪
[联] drone 无人机, VR headset VR 头显, camera 摄像头, video 视频, racing 竞速, pilot 飞手, wireless 无线的
[记] 可数：FPV goggles（复数形式）。FPV = first-person view。与 VR headset 用途不同：FPV 接实时图传

**car vacuum**
英/美 /kɑːr ˈvækjuːm/
n. 车载吸尘器
[义] A small vacuum cleaner powered by a car outlet for cleaning seats, floors, and crumbs. 由车内电源供电、清洁座椅、地板和碎屑的小型吸尘器（portable car vacuum 便携车载吸尘器, plug into 12V 插 12V 电源, clean car interior 清洁车内）。可数。
[例] ① He keeps a car vacuum in the trunk for after road trips. 长途旅行后他在后备厢备车载吸尘器。 ② The car vacuum reached crumbs between the seats. 车载吸尘器吸到了座椅间的碎屑。
[搭] portable car vacuum 便携车载吸尘器, plug into 12V 插 12V 电源, clean car interior 清洁车内, handheld car vacuum 手持车载吸尘器
[联] vacuum cleaner 吸尘器, car 汽车, clean 清洁, dust 灰尘, seat 座椅, portable 便携的, charger 充电器
[记] 可数：a car vacuum。12V 车载口供电。与 household stick vacuum 相比更小、更专用于车内

**bug zapper**
英/美 /bʌɡ ˈzæpər/
n. 灭蚊灯；电蚊拍灯
[义] A device that attracts insects with light and kills them with an electric grid, used outdoors or in garages. 用灯光吸引昆虫并以电网击杀的装置，用于户外或车库（outdoor bug zapper 户外灭蚊灯, insect killer lamp 杀虫灯, electric grid 电网）。可数。
[例] ① They hung a bug zapper on the porch for summer evenings. 夏夜他们在门廊挂了灭蚊灯。 ② The bug zapper crackled when mosquitoes flew into it. 蚊子飞进灭蚊灯时噼啪作响。
[搭] outdoor bug zapper 户外灭蚊灯, insect killer lamp 杀虫灯, electric grid 电网, porch light 门廊灯
[联] insect 昆虫, mosquito 蚊子, outdoor 户外, summer 夏天, light 灯, garden 花园, appliance 电器
[记] 可数：a bug zapper。亦说 insect zapper。不适合室内卧室（噪音与气味）

**monitor light bar**
英/美 /ˈmɒnɪtər laɪt bɑːr/
n. 显示器挂灯
[义] A lamp that clips onto the top of a monitor to light the desk without screen glare. 夹在显示器顶部、照亮桌面且不造成屏幕反光的灯（clip-on monitor light 夹式显示器灯, reduce screen glare 减少屏幕反光, adjustable brightness 可调亮度）。可数。
[例] ① The monitor light bar lit her keyboard without shining on the screen. 显示器挂灯照亮键盘却不晃屏幕。 ② She replaced the desk lamp with a monitor light bar to save space. 她为省空间用显示器挂灯替了台灯。
[搭] clip-on monitor light 夹式显示器灯, reduce screen glare 减少屏幕反光, adjustable brightness 可调亮度, eye-care lighting 护眼照明
[联] monitor 显示器, desk lamp 台灯, light 灯, screen 屏幕, desk 书桌, work 工作, glare 反光
[记] 可数：a monitor light bar。远程办公与夜间加班场景流行。比台灯更省桌面空间

**vertical mouse**
英/美 /ˈvɜːrtɪkl maʊs/
n. 垂直鼠标
[义] A mouse held in a handshake position to keep the wrist neutral and reduce strain. 以握手姿势握持、保持手腕中立、减轻劳损的鼠标（wireless vertical mouse 无线垂直鼠标, neutral wrist position 手腕中立位, ergonomic design 人体工学设计）。可数。
[例] ① Her vertical mouse stopped the tingling in her forearm. 垂直鼠标让她前臂发麻消失了。 ② It takes a few days to get used to a vertical mouse. 垂直鼠标需要几天适应。
[搭] wireless vertical mouse 无线垂直鼠标, neutral wrist position 手腕中立位, ergonomic design 人体工学设计, reduce strain 减轻劳损
[联] ergonomic mouse 人体工学鼠标, mouse 鼠标, wrist 手腕, computer 电脑, trackball 轨迹球, desk 书桌, pain 疼痛
[记] 可数：a vertical mouse。ergonomic mouse 的子类型。左撇子款亦常见

**smart night light**
英/美 /smɑːt naɪt laɪt/
n. 智能夜灯
[义] A small lamp that turns on automatically in the dark or by motion, often controlled by app or voice. 在暗处或感应移动时自动亮起的小型灯，常可用应用或语音控制（motion-activated night light 感应夜灯, smart night light plug 智能夜灯插座, soft glow 柔光）。可数。
[例] ① The smart night light guided them to the bathroom without waking the baby. 智能夜灯引他们去卫生间却没吵醒婴儿。 ② She dimmed the smart night light from her phone at bedtime. 睡前她用手机把智能夜灯调暗。
[搭] motion-activated night light 感应夜灯, smart night light plug 智能夜灯插座, soft glow 柔光, auto on/off 自动开关
[联] smart bulb 智能灯泡, night 夜晚, light 灯, bedroom 卧室, motion sensor 人体感应器, smart home 智能家居, sleep 睡眠
[记] 可数：a smart night light。hallway / nursery 常见。比主灯更柔和、不刺眼

**OBD scanner**
英/美 /ˌoʊ biː ˈdiː ˈskænər/
n. OBD 诊断仪
[义] A device that plugs into a car's OBD port to read engine fault codes and basic diagnostic data. 插入汽车 OBD 接口、读取发动机故障码与基本诊断数据的设备（OBD-II scanner OBD-II 诊断仪, check engine light 检查发动机灯, diagnostic code 故障码）。可数。
[例] ① He used an OBD scanner to see why the check engine light was on. 检查发动机灯亮时他用 OBD 诊断仪查原因。 ② The OBD scanner showed a loose gas cap code. OBD 诊断仪显示油箱盖未拧紧的故障码。
[搭] OBD-II scanner OBD-II 诊断仪, check engine light 检查发动机灯, diagnostic code 故障码, plug into the OBD port 插入 OBD 口
[联] car 汽车, engine 发动机, diagnostic 诊断, repair 维修, drive 驾驶, tool 工具, error 错误
[记] 可数：an OBD scanner。OBD = On-Board Diagnostics。DIY 车主与修车店均常用

**solar charger**
英/美 /ˈsəʊlər ˈtʃɑːrdʒər/
n. 太阳能充电器
[义] A panel or device that converts sunlight into electricity to charge phones and small gadgets outdoors. 将阳光转化为电、在户外为手机和小装置充电的板或设备（portable solar charger 便携太阳能充电器, solar panel charger 太阳能板充电器, charge outdoors 户外充电）。可数。
[例] ① They packed a solar charger for the backpacking trip. 徒步旅行他们带了太阳能充电器。 ② The solar charger topped up the phone on the sunny balcony. 在向阳阳台太阳能充电器给手机补了电。
[搭] portable solar charger 便携太阳能充电器, solar panel charger 太阳能板充电器, charge outdoors 户外充电, camping gear 露营装备
[联] charger 充电器, solar 太阳能, camping 露营, phone 手机, battery 电池, outdoor 户外, portable 便携的
[记] 可数：a solar charger。比 power bank 依赖日照。与 portable power station 可搭配使用

**inkjet printer**
英/美 /ˈɪndʒet ˌprɪntə(r)/
n. 喷墨打印机
[义] A printer that sprays tiny droplets of ink onto paper, common for photos and home colour printing. 将微小墨滴喷到纸上的打印机，常用于照片与家庭彩色打印（home inkjet printer 家用喷墨打印机, ink cartridge 墨盒, photo printing 照片打印）。可数。
[例] ① The inkjet printer produces vivid colour photos at home. 喷墨打印机在家能印出色彩鲜艳的照片。 ② Inkjet printers need regular ink cartridge replacements. 喷墨打印机需定期更换墨盒。
[搭] home inkjet printer 家用喷墨打印机, ink cartridge 墨盒, photo printing 照片打印, colour printing 彩色打印
[联] laser printer 激光打印机, printer 打印机, ink cartridge 墨盒, photo 照片, office 办公室, print 打印, paper 纸
[记] 可数：an inkjet printer。与 laser printer 相对：喷墨擅长大彩照，激光擅长大批量黑白文

**high-speed blender**
英/美 /haɪ spiːd ˈblendə(r)/
n. 破壁机；高速搅拌机
[义] A powerful blender with a high-speed motor that can crush ice, nuts, and tough ingredients into smooth mixtures. 马达强劲、可碎冰、坚果等硬料并打成细滑混合液的高速搅拌机（power blender 强力搅拌机, crush ice 碎冰, smoothie maker 果昔机）。可数。
[例] ① The high-speed blender turned frozen fruit into a smooth smoothie. 破壁机把冷冻水果打成顺滑果昔。 ② A high-speed blender can heat soup through blade friction on some models. 部分破壁机可通过刀片摩擦加热汤品。
[搭] power blender 强力搅拌机, crush ice 碎冰, smoothie maker 果昔机, pulse mode 点动模式
[联] blender 搅拌机, kitchen 厨房, smoothie 果昔, ice 冰, juice 果汁, appliance 电器, motor 马达
[记] 可数：a high-speed blender。Vitamix / Blendtec 等为高端代表。比普通 blender 功率与转速更高，可碎冰打坚果

**sandwich maker**
英/美 /ˈsænwɪtʃ ˌmeɪkə(r)/
n. 三明治机
[义] A small electric appliance with triangular or square pockets that toasts and seals sandwich fillings. 带三角或方格槽、烤封三明治内馅的小型电器（toasted sandwich maker 烤三明治机, grill sandwiches 烤三明治, non-stick plates 不粘烤盘）。可数。
[例] ① He made grilled cheese in the sandwich maker for a quick lunch. 他用三明治机快速做了烤芝士三明治当午餐。 ② The sandwich maker cuts and seals the bread edges. 三明治机把面包边压合切开。
[搭] toasted sandwich maker 烤三明治机, grill sandwiches 烤三明治, non-stick plates 不粘烤盘, quick lunch 快速午餐
[联] panini press 帕尼尼机, toaster 烤面包机, sandwich 三明治, kitchen 厨房, lunch 午餐, cheese 芝士, appliance 电器
[记] 可数：a sandwich maker。英式常叫 toastie maker。与 panini press 功能相近，造型不同

**electric hot pot**

[例] ① They gathered around an electric hot pot on a cold evening. 寒夜他们围坐吃电火锅。 ② The electric hot pot keeps broth simmering during the meal. 电火锅让汤底在整个用餐过程中保持微沸。

n. 干湿两用吸尘器

**window fan**

n. 窗式风扇

[例] ① They installed a window fan to cool the bedroom at night. 他们装了窗式风扇夜间给卧室降温。 ② Set the window fan to exhaust mode on smoky days. 烟霾天把窗式风扇设为排气模式。

[例] ① The spot cleaner lifted the wine stain from the sofa cushion. 斑点清洁机去掉了沙发垫上的酒渍。 ② Keep a spot cleaner handy for pet accidents. 宠物弄脏时随手可用斑点清洁机。

**smart sprinkler controller**

n. 智能洒水控制器

**radon detector**

n. 氡气检测仪
[义] A device that measures radon gas levels in a home, often placed in basements for long-term monitoring. 测量家中氡气浓度的设备，常长期置于地下室监测（home radon detector 家用氡气检测仪, radon level 氡浓度, basement monitoring 地下室监测）。可数。
[例] ① They installed a radon detector after buying an older house. 买了老房后他们装了氡气检测仪。 ② The radon detector showed levels above the recommended limit. 氡气检测仪显示浓度超过建议上限。

**air ionizer**
英/美 /eər ˈaɪənaɪzə(r)/
n. 空气离子发生器
[义] A device that releases charged ions into the air, marketed to freshen air or reduce static and odors. 向空气释放带电离子、宣传可清新空气或减少静电与异味的设备（room air ionizer 房间空气离子发生器, negative ion generator 负离子发生器, freshen the air 清新空气）。可数。
[例] ① Some people run an air ionizer in the office for perceived fresher air. 有些人在办公室开空气离子发生器，感觉空气更清新。 ② The air ionizer produces a faint ozone smell when running. 空气离子发生器运行时会有轻微臭氧味。

[记] 可数：an air ionizer。与 HEPA air purifier 原理不同；功效争议较大，编纂作产品类别词

**FM transmitter**
英/美 /ˌef ˈem trænsˈmɪtə(r)/
n. FM 发射器
[义] A small device that plugs into a phone or car and broadcasts audio to a nearby FM radio frequency. 插入手机或汽车、将音频广播到附近 FM 频率的小型设备（car FM transmitter 车载 FM 发射器, tune to an FM station 调频收听, Bluetooth FM transmitter 蓝牙 FM 发射器）。可数。
[例] ① He played podcasts through an FM transmitter when the car lacked Bluetooth. 车没有蓝牙时他用 FM 发射器播播客。 ② Find a clear FM frequency for the transmitter to avoid static. 找空闲 FM 频率以减少 FM 发射器杂音。

n. 蓝牙适配器

**waterproof phone case**

[搭] ring phone grip 指环支架, PopSocket-style grip 吸附式指环, phone stand grip 手机支架, adhesive mount 粘贴安装

[记] 可数：a phone grip。PopSocket 为知名品牌，常泛指此类产品。比裸机更防滑

**voice recorder**
英/美 /vɔɪs rɪˈkɔːdə(r)/
n. 录音笔
[义] A portable device that records spoken audio for meetings, interviews, lectures, or notes. 便携设备，录制口语音频，用于会议、采访、讲座或笔记（digital voice recorder 数字录音笔, record a lecture 录讲座, voice memo 语音备忘）。可数。
[例] ① The journalist used a voice recorder during the interview. 记者采访期间用录音笔。 ② A voice recorder is still useful when phone battery is low. 手机没电时录音笔仍然有用。
[搭] digital voice recorder 数字录音笔, record a lecture 录讲座, voice memo 语音备忘, playback recording 回放录音
[联] microphone 麦克风, audio 音频, interview 采访, meeting 会议, lecture 讲座, portable 便携的, record 录制
[记] 可数：a voice recorder。smartphone 可替代，但专用录音笔续航与收音更稳。journalist / student 常用

**photo printer**
英/美 /ˈfəʊtəʊ ˌprɪntə(r)/
n. 照片打印机
[义] A printer designed to produce high-quality photo prints, often from phones or cameras directly. 专为输出高质量照片设计的打印机，常可直接连手机或相机（portable photo printer 便携照片打印机, print photos at home 在家冲印, photo paper 相纸）。可数。
[例] ① She printed holiday snapshots on a small photo printer. 她用小型照片打印机印了度假快照。 ② The photo printer connects to the phone over Bluetooth. 照片打印机通过蓝牙连手机。
[搭] portable photo printer 便携照片打印机, print photos at home 在家冲印, photo paper 相纸, Bluetooth printing 蓝牙打印

[记] 可数：a photo printer。instant camera 直接出片；photo printer 用相纸墨水打印。Canon Selphy 等为便携代表

**vinyl cutter**
英/美 /ˈvaɪnl ˈkʌtər/
n. 刻字机；乙烯切割机
[义] A machine that cuts shapes and letters from vinyl sheets for stickers, signs, and crafts. 从乙烯薄片切割形状与文字、用于贴纸、标牌与手工的机器（craft vinyl cutter 手工刻字机, cut vinyl decals 切割乙烯贴, design software 设计软件）。可数。
[例] ① She made custom laptop stickers with a vinyl cutter. 她用刻字机做了定制笔记本贴纸。 ② The vinyl cutter follows designs sent from the computer. 刻字机按电脑发送的设计切割。

[记] 可数：a vinyl cutter。Cricut / Silhouette 为消费级常见品牌。与 3D printer 不同：刻字机做平面切割

**foot pedal**
英/美 /fʊt ˈpedl/
n. 脚踏开关
[义] A pedal on the floor used to control functions such as muting a mic, advancing slides, or transcription playback. 地板踏板，用于静音麦克风、切换幻灯片或控制转录回放等功能（USB foot pedal USB 脚踏开关, hands-free control 免手控制, mute pedal 静音踏板）。可数。
[例] ① The podcaster used a foot pedal to mute the mic when coughing. 播客主用脚踏开关在咳嗽时静音麦克风。 ② A foot pedal lets transcribers pause audio without leaving the keyboard. 转录员用脚踏开关暂停音频而不离开键盘。
[搭] USB foot pedal USB 脚踏开关, hands-free control 免手控制, mute pedal 静音踏板, transcription pedal 转录踏板
[联] microphone 麦克风, streaming 直播, podcast 播客, keyboard 键盘, presentation clicker 翻页笔, control 控制, hands-free 免提
[记] 可数：a foot pedal。streaming / podcast / 医疗转录等场景常见。比键盘快捷键更无缝

**neck massager**
英/美 /nek ˈmæsɪdʒə(r)/
n. 颈部按摩器
[义] A U-shaped electric device worn around the neck that kneads and heats sore neck and shoulder muscles. U 形电动设备，围在颈部揉捏并加热酸痛的颈肩肌肉（heated neck massager 加热颈部按摩器, shoulder and neck massager 颈肩按摩器, shiatsu neck massager 指压颈部按摩器）。可数。
[例] ① She wore a neck massager after hours at the computer. 长时间用电脑后她戴了颈部按摩器。 ② The neck massager has several massage modes and heat levels. 颈部按摩器有多种按摩模式与加热档位。
[搭] heated neck massager 加热颈部按摩器, shoulder and neck massager 颈肩按摩器, shiatsu neck massager 指压按摩器, massage mode 按摩模式
[联] foot massager 足部按摩器, massage chair 按摩椅, neck 脖子, shoulder 肩膀, relax 放松, heat 加热, appliance 电器
[记] 可数：a neck massager。久坐办公者常见。shiatsu 指「指压」按摩风格

**electric foot bath**
英/美 /ɪˈlektrɪk fʊt bɑːθ/
n. 足浴盆
[义] A basin with heated water and massage rollers for soaking and relaxing tired feet. 带加热水与按摩滚轮的盆，用于泡脚放松疲惫双脚（heated foot spa 加热足浴盆, foot bath massage 足浴按摩, soak feet 泡脚）。可数。
[例] ① She filled the electric foot bath after a long walk. 长途步行后她装满足浴盆。 ② The electric foot bath keeps water warm for thirty minutes. 足浴盆让水温保持三十分钟。
[搭] heated foot spa 加热足浴盆, foot bath massage 足浴按摩, soak feet 泡脚, massage rollers 按摩滚轮
[联] foot massager 足部按摩器, spa 水疗, foot 脚, relax 放松, heat 加热, water 水, appliance 电器
[记] 可数：an electric foot bath。亦说 foot spa。与 foot massager（干按摩）不同：足浴盆涉水

**handheld fan**
英/美 /ˈhændheld fæn/
n. 手持风扇
[义] A small portable fan, often USB-rechargeable, used for personal cooling outdoors or at a desk. 小型便携风扇，常 USB 充电，用于户外或桌面个人降温（USB handheld fan USB 手持风扇, rechargeable mini fan 充电迷你风扇, personal cooling 个人降温）。可数。
[例] ① She carried a handheld fan during the outdoor concert. 户外音乐会她带了手持风扇。 ② The USB handheld fan plugs into a power bank in summer. 夏天 USB 手持风扇可插移动电源。

[联] electric fan 电风扇, portable 便携的, summer 夏天, heat 炎热, USB USB 接口, battery 电池, cool 降温
[记] 可数：a handheld fan。比 electric fan 更个人化、更便携。折叠款适合随身带

**digital media player**
英/美 /ˈdɪdʒɪtl ˈmiːdiə ˌpleɪə(r)/
n. 数字媒体播放器
[义] A device that plays digital music and video files on a TV or speakers, often with streaming apps built in. 在电视或扬声器上播放数字音视频文件的设备，常内置串流应用（home media player 家庭媒体播放器, stream movies 串流电影, media box 媒体盒）。可数。
[例] ① The digital media player connected the old TV to streaming services. 数字媒体播放器让旧电视能看串流。 ② They replaced the cable box with a smaller digital media player. 他们用更小的数字媒体播放器换了有线盒。

**satellite receiver**
英/美 /ˈsætəlaɪt rɪˈsiːvə(r)/
n. 卫星接收机

[例] ① The satellite receiver showed a signal strength menu for alignment. 卫星接收机有信号强度菜单用于对准。 ② They upgraded the satellite receiver for HD channels. 他们为高清频道升级了卫星接收机。

[联] satellite dish 卫星锅, TV 电视, broadcast 广播, channel 频道, signal 信号, receiver 接收机, cable 有线电视
[记] 可数：a satellite receiver。与 satellite dish 配套使用。农村与偏远地区较常见

**headphone amplifier**
英/美 /ˈhedfəʊn ˌæmplɪfaɪə(r)/
n. 耳机放大器
[义] A device that boosts audio signal to drive high-end headphones with clearer, louder output. 增强音频信号以驱动高端耳机、输出更清晰响亮声音的装置（portable headphone amp 便携耳放, drive high-impedance headphones 驱动高阻抗耳机, audio quality 音质）。可数。
[例] ① Audiophiles use a headphone amplifier with studio headphones. 发烧友配监听耳机用耳放。 ② The portable headphone amp improved detail on long flights. 便携耳放让长途航班上细节更好。

[联] headphone 耳机, amplifier 功放, audio 音频, music 音乐, sound 声音, portable 便携的, listener 听众
[记] 可数：a headphone amplifier。口语简称 amp。DAC + amp 一体机亦常见

**all-in-one computer**
英/美 /ˌɔːl ɪn wʌn kəmˈpjuːtə(r)/

[义] A desktop computer with the monitor and computer built into one unit, reducing cable clutter. 显示器与主机一体的台式电脑，减少线缆杂乱（all-in-one PC 一体机 PC, built-in display 内置屏幕, space-saving computer 省空间电脑）。可数。
[例] ① The reception desk uses an all-in-one computer to save space. 前台用一体机电脑省空间。 ② An all-in-one computer is harder to upgrade than a tower PC. 一体机比塔式机更难升级。

[联] computer 电脑, desktop 台式机, monitor 显示器, laptop 笔记本, tower 主机箱, office 办公室, screen 屏幕

**rugged phone**
英/美 /ˈrʌɡɪd fəʊn/
n. 三防手机
[义] A smartphone built to resist drops, water, dust, and harsh outdoor or work environments. 防摔、防水、防尘，适应户外或恶劣工作环境的智能手机（waterproof rugged phone 防水三防手机, drop-resistant phone 防摔手机, outdoor smartphone 户外智能手机）。可数。
[例] ① Construction workers often carry a rugged phone on site. 建筑工人常在工地带三防手机。 ② The rugged phone survived a drop into muddy water. 三防手机掉进泥水仍完好。
[搭] waterproof rugged phone 防水三防手机, drop-resistant phone 防摔手机, outdoor smartphone 户外智能手机, IP rating 防护等级
[联] smartphone 智能手机, waterproof 防水的, durable 耐用的, outdoor 户外, construction 施工, drop 摔落, dust 灰尘
[记] 可数：a rugged phone。Cat、Ulefone Armor 等为常见品牌。比普通 smartphone 更重更厚

**endoscope camera**
英/美 /ˈendəskəʊp ˌkæmərə/
n. 内窥镜摄像头
[义] A flexible camera on a long cable used to inspect drains, engines, walls, or other hard-to-reach spaces. 长线缆上的柔性摄像头，用于检查管道、发动机、墙体或其他难达空间（USB endoscope camera USB 内窥镜, inspect pipes 检查管道, borescope 孔探仪〔同义〕）。可数。
[例] ① He fed the endoscope camera into the wall to check for mold. 他把内窥镜伸进墙里查霉菌。 ② The endoscope camera connected to his phone showed live video. 内窥镜连手机显示实时画面。
[搭] USB endoscope camera USB 内窥镜, inspect pipes 检查管道, borescope 孔探仪, flexible cable 柔性线缆
[联] camera 摄像头, inspect 检查, pipe 管道, USB USB 接口, video 视频, tool 工具, DIY 自己动手
[记] 可数：an endoscope camera。自己动手与维修场景实用。borescope 为同义工业/消费通用词

**metal detector**
英/美 /ˈmetl dɪˌtektə(r)/
n. 金属探测器
[义] A handheld device that beeps when it passes over buried metal, used for hobby treasure hunting or finding lost items. 经过地下金属时发出提示声的手持设备，用于业余寻宝或找失物（hobby metal detector 业余金属探测器, beach metal detecting 海滩探宝, search coil 探测线圈）。可数。
[例] ① He found old coins with a metal detector on the beach. 他在海滩用金属探测器找到旧硬币。 ② Adjust the metal detector sensitivity for different soil types. 按不同土壤调节金属探测器灵敏度。
[搭] hobby metal detector 业余金属探测器, beach metal detecting 海滩探宝, search coil 探测线圈, find buried metal 探测地下金属
[联] detector 探测器, hobby 爱好, beach 海滩, treasure 宝藏, coin 硬币, outdoor 户外, beep 哔哔声
[记] 可数：a metal detector。安检探测棒为不同品类。业余探宝在欧美较流行

**thermal camera**
英/美 /ˈθɜːrml ˈkæmərə/
n. 热成像仪
[义] A camera that detects heat and shows temperature differences as an image, used for home inspection and repair work. 检测热量并以图像显示温差的相机，用于房屋检测与检修（handheld thermal camera 手持热成像仪, heat detection 热检测, find drafts 找漏风）。可数。
[例] ① A thermal camera revealed poor insulation around the window frame. 热成像仪显示窗框周围保温差。 ② Electricians sometimes use a thermal camera to spot overheating wires. 电工有时用热成像仪找过热线路。
[搭] handheld thermal camera 手持热成像仪, heat detection 热检测, find drafts 找漏风, infrared image 红外图像
[联] camera 相机, heat 热量, temperature 温度, inspect 检查, home 家庭, DIY 自己动手, sensor 传感器
[记] 可数：a thermal camera。FLIR 为常见品牌。消费级款比专业工业款便宜但精度较低
